    Public Proclib As String

    Public Param2 As String
    Public Param3 As String

    Public Param5 As String

End Class
Public Class Proclib
    Implements IDisposable

    Public Const MaxJournalEntries = 1
    ' Declare Global Variable
    Dim db As ClassLib
    Dim ServiceName As String
    Dim db_connect As String
    Dim db_Server As String
    Dim db_Name As String
    Dim db_user_type As String
    Dim db_User_CCode As String
    Dim db_exportoption As String
    Dim db_sysuser As String
    Dim db_syspassword As String
    Dim db_user As String
    Dim db_password As String
    Dim db_TimeOut As Integer
    Dim db_Trusted As Boolean
    Dim StrMsg As String
    Dim ObjSession As AppEngineV4.Session

    Dim br_WithHoldingTax As Boolean
    Dim br_currency As String
    Dim br_currency_1 As String
    Dim br_branch As String
    Dim CheckMonthlyExchangeRateF As Boolean
    Dim CheckWeeklyExchangeRateF As Boolean
    Dim BudgetFeaturesF As Boolean
    Dim PaidEliminationF As Boolean
    Dim FundEliminationF As Boolean
    Dim CalculationRoundingF As Boolean
    Dim LockedDate As Date
    Dim TaxTableF As Boolean

    Private disposed As Boolean = False

    ' Implement IDisposable.
    Public Overloads Sub Dispose() Implements IDisposable.Dispose
        Dispose(True)
        GC.SuppressFinalize(Me)
    End Sub
    Private Overloads Sub Dispose(ByVal disposing As Boolean)
        ' Check to see if Dispose has already been called.
        If Not disposed Then
            If disposing Then
                ' Clean-Up Managed Resources
                If Not db Is Nothing Then
                    db.Dispose()
                    Initialize()
                End If
            End If
            disposed = True
        End If
    End Sub
    Private Sub Initialize()
        db = Nothing
    End Sub
    Public Sub New()
        MyBase.New()
        Initialize()
    End Sub
    Protected Overrides Sub Finalize()
        Dispose(False)
        MyBase.Finalize()
    End Sub

    Public Delegate Function Call_Process_delegate(ByVal SName As String, ByVal ProcName As String, ByVal Param1 As Object, ByVal Param2 As Object, ByVal Param3 As Object, ByVal Param4 As Object, ByVal Param5 As Object, ByVal Param6 As Object, ByVal NewSessionFlag As Boolean) As Object
    Private Sub CallBackFunction(ByVal Result As IAsyncResult)
        Dim ProcessInfo As Process_Info
        ProcessInfo = CType(Result.AsyncState, Process_Info)
        ' Insert into Queue Process
        Dim db As New ClassLib
        If db.OpenConnection(ServiceName, db_Name, db_Server, db_sysuser, db_syspassword, db_TimeOut, db_Trusted) Then
            db.Var_db_user = db_user
            db.Var_db_Password = db_password
            db.Var_db_User_Type = db_user_type
            db.Var_db_User_CCode = db_User_CCode
            With ProcessInfo
                If StrMsg = "" Then
                    db.Call_Queued_Process(.Proclib, .ProcName, .Param1, .Param2, .Param3, .Param4, .Param5, .Param6, StrMsg, False, "C")
                Else
                    db.Call_Queued_Process(.Proclib, .ProcName, .Param1, .Param2, .Param3, .Param4, .Param5, .Param6, StrMsg, False, "P")
                End If
            End With
            db.CloseSession()
        End If
    End Sub
    Private Function Determine_Process_Timeout(ByVal ProcName As String) As Long
        Dim TimeOut As Long
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim db As New ClassLib
        db.Var_ObjSession = ObjSession
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select TimeOut from SysProcess Where ProcName='" & ProcName & "'")
        TimeOut = toval(rs.fields("TimeOut"))

        If TimeOut <> 0 Then
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select count(*) from SysProcLib Where (Status='P' and ErrMsg='Asynchronous Process (Working...)')")
            If toval(rs.fields(0)) > 0 Then
                TimeOut = 0
            End If
        End If
        Determine_Process_Timeout = TimeOut
    End Function
    Private Function Check_Process_Execution(ByVal ProcName As String) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select StartTime, EndTime, Startday, EndDay from Sysprocess where ProcName='" & ProcName & "'")
        If Not rs.Eof Then
            If toval(rs.fields("StartDay")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("EndDay")) <> 0 Then
                If Not (Today.Date.Day >= toval(rs.fields("StartDay")) And Today.Date.Day <= toval(rs.fields("EndDay"))) Then
                    Check_Process_Execution = False
                    Exit Function
                End If
            End If
            If tostr(rs.fields("StartTime")) <> "00:00" Or tostr(rs.fields("EndTime")) <> "00:00" Then
                If Not (tostr(Time()) >= tostr(rs.fields("StartTime")) And tostr(Time()) <= tostr(rs.fields("EndTime"))) Then
                    Check_Process_Execution = False
                    Exit Function
                End If
            End If
        End If
        Check_Process_Execution = True
    End Function
    Public Function Call_Process(ByVal SName As String, ByVal ProcName As String, Optional ByVal Param1 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param2 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param3 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param4 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param5 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param6 As Object = Nothing) As Object
        Dim caller As New Call_Process_delegate(AddressOf Call_Process_Execute)
        Dim callBack As AsyncCallback = AddressOf CallBackFunction
        Dim ProcessInfo As New Process_Info

        ServiceName = SName
        ProcessInfo.Proclib = "ProcessFA.NetV4.Proclib"
        ProcessInfo.ProcName = ProcName
        ProcessInfo.Param1 = SetParamValue(Param1)
        ProcessInfo.Param2 = SetParamValue(Param2)
        ProcessInfo.Param3 = SetParamValue(Param3)
        ProcessInfo.Param4 = SetParamValue(Param4)
        ProcessInfo.Param5 = SetParamValue(Param5)
        ProcessInfo.Param6 = SetParamValue(Param6)
        ProcessInfo.IsCompleted = False

        Dim Timeout As Long
        Timeout = Determine_Process_Timeout(ProcName)
        ' For Debug Purposes only
        If Timeout = 0 Then
            Call_Process = Call_Process_Execute(SName, ProcName, Param1, Param2, Param3, Param4, Param5, Param6, True)
        Else
            ' Insert into Queue Process
            Dim db As New ClassLib
            If db.OpenConnection(ServiceName, db_Name, db_Server, db_sysuser, db_syspassword, db_TimeOut, db_Trusted) Then
                db.Var_db_user = db_user
                db.Var_db_Password = db_password
                db.Var_db_User_Type = db_user_type
                db.Var_db_User_CCode = db_User_CCode
                With ProcessInfo
                    db.Call_Queued_Process(.Proclib, .ProcName, .Param1, .Param2, .Param3, .Param4, .Param5, .Param6, "Asynchronous Process (Working...)", False, "P")
                End With
                db.CloseSession()
            End If

            Dim result As IAsyncResult = caller.BeginInvoke(SName, ProcName, Param1, Param2, Param3, Param4, Param5, Param6, True, callBack, ProcessInfo)
            result.AsyncWaitHandle.WaitOne(Timeout, False)
            ProcessInfo.IsCompleted = result.IsCompleted
            If ProcessInfo.IsCompleted Then
                Call_Process = caller.EndInvoke(result)
                If db.OpenConnection(ServiceName, db_Name, db_Server, db_sysuser, db_syspassword, db_TimeOut, db_Trusted) Then
                    db.Var_db_user = db_user
                    db.Var_db_Password = db_password
                    db.Var_db_User_Type = db_user_type
                    db.Var_db_User_CCode = db_User_CCode
                    With ProcessInfo
                        If Call_Process Then
                            db.Call_Queued_Process(.Proclib, .ProcName, .Param1, .Param2, .Param3, .Param4, .Param5, .Param6, StrMsg, False, "C")
                        Else
                            db.Call_Queued_Process(.Proclib, .ProcName, .Param1, .Param2, .Param3, .Param4, .Param5, .Param6, StrMsg, False, "P")
                        End If
                    End With
                    db.CloseSession()
                End If
            Else
                Call_Process = Nothing
            End If
        End If
    End Function

    Public Function Call_Process_Execute(ByVal SName As String, ByVal ProcName As String, Optional ByVal Param1 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param2 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param3 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param4 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param5 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param6 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal NewSessionFlag As Boolean = False) As Object
        Dim Process_OK As Boolean
        StrMsg = ""
        Process_OK = False
        'Get Class Library
        db = New ClassLib
        If NewSessionFlag Then
            If Not db.OpenConnection(SName, db_Name, db_Server, db_sysuser, db_syspassword, db_TimeOut, db_Trusted) Then
                StrMsg = "Opening Session Failed !"
                db = Nothing
                Call_Process_Execute = False
                Exit Function
            End If
        Else
            db.Var_ObjSession = ObjSession
        End If
        ' For Debug Purposes Only
        'db.Var_ObjSession = ObjSession
        db.Var_db_user = db_user
        db.Var_db_Password = db_password
        db.Var_db_User_Type = db_user_type
        db.Var_db_User_CCode = db_User_CCode

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        If NewSessionFlag Then db.Begintrans()
        If Not Check_Process_Execution(ProcName) Then
            StrMsg = "Request Process : " & ProcName & " can't execute at this time !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        GetSystemParameter()
        Select Case UCase(ProcName)
            Case "UNPOSTING_JOURNAL"
                Process_OK = UnPosting_Journal(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2), todate(Param3))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "POSTING_JOURNAL"
                Process_OK = Posting_Journal(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "POSTING_PROCESS"      
                Process_OK = Posting_Process(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "CLOSING_PROCESS_1"
                Process_OK = Closing_Process_1(tostr(Param1), toval(Param2), toval(Param3), todate(Param4))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param4), tostr(Param1))
            Case "CLOSING_PROCESS_2"
                Process_OK = Closing_Process_2(tostr(Param1), toval(Param2), toval(Param3), todate(Param4))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param4), tostr(Param1))
            Case "UNDO_CLOSING_PROCESS_1"
                Process_OK = Undo_Closing_Process_1(tostr(Param1), toval(Param2), toval(Param3), todate(Param4), toval(Param5))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param4), tostr(Param1))
            Case "UNDO_CLOSING_PROCESS_2"
                Process_OK = Undo_Closing_Process_2(tostr(Param1), toval(Param2), toval(Param3), todate(Param4), toval(Param5))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param4), tostr(Param1))
            Case "DEPRECIATION_PROCESS"
                Process_OK = Depreciation_Process(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "AMORTIZATION_PROCESS"
                Process_OK = Amortization_Process(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "DEPRECIATION_PROCESS_ALL"
                Process_OK = Depreciation_Process_All(todate(Param1))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param1))
            Case "UPDATE_GL_TREE"
                Process_OK = Update_GL_Tree(tostr(Param1), toval(Param2), toval(Param3))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, Today.Date, tostr(Param1))
            Case "CREATE_ADMLINK_VOUCHER"
                Process_OK = Create_Admlink_Voucher(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2), todate(Param3))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "CREATE_ACCLINK_MEMORIAL"
                Process_OK = Create_AccLink_Memorial(toval(Param1), toval(Param2), toval(Param3))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, Today.Date, tostr(Param1))
            Case "PROCESS_ACCLINK"
                Process_OK = Process_AccLink(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2), Param3)
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "PROCESS_ACCLINK_ALL"
                Process_OK = Process_AccLink_All(todate(Param1), Param2)
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param1))
            Case "DELETE_ACCLINK"
                Process_OK = Delete_AccLink(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2), toval(Param3))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "AMORTIZATION_PROCESS_ALL"
                Process_OK = Amortization_Process_All(todate(Param1))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param1))
            Case "CREATE_ADMLINK_VOUCHER_ALL"
                Process_OK = Create_Admlink_Voucher_All(todate(Param1))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param1))
            Case "CREATE_WITHHOLDINGTAX_ALL"
                Process_OK = Create_WithHoldingTax_All(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2), toval(Param3), todate(Param4), todate(Param5), tostr(Param6))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "CREATE_WITHHOLDINGVAT_ALL"
                Process_OK = Create_WithHoldingVAT_All(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2), toval(Param3), todate(Param4), todate(Param5))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "PROCESS_ADMLINK"
                Process_OK = Process_Admlink(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2), Param3, Param4)
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "DELETE_ADMLINK"
                Process_OK = Delete_Admlink(tostr(Param1), todate(Param2))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, todate(Param2), tostr(Param1))
            Case "CANCEL_ACCLINK_MEMORIAL"
                Process_OK = Cancel_AccLink_Memorial(tostr(Param1))
                If Process_OK Then Save_Log_Process(db, ProcName, Today.Date, tostr(Param1))
        End Select

        If Process_OK Then
            If NewSessionFlag Then db.Committrans()
        Else
            If NewSessionFlag Then db.RollBack()
        End If
        If NewSessionFlag Then db.CloseSession()
        db = Nothing
        Call_Process_Execute = Process_OK
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        If NewSessionFlag Then db.RollBack()
        If NewSessionFlag Then db.CloseSession()
        db = Nothing
        Call_Process_Execute = Process_OK
    End Function
    Private Function Posting_Journal(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal PDate As Date, Optional ByVal GetLockedObjectF As Boolean = True) As Boolean
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim j As Integer
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double
        Dim Num_Debit As Double
        Dim Num_Credit As Double
        Dim sAutoNum As String
        Dim sAccount As String
        Dim Account As String

        'update document source
        Dim RefNo As String
        Dim RefType As String
        Dim ProAcc As String
        Dim ComAcc As String
        Dim VType As String
        Dim SL_Cat As String
        Dim SL_Tag As Integer
        Dim sCT As String   ' SL CT
        Dim CT As String    ' Voucher CT
        Dim Ccy As String
        Dim sType As String
        Dim Voucher As String
        Dim DebtorF As Boolean
        Dim CreditorF As Boolean
        Dim Last_Date As Date
        Dim Last_Time As String

        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Voucher As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Journal As RemoteSet
        Dim Source As String
        Dim vDate As Date
        Dim Nominal As Double
        Dim Nominal_CC As Double
        Dim Rate As Double
        Dim Rate_RC As Double
        Dim Description As String
        Dim BufferData As Object

        On Error GoTo ErrMsg

        Dim sCurrency As String

        Dim TBName As String

        ' Match previous db.begintrans from Calling Function
        If GetLockedObjectF Then
            db.Committrans()
        End If

        Dim SDate As Date
        Dim BL_Account As String = ""
        Dim BL_AccountDesc As String = ""
        Dim BL_AccountBSFlag As Boolean

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select End_Date, BL_Account from Branch Where Branch='" & br_branch & "'")
        SDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, rs.fields("End_Date"))
        BL_Account = tostr(rs.fields("BL_Account"))

        If GetLockedObjectF Then
            db.Begintrans()
        End If

        If Not Create_Admlink_Voucher(br_branch, SDate, PDate) Then GoTo ErrMsg

        If GetLockedObjectF Then
            db.Committrans()
        End If

        Dim SQL As String
        ' Loop for each physical voucher table
        For Each TBName In New String() {"N", "M", "P"}
            Select Case TBName
                Case "N"
                    SQL = "select Voucher.Voucher, vPattern.Debit, vPattern.Credit, vPattern.Account, SL.Account as SAccount, Case When SL.CT='' Then Voucher.CT Else SL.CT end CT, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.SL_Tag) as SL_Tag, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.VType) as VType, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Rate) as Rate, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Rate_RC) as Rate_RC, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Source) as Source, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as integer)) as DebtorF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Voucher.CreditorF as integer)) as CreditorF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.ProAcc) as ProAcc, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.ComAcc) as ComAcc, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.RefNo) as RefNo, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.RefType) as RefType, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.SL_Cat) as SL_Cat, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Last_Date) as Last_Date, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Last_Time) as Last_Time, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.Open_ItemF as Integer)) as Open_ItemF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.NL_FLag as Integer)) as NL_Flag, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.PL_Flag as Integer)) as PL_Flag,"
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(vType.sAutoNum) as sAutoNum, "
                    SQL = SQL & " SL.DOC_CCY,"
                    SQL = SQL & " SL.Rate as SLRate,"
                    SQL = SQL & " sum(SL.Nominal_OC) as Nominal_OC,"
                    SQL = SQL & " sum(SL.Nominal) as Nominal"
                    SQL = SQL & " from VType, vPattern, nSL SL, Account, nVoucher Voucher "
                    SQL = SQL & " where SL.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Voucher.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Voucher.Posting_Flag=0 and Voucher.Status='U' and vPattern.vType=vType.VType and VType.VType=Voucher.VType and Account.Account=vPattern.Account and (vPattern.Debit=SL.Hint or vPattern.Credit=SL.Hint) "
                    SQL = SQL & " Group By Voucher.Voucher, vPattern.Debit, vPattern.Credit, vPattern.Account, SL.Account, Case When SL.CT='' Then Voucher.CT Else SL.CT end, SL.DOC_CCY, SL.Rate"
                    SQL = SQL & " Order By Voucher.Voucher"
                    rs_Voucher = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL, adOpenForwardonly)
                Case "M"
                    SQL = "select Voucher.Voucher, vPattern.Debit, vPattern.Credit, vPattern.Account, SL.Account as SAccount, Case When SL.CT='' Then Voucher.CT Else SL.CT end as CT, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.SL_Tag) as SL_Tag, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.VType) as VType, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Rate) as Rate, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Rate_RC) as Rate_RC, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Source) as Source, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as integer)) as DebtorF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Voucher.CreditorF as integer)) as CreditorF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.ProAcc) as ProAcc, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.ComAcc) as ComAcc, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.RefNo) as RefNo, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.RefType) as RefType, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.SL_Cat) as SL_Cat, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Last_Date) as Last_Date, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Last_Time) as Last_Time, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.Open_ItemF as Integer)) as Open_ItemF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.NL_FLag as Integer)) as NL_Flag, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.PL_Flag as Integer)) as PL_Flag,"
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(vType.sAutoNum) as sAutoNum, "
                    SQL = SQL & " SL.DOC_CCY,"
                    SQL = SQL & " SL.Rate as SLRate,"
                    SQL = SQL & " sum(SL.Nominal_OC) as Nominal_OC,"
                    SQL = SQL & " sum(SL.Nominal) as Nominal"
                    SQL = SQL & " from VType, vPattern, mSL SL, Account, mVoucher Voucher "
                    SQL = SQL & " where SL.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Voucher.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Voucher.Posting_Flag=0 and Voucher.Status='U' and vPattern.vType=vType.VType and VType.VType=Voucher.VType and Account.Account=vPattern.Account and (vPattern.Debit=SL.Hint or vPattern.Credit=SL.Hint) "
                    SQL = SQL & " Group By Voucher.Voucher, vPattern.Debit, vPattern.Credit, vPattern.Account, SL.Account, Case When SL.CT='' Then Voucher.CT Else SL.CT end, SL.DOC_CCY, SL.Rate"
                    SQL = SQL & " Order By Voucher.Voucher"
                    rs_Voucher = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL, adOpenForwardonly)
                Case Else
                    SQL = "select Voucher.Voucher, vPattern.Debit, vPattern.Credit, Case When coalesce(SL.SAccount,'')='' then vPattern.Account else SL.SAccount end as Account, SL.Account as SAccount, Case When SL.CT='' Then Voucher.CT Else SL.CT end as CT, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.SL_Tag) as SL_Tag, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.VType) as VType, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Rate) as Rate, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Rate_RC) as Rate_RC, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Source) as Source, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as integer)) as DebtorF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Voucher.CreditorF as integer)) as CreditorF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.ProAcc) as ProAcc, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.ComAcc) as ComAcc, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.RefNo) as RefNo, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.RefType) as RefType, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.SL_Cat) as SL_Cat, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Last_Date) as Last_Date, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Voucher.Last_Time) as Last_Time, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.Open_ItemF as Integer)) as Open_ItemF, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.NL_FLag as Integer)) as NL_Flag, "
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(Cast(Account.PL_Flag as Integer)) as PL_Flag,"
                    SQL = SQL & " Max(vType.sAutoNum) as sAutoNum, "
                    SQL = SQL & " SL.DOC_CCY,"
                    SQL = SQL & " SL.Rate as SLRate,"
                    SQL = SQL & " sum(SL.Nominal_OC) as Nominal_OC,"
                    SQL = SQL & " sum(SL.Nominal) as Nominal"
                    SQL = SQL & " from VType, vPattern, pSL SL, Account, pVoucher Voucher "
                    SQL = SQL & " where SL.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Voucher.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Voucher.Posting_Flag=0 and Voucher.Status='U' and vPattern.vType=vType.VType and VType.VType=Voucher.VType and Account.Account=vPattern.Account and (vPattern.Debit=SL.Hint or vPattern.Credit=SL.Hint) "
                    SQL = SQL & " Group By Voucher.Voucher, vPattern.Debit, vPattern.Credit, Case When coalesce(SL.SAccount,'')='' then vPattern.Account else SL.SAccount end, SL.Account, Case When SL.CT='' Then Voucher.CT Else SL.CT end, SL.DOC_CCY, SL.Rate"
                    SQL = SQL & " Order By Voucher.Voucher"
                    rs_Voucher = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL, adOpenForwardonly)
            End Select
            Do While Not rs_Voucher.Eof
                If GetLockedObjectF Then
                    db.Begintrans()
                    If Not db.GetLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then
                        StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
                        GoTo ErrMsg
                    End If
                End If

                Select Case TBName
                    Case "N"
                        rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from NJournal where 1<>1")
                    Case "M"
                        rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from MJournal where 1<>1")
                    Case Else
                        rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from PJournal where 1<>1")
                End Select

                Dim RowNum As Integer = 0
                Dim vRate As Double
                Dim vRate_RC As Double
                Do While Not rs_Voucher.Eof And RowNum < 100

                    Voucher = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Voucher"))
                    VType = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("VType"))
                    ProAcc = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("ProAcc"))
                    ComAcc = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("ComAcc"))
                    RefNo = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("RefNo"))
                    RefType = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("RefType"))
                    ComAcc = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("ComAcc"))
                    SL_Cat = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Cat"))
                    Source = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Source"))
                    DebtorF = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("DebtorF"))
                    CreditorF = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("CreditorF"))
                    Ccy = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Currency"))
                    vDate = todate(rs_Voucher.fields("Date"))
                    vRate = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Rate"))
                    vRate_RC = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Rate_RC"))
                    If br_currency_1.ToUpper = br_currency.ToUpper Then
                        vRate_RC = vRate
                    End If
                    SL_Tag = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Tag"))
                    sAutoNum = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("sAutoNum"))
                    CT = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("CT"))
                    Last_Date = todate(rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Date"))
                    Last_Time = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Time"))

                    ' Initialize BufferData
                    ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
                    For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                        BufferData(0, i) = ""
                        BufferData(1, i) = ""
                        BufferData(2, i) = ""
                        BufferData(3, i) = 0
                        BufferData(4, i) = 0
                        BufferData(5, i) = 0
                        BufferData(6, i) = 0
                        BufferData(7, i) = ""
                        BufferData(8, i) = 0
                        BufferData(9, i) = 0
                    Next i

                    i = 0
                    Num_Debit = 0
                    Num_Credit = 0

                    Do While Voucher = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Voucher"))
                        sAccount = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Account"))

                        If toval(rs_Voucher.fields("NL_Flag")) Then
                            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account from ComAcc where ComAcc='" & ComAcc & "'")
                            sAccount = tostr(rs.fields("Account"))
                            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
                        ElseIf toval(rs_Voucher.fields("PL_Flag")) Then
                            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account_Dr, Account_Cr from ProAcc where ProAcc='" & ProAcc & "'")
                            If Not rs.Eof Then
                                If rs_Voucher.fields("Debit") <> 0 Then
                                    If tostr(rs.fields("Account_Dr")) <> "" Then
                                        sAccount = tostr(rs.fields("Account_Dr"))
                                    End If
                                End If
                                If rs_Voucher.fields("Credit") <> 0 Then
                                    If tostr(rs.fields("Account_Cr")) <> "" Then
                                        sAccount = tostr(rs.fields("Account_Cr"))
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
                        ElseIf SL_Tag <> 0 Then
                            If InStr("ABPN", SL_Cat) <> 0 Then
                                If (DebtorF = True And rs_Voucher.fields("Debit") <> 0) Or (CreditorF And rs_Voucher.fields("Credit") <> 0) Then
                                    sAccount = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Account"))
                                Else
                                    sAccount = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("SAccount"))
                                End If
                            Else
                                If (DebtorF = True And rs_Voucher.fields("Debit") <> 0) Or (CreditorF And rs_Voucher.fields("Credit") <> 0) Then
                                    sAccount = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("SAccount"))
                                Else
                                    sAccount = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Account"))
                                End If
                            End If
                        ElseIf SL_Tag = 0 Then
                            If (DebtorF And rs_Voucher.fields("Debit") <> 0) Or (CreditorF And rs_Voucher.fields("Credit") <> 0) Then
                                If tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("SAccount")) <> "" And toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Open_ItemF")) Then
                                    ' Only One Open Item
                                    sAccount = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("SAccount"))
                                Else
                                    sAccount = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Account"))
                                End If
                            Else
                                sAccount = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Account"))
                            End If
                        End If
                        nDebit = 0
                        nCredit = 0
                        If toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Debit")) < 0 Then
                            Nominal = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal"))
                            If Nominal >= 0 Then
                                nDebit = Abs(Nominal)
                            Else
                                nCredit = Abs(Nominal)
                            End If
                        End If
                        If toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Credit")) < 0 Then
                            Nominal = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal"))
                            If Nominal >= 0 Then
                                nCredit = Abs(Nominal)
                            Else
                                nDebit = Abs(Nominal)
                            End If
                        End If

                        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Type.BS_Flag, Account.Monetary_Flag, Account.Description from Account, [Type] Where [Type].Type=Account.Type and Account='" & sAccount & "'")
                        If toval(rs.fields("BS_Flag")) Then
                            sCT = ""
                        Else
                            sCT = IIf(tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("CT")) <> "", tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("CT")), CT)
                        End If
                        Description = tostr(rs.fields("Description"))

                        If toval(rs.fields("Monetary_Flag")) Then
                            If SL_Cat = "N" And sAccount <> tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("sAccount")) And Ccy <> tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Doc_CCY")) Then
                                sCurrency = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Doc_CCY"))
                                If nDebit <> 0 Then nDebit = Abs(toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal_OC")))
                                If nCredit <> 0 Then nCredit = Abs(toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal_OC")))
                                Rate = rs_Voucher.fields("SLRate") * vRate
                                Rate_RC = rs_Voucher.fields("SLRate") * vRate_RC
                            Else
                                sCurrency = Ccy
                                Rate = vRate
                                Rate_RC = vRate_RC
                            End If
                        Else
                            sCurrency = br_currency
                            nDebit = nDebit * vRate
                            nCredit = nCredit * vRate
                            Rate = 1
                            Rate_RC = vRate_RC / vRate
                        End If

                        j = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, sAccount, 2, sCurrency, 7, sCT)
                        If j < i Then
                            ' Combine for the same account
                            i = i - 1
                        Else
                            BufferData(0, j) = sAccount
                            BufferData(1, j) = Description
                            BufferData(2, j) = sCurrency
                            BufferData(3, j) = 0
                            BufferData(4, j) = 0
                            BufferData(5, j) = 0
                            BufferData(6, j) = 0
                            BufferData(7, j) = sCT
                            BufferData(8, j) = 0
                            BufferData(9, j) = 0
                        End If

                        BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) + nDebit
                        BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) + nCredit

                        If BufferData(3, j) > BufferData(4, j) Then
                            BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) - BufferData(4, j)
                            nDebit = nDebit - BufferData(4, j)
                            nCredit = nCredit - BufferData(4, j)
                            BufferData(4, j) = 0
                        Else
                            BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) - BufferData(3, j)
                            nDebit = nDebit - BufferData(3, j)
                            nCredit = nCredit - BufferData(3, j)
                            BufferData(3, j) = 0
                        End If

                        BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(3, j) * Rate
                        BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(4, j) * Rate

                        BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(3, j) * Rate_RC
                        BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(4, j) * Rate_RC

                        Num_Debit = Num_Debit + toval(BufferData(5, j))
                        Num_Credit = Num_Credit + toval(BufferData(6, j))
                        i = i + 1
                        rs_Voucher.MoveNext()
                    Loop

                    Dim K As Integer
                    Dim L As Integer
                    Dim TDebit As Double
                    Dim TCredit As Double
                    Dim eDebit As Double
                    Dim eCredit As Double
                    Dim eRate As Double
                    Dim eRate_RC As Double
                    Dim Debit_OC As Double
                    Dim Credit_OC As Double
                    Dim Debit_CC As Double
                    Dim Credit_CC As Double
                    Dim nDebit_CC As Double
                    Dim nCredit_CC As Double
                    Dim rsV As RemoteSet
                    Dim m As Integer
                    j = 0
                    K = i

                    sCT = CT    ' Voucher CT
                    Do While j < i And SL_Cat <> "N" And SL_Cat <> "M" And SL_Cat <> "X" And SL_Cat <> "J" ' and Not Memorial Voucher
                        nDebit_CC = 0
                        nCredit_CC = 0
                        nDebit = 0
                        nCredit = 0
                        nDebit_RC = 0
                        nCredit_RC = 0
                        TDebit = 0
                        TCredit = 0
                        eCredit = 0
                        eDebit = 0
                        sAccount = ""

                        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select cast(Max(cast(CLP.FX_Flag as int)) as Bit) as FX_Flag, CLProcess.AccTo as Account, SL.Doc_No, SL.Doc_CCY, SL.PaymentF, sum(SL.Nominal_OC) as Nominal_OC, sum(SL.Nominal) as Nominal_CC from pSL SL Inner Join pVoucher on (SL.Voucher=pVoucher.Voucher) Inner Join CLProcess on (CLProcess.AccFrom=SL.Account and CLProcess.FXJ_Flag=1) Inner Join Account A1 on (A1.Account=CLProcess.AccFrom) Outer Apply (Select FX_Flag from CLProcess C Where C.AccFrom=SL.Account and C.FX_Flag=1) CLP Where (SL.PaymentF=1 or A1.Open_ItemF=0) and pVoucher.Voucher='" & Voucher & "' and SL.Account='" & BufferData(0, j) & "' and (pVoucher.Currency<>'" & br_currency & "' or pVoucher.Currency<>'" & br_currency_1 & "' or SL.Doc_CCY<>'" & br_currency & "' or SL.Doc_CCY<>'" & br_currency_1 & "') Group By CLProcess.AccTo, SL.Doc_No, SL.Doc_CCY, SL.PaymentF")
                        Do While Not rs.Eof
                            If toval(rs.fields("PaymentF")) Then
                                Select Case SL_Cat
                                    Case "C"
                                        rsV = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Currency, Date, Rate, Rate_RC, DebtorF as DebtorF, SL_Cat from nVoucher Voucher Where Voucher.Voucher='" & rs.fields("Doc_No") & "'")
                                    Case "O"    ' Overpaid
                                        rsV = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Currency, Date, Rate, Rate_RC, DebtorF as DebtorF, SL_Cat from pVoucher Voucher Where Voucher.Voucher='" & rs.fields("Doc_No") & "'")
                                    Case Else
                                        rsV = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Currency, Date, Rate, Rate_RC, DebtorF as DebtorF, SL_Cat from Voucher  Voucher Where Voucher.Voucher='" & rs.fields("Doc_No") & "'")
                                End Select
                            Else
                                rsV = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Currency, Date, Rate, Rate_RC, CreditorF as DebtorF, SL_Cat from pVoucher Voucher Where Voucher.Voucher='" & Voucher & "'")
                            End If

                            If Not rsV.Eof Then
                                sAccount = tostr(rs.fields("Account"))

                                If br_currency.ToUpper = tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")).ToUpper Then
                                    eRate = 1
                                Else
                                    If toval(rs.fields("PaymentF")) Then
                                        If Not toval(rs.fields("FX_Flag")) Or (tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")).ToUpper = tostr(rsV.fields("Currency")).ToUpper And Year(vDate) = Year(rsV.fields("Date")) And Month(vDate) = Month(rsV.fields("Date"))) Then
                                            eRate = toval(rsV.fields("Rate"))
                                        ElseIf tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")).ToUpper = Ccy.ToUpper Then
                                            eRate = Revaluation_Rate(tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), vDate, br_currency)
                                        Else
                                            eRate = Revaluation_Rate(tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), vDate, br_currency)
                                        End If
                                    ElseIf tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")).ToUpper = Ccy.ToUpper Then
                                        eRate = Revaluation_Rate(tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), vDate, br_currency)
                                    Else
                                        eRate = Revaluation_Rate(tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), vDate, br_currency)
                                    End If
                                End If

                                If br_currency_1.ToUpper <> "" AndAlso br_currency_1.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper Then
                                    If br_currency_1.ToUpper = tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")).ToUpper Then
                                        eRate_RC = 1
                                    Else
                                        If toval(rs.fields("PaymentF")) Then
                                            If Not toval(rs.fields("FX_Flag")) Or (tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")).ToUpper = tostr(rsV.fields("Currency")).ToUpper And Year(vDate) = Year(rsV.fields("Date")) And Month(vDate) = Month(rsV.fields("Date"))) Then
                                                eRate_RC = toval(rsV.fields("Rate_RC"))
                                            ElseIf tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")).ToUpper = Ccy.ToUpper Then
                                                eRate_RC = Revaluation_Rate_RC(tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), vDate, br_currency_1)
                                            Else
                                                eRate_RC = Revaluation_Rate_RC(tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), vDate, br_currency_1)
                                            End If
                                        ElseIf tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")).ToUpper = Ccy.ToUpper Then
                                            eRate_RC = Revaluation_Rate_RC(tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), vDate, br_currency_1)
                                        Else
                                            eRate_RC = Revaluation_Rate_RC(tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), vDate, br_currency_1)
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                Else
                                    eRate_RC = eRate
                                End If

                                Debit_OC = 0
                                Credit_OC = 0
                                Debit_CC = 0
                                Credit_CC = 0

                                If rsV.fields("DebtorF") Then
                                    If InStr("ABP", SL_Cat) <> 0 Then
                                        If rsV.fields("DebtorF") = DebtorF Then
                                            Credit_OC = rs.fields("Nominal_OC")
                                            Credit_CC = rs.fields("Nominal_CC")
                                        Else
                                            Debit_OC = rs.fields("Nominal_OC")
                                            Debit_CC = rs.fields("Nominal_CC")
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        If rsV.fields("DebtorF") = DebtorF Then
                                            Debit_OC = rs.fields("Nominal_OC")
                                            Debit_CC = rs.fields("Nominal_CC")
                                        Else
                                            Credit_OC = rs.fields("Nominal_OC")
                                            Credit_CC = rs.fields("Nominal_CC")
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                Else
                                    If InStr("ABP", SL_Cat) <> 0 Then
                                        If rsV.fields("DebtorF") = DebtorF Then
                                            Debit_OC = rs.fields("Nominal_OC")
                                            Debit_CC = rs.fields("Nominal_CC")
                                        Else
                                            Credit_OC = rs.fields("Nominal_OC")
                                            Credit_CC = rs.fields("Nominal_CC")
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        If rsV.fields("DebtorF") = DebtorF Then
                                            Credit_OC = rs.fields("Nominal_OC")
                                            Credit_CC = rs.fields("Nominal_CC")
                                        Else
                                            Debit_OC = rs.fields("Nominal_OC")
                                            Debit_CC = rs.fields("Nominal_CC")
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                End If

                                If Debit_OC < 0 Then
                                    Credit_OC = -Debit_OC
                                    Credit_CC = -Debit_CC
                                    Debit_OC = 0
                                    Debit_CC = 0
                                ElseIf Credit_OC < 0 Then
                                    Debit_OC = -Credit_OC
                                    Debit_CC = -Credit_CC
                                    Credit_OC = 0
                                    Credit_CC = 0
                                End If

                                m = Find_3_Element(BufferData, j, K - 1, 0, tostr(BufferData(0, j)), 2, tostr(rs.fields("Doc_CCY")), 7, "")
                                If m = K Then
                                    BufferData(0, K) = BufferData(0, j)
                                    BufferData(1, K) = ""
                                    BufferData(2, K) = rs.fields("Doc_CCY")
                                    BufferData(3, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(4, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(5, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(6, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(7, K) = ""
                                    BufferData(8, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(9, K) = 0
                                    K = K + 1
                                End If
                                If m > j Then
                                    BufferData(3, m) = BufferData(3, m) + Debit_OC
                                    BufferData(4, m) = BufferData(4, m) + Credit_OC
                                    BufferData(5, m) = BufferData(5, m) + Debit_OC * eRate
                                    BufferData(6, m) = BufferData(6, m) + Credit_OC * eRate
                                    BufferData(8, m) = BufferData(8, m) + Debit_OC * eRate_RC
                                    BufferData(9, m) = BufferData(9, m) + Credit_OC * eRate_RC
                                Else
                                    BufferData(5, m) = BufferData(5, m) - (Debit_CC * vRate - Debit_OC * eRate)
                                    BufferData(6, m) = BufferData(6, m) - (Credit_CC * vRate - Credit_OC * eRate)
                                    BufferData(8, m) = BufferData(8, m) - (Debit_CC * vRate_RC - Debit_OC * eRate_RC)
                                    BufferData(9, m) = BufferData(9, m) - (Credit_CC * vRate_RC - Credit_OC * eRate_RC)
                                End If

                                If BufferData(3, m) > BufferData(4, m) Then
                                    BufferData(3, m) = BufferData(3, m) - BufferData(4, m)
                                    BufferData(4, m) = 0
                                Else
                                    BufferData(4, m) = BufferData(4, m) - BufferData(3, m)
                                    BufferData(3, m) = 0
                                End If

                                If BufferData(5, m) > BufferData(6, m) Then
                                    BufferData(5, m) = BufferData(5, m) - BufferData(6, m)
                                    BufferData(6, m) = 0
                                Else
                                    BufferData(6, m) = BufferData(6, m) - BufferData(5, m)
                                    BufferData(5, m) = 0
                                End If

                                If BufferData(8, m) > BufferData(9, m) Then
                                    BufferData(8, m) = BufferData(8, m) - BufferData(9, m)
                                    BufferData(9, m) = 0
                                Else
                                    BufferData(9, m) = BufferData(9, m) - BufferData(8, m)
                                    BufferData(8, m) = 0
                                End If

                                ' Base Currency #1
                                eDebit = Debit_CC * vRate - Debit_OC * eRate
                                eCredit = Credit_CC * vRate - Credit_OC * eRate

                                L = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, K - 1, 0, sAccount, 2, br_currency, 7, sCT)
                                If L = K Then
                                    BufferData(0, K) = sAccount
                                    BufferData(1, K) = ""
                                    BufferData(2, K) = br_currency
                                    BufferData(3, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(4, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(5, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(6, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(7, K) = sCT
                                    BufferData(8, K) = 0
                                    BufferData(9, K) = 0
                                    K = K + 1
                                End If

                                If eDebit < 0 Or eCredit < 0 Then
                                    BufferData(5, L) = BufferData(5, L) - eCredit
                                    BufferData(6, L) = BufferData(6, L) - eDebit
                                Else
                                    BufferData(5, L) = BufferData(5, L) + eDebit
                                    BufferData(6, L) = BufferData(6, L) + eCredit
                                End If

                                If BufferData(5, L) > BufferData(6, L) Then
                                    BufferData(5, L) = BufferData(5, L) - BufferData(6, L)
                                    BufferData(6, L) = 0
                                Else
                                    BufferData(6, L) = BufferData(6, L) - BufferData(5, L)
                                    BufferData(5, L) = 0
                                End If

                                ' Base Currency #2
                                If br_currency_1.ToUpper <> "" AndAlso br_currency_1.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper AndAlso vRate_RC <> 0 AndAlso eRate_RC <> 0 Then
                                    eDebit = Debit_CC * vRate_RC - Debit_OC * eRate_RC
                                    eCredit = Credit_CC * vRate_RC - Credit_OC * eRate_RC

                                    L = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, K - 1, 0, sAccount, 2, br_currency_1, 7, sCT)
                                    If L = K Then
                                        BufferData(0, K) = sAccount
                                        BufferData(1, K) = ""
                                        BufferData(2, K) = br_currency_1
                                        BufferData(3, K) = 0
                                        BufferData(4, K) = 0
                                        BufferData(5, K) = 0
                                        BufferData(6, K) = 0
                                        BufferData(7, K) = sCT
                                        BufferData(8, K) = 0
                                        BufferData(9, K) = 0
                                        K = K + 1
                                    End If

                                    If eDebit < 0 Or eCredit < 0 Then
                                        BufferData(8, L) = BufferData(8, L) - eCredit
                                        BufferData(9, L) = BufferData(9, L) - eDebit
                                    Else
                                        BufferData(8, L) = BufferData(8, L) + eDebit
                                        BufferData(9, L) = BufferData(9, L) + eCredit
                                    End If

                                    If BufferData(8, L) > BufferData(9, L) Then
                                        BufferData(8, L) = BufferData(8, L) - BufferData(9, L)
                                        BufferData(9, L) = 0
                                    Else
                                        BufferData(9, L) = BufferData(9, L) - BufferData(8, L)
                                        BufferData(8, L) = 0
                                    End If
                                End If

                                If m > j Then
                                    nDebit_CC = nDebit_CC + Debit_CC
                                    nCredit_CC = nCredit_CC + Credit_CC

                                    nDebit = nDebit + Debit_CC * vRate
                                    nCredit = nCredit + Credit_CC * vRate

                                    nDebit_RC = nDebit_RC + Debit_CC * vRate_RC
                                    nCredit_RC = nCredit_RC + Credit_CC * vRate_RC
                                End If

                            End If

                            rs.MoveNext()
                        Loop
                        If rs.RecordCount <> 0 Then
                            BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) - nDebit
                            BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - nCredit
                            BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - BufferData(5, j)
                            BufferData(5, j) = 0
                            If BufferData(6, j) < 0 Then
                                BufferData(5, j) = -BufferData(6, j)
                                BufferData(6, j) = 0
                            End If

                            BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) - nDebit_RC
                            BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - nCredit_RC
                            BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - BufferData(8, j)
                            BufferData(8, j) = 0
                            If BufferData(9, j) < 0 Then
                                BufferData(8, j) = -BufferData(9, j)
                                BufferData(9, j) = 0
                            End If

                            If BufferData(2, j).ToUpper = br_currency.ToUpper Then
                                nDebit = BufferData(3, j) - nDebit
                                nCredit = BufferData(4, j) - nCredit
                                nCredit = nCredit - nDebit
                                nDebit = 0
                                If nCredit < 0 Then
                                    nDebit = -nCredit
                                    nCredit = 0
                                End If

                                BufferData(3, j) = nDebit
                                BufferData(4, j) = nCredit
                            ElseIf BufferData(2, j).ToUpper = br_currency_1.ToUpper AndAlso br_currency.ToUpper <> br_currency_1.ToUpper Then
                                nDebit_RC = BufferData(3, j) - nDebit_RC
                                nCredit_RC = BufferData(4, j) - nCredit_RC
                                nCredit_RC = nCredit_RC - nDebit_RC
                                nDebit_RC = 0
                                If nCredit_RC < 0 Then
                                    nDebit_RC = -nCredit_RC
                                    nCredit_RC = 0
                                End If

                                BufferData(3, j) = nDebit_RC
                                BufferData(4, j) = nCredit_RC
                            Else
                                nDebit_CC = BufferData(3, j) - nDebit_CC
                                nCredit_CC = BufferData(4, j) - nCredit_CC
                                nCredit_CC = nCredit_CC - nDebit_CC
                                nDebit_CC = 0
                                If nCredit_CC < 0 Then
                                    nDebit_CC = -nCredit_CC
                                    nCredit_CC = 0
                                End If

                                BufferData(3, j) = nDebit_CC
                                BufferData(4, j) = nCredit_CC
                            End If
                        End If
                        j = j + 1
                    Loop

                    ' Create Source Doc.
                    If SL_Cat = "P" Then
                        If DebtorF Then
                            sType = "CR"
                        Else
                            sType = "CP"
                        End If
                    Else
                        sType = "GJ"
                    End If
                    Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, sType, RefNo, RefType, ProAcc, ComAcc, SL_Cat, vDate, sAutoNum, Source)
                    If Source = "" Then
                        StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If

                    Select Case SL_Cat
                        Case "N"
                            If Not db.Execute("Update nVoucher Set Rate_RC=" & tovalSF(vRate_RC) & ", Posting_Flag=1, Posting_Opr='" & db_user & "', Posting_Time='" & Time() & "', Posting_Date='" & Format(Today.Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "', Source='" & Source & "' Where Posting_Flag=0 and Last_Date='" & Format(Last_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Last_Time='" & Last_Time & "' and Voucher='" & Voucher & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg
                        Case "M", "X"
                            If Not db.Execute("Update mVoucher Set Rate_RC=" & tovalSF(vRate_RC) & ", Posting_Flag=1, Posting_Opr='" & db_user & "', Posting_Time='" & Time() & "', Posting_Date='" & Format(Today.Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "', Source='" & Source & "' Where Posting_Flag=0 and Last_Date='" & Format(Last_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Last_Time='" & Last_Time & "' and Voucher='" & Voucher & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg
                        Case Else
                            If Not db.Execute("Update pVoucher Set Rate_RC=" & tovalSF(vRate_RC) & ", Posting_Flag=1, Posting_Opr='" & db_user & "', Posting_Time='" & Time() & "', Posting_Date='" & Format(Today.Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "', Source='" & Source & "' Where Posting_Flag=0 and Last_Date='" & Format(Last_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Last_Time='" & Last_Time & "' and Voucher='" & Voucher & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg
                    End Select

                    If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then
                        StrMsg = "Failed To Update Voucher Status ! "
                        GoTo ErrMsg
                    End If

                    ' Save Detail

                    Dim TotalDebit As Double = 0
                    Dim TotalCredit As Double = 0
                    Dim TotalDebit_RC As Double = 0
                    Dim TotalCredit_RC As Double = 0

                    Dim MaxRows As Integer = CType(BufferData, Object(,)).GetUpperBound(1) + 1
                    For i = 0 To MaxRows - 1
                        If Trim(BufferData(0, i)) <> "" And Trim(BufferData(2, i)) <> "" And (toval(BufferData(5, i)) <> 0 Or toval(BufferData(6, i)) <> 0 Or toval(BufferData(8, i)) <> 0 Or toval(BufferData(9, i)) <> 0) Then
                            Account = BufferData(0, i)
                            If tostr(BufferData(1, i)) = "" Then
                                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Description from Account Where Account='" & Account & "'")
                                BufferData(1, i) = tostr(rs.fields("Description"))
                            End If
                            Description = tostr(BufferData(1, i))
                            sCT = tostr(Trim(BufferData(7, i)))

                            rs_Journal.AddNew()
                            rs_Journal.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                            rs_Journal.fields("SortNo") = i
                            rs_Journal.fields("Account") = Account
                            rs_Journal.fields("Description") = Description
                            rs_Journal.fields("Currency") = tostr(BufferData(2, i))

                            If Not CalculationRoundingF Then
                                rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC") = toval(BufferData(3, i))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC") = toval(BufferData(4, i))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Debit") = toval(BufferData(5, i))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Credit") = toval(BufferData(6, i))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(BufferData(8, i))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(BufferData(9, i))
                            Else
                                rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC") = RoundCCY(toval(BufferData(3, i)), UCase(tostr(BufferData(2, i))), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC") = RoundCCY(toval(BufferData(4, i)), UCase(tostr(BufferData(2, i))), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Debit") = RoundCCY(toval(BufferData(5, i)), br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Credit") = RoundCCY(toval(BufferData(6, i)), br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC") = RoundCCY(toval(BufferData(8, i)), br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                                rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC") = RoundCCY(toval(BufferData(9, i)), br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                            End If

                            rs_Journal.fields("CT") = sCT
                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                            If Not rs_Journal.UpdateRow Then GoTo ErrMsg

                            TotalDebit = TotalDebit + rs_Journal.fields("Debit")
                            TotalCredit = TotalCredit + rs_Journal.fields("Credit")
                            TotalDebit_RC = TotalDebit_RC + rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC")
                            TotalCredit_RC = TotalCredit_RC + rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC")

                            'Updating Journal_Account
                            If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, Account, rs_Journal.fields("Currency"), sCT, rs_Journal.fields("Debit"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit"), rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC"), rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC"), StrMsg) Then GoTo errmsg
                        End If
                    Next i

                    If CalculationRoundingF AndAlso BL_Account <> "" AndAlso (TotalDebit <> TotalCredit OrElse TotalDebit_RC <> TotalCredit_RC) Then
                        If BL_AccountDesc = "" Then
                            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select A.AccGroup, A.Description, Type.BS_Flag from Account A Inner Join Type on (Type.Type=A.Type) Where A.Account='" & BL_Account & "'")
                            BL_AccountDesc = tostr(rs.fields("Description"))
                            BL_AccountBSFlag = toval(rs.fields("BS_Flag"))
                        End If

                        If TotalDebit <> TotalCredit Then
                            rs_Journal.AddNew()
                            rs_Journal.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                            rs_Journal.fields("SortNo") = i
                            rs_Journal.fields("Account") = BL_Account
                            rs_Journal.fields("Description") = BL_AccountDesc
                            rs_Journal.fields("Currency") = br_currency

                            If TotalDebit > TotalCredit Then
                                TotalCredit = TotalDebit - TotalCredit
                                TotalDebit = 0
                            Else
                                TotalDebit = TotalCredit - TotalDebit
                                TotalCredit = 0
                            End If

                            rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC") = 0
                            rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC") = 0
                            rs_Journal.fields("Debit") = TotalDebit
                            rs_Journal.fields("Credit") = TotalCredit
                            If br_currency_1 = br_currency Then
                                rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC") = TotalDebit
                                rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC") = TotalCredit
                            Else
                                rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC") = 0
                                rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC") = 0
                            End If

                            If BL_AccountBSFlag Then
                                rs_Journal.fields("CT") = ""
                            Else
                                rs_Journal.fields("CT") = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("CT"))
                            End If

                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                            If Not rs_Journal.UpdateRow Then GoTo ErrMsg

                            'Updating Journal_Account
                            If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, BL_Account, rs_Journal.fields("Currency"), rs_Journal.fields("CT"), rs_Journal.fields("Debit"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit"), rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC"), rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC"), StrMsg) Then GoTo ErrMsg
                        End If

                        If br_currency <> br_currency_1 And TotalDebit_RC <> TotalCredit_RC Then
                            i = i + 1
                            rs_Journal.AddNew()
                            rs_Journal.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                            rs_Journal.fields("SortNo") = i
                            rs_Journal.fields("Account") = BL_Account
                            rs_Journal.fields("Description") = BL_AccountDesc
                            rs_Journal.fields("Currency") = br_currency_1

                            If TotalDebit_RC > TotalCredit_RC Then
                                TotalCredit_RC = TotalDebit_RC - TotalCredit_RC
                                TotalDebit_RC = 0
                            Else
                                TotalDebit_RC = TotalCredit_RC - TotalDebit_RC
                                TotalCredit_RC = 0
                            End If
                            rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC") = 0
                            rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC") = 0
                            rs_Journal.fields("Debit") = 0
                            rs_Journal.fields("Credit") = 0
                            rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC") = TotalDebit_RC
                            rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC") = TotalCredit_RC

                            If BL_AccountBSFlag Then
                                rs_Journal.fields("CT") = ""
                            Else
                                rs_Journal.fields("CT") = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("CT"))
                            End If

                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                            rs_Journal.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                            If Not rs_Journal.UpdateRow Then GoTo ErrMsg
                            'Updating Journal_Account
                            If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, BL_Account, rs_Journal.fields("Currency"), rs_Journal.fields("CT"), rs_Journal.fields("Debit"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit"), rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC"), rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC"), rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC"), StrMsg) Then GoTo ErrMsg
                        End If
                    End If

                    If CBool(toval(GetAppSetting("GenerateAcclinkByVoucherF"))) AndAlso SL_Cat = "P" Then
                        If Not Process_AccLink_By_Voucher(Voucher, StrMsg) Then
                            GoTo ErrMsg
                        End If
                    End If

                    RowNum = RowNum + 1
                Loop

                ' Finally
                If Not rs_Journal.Eof Then
                    If Not rs_Journal.Update Then GoTo ErrMsg
                End If

                If GetLockedObjectF Then
                    If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then
                        StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
                        GoTo ErrMsg
                    End If
                    db.Committrans()
                End If
            Loop
        Next
        rs_Voucher.CloseRemoteSet()

        If GetLockedObjectF Then
            db.Begintrans()
        End If

        If GetLockedObjectF Then
            Dim rs_branch As RemoteSet
            rs_branch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch from Branch where Parent='" & br_branch & "'")
            Do While Not rs_branch.Eof
                If Not Posting_Journal(rs_branch.fields("Branch"), PDate) Then GoTo ErrMsg
                rs_branch.MoveNext()
            Loop
        End If

        Posting_Journal = True
        Exit Function
ErrMsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch)
        Posting_Journal = False
    End Function
    Private Function Process_AccLink_By_Voucher(ByVal RefVoucher As String, ByRef ErrMsg As String) As Boolean
        Try
            Dim SQL As String = ""
            Dim AccNo As Long
            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            Dim rsAcc As RemoteSet
            Dim PType As String

            For Each PType In New String() {"VO", "PT"}
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Type from Mparam Where Type='" & PType & "' and MemorialF=1")
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    Select Case PType
                        Case "VO"
                            SQL = "SELECT * FROM VAT_OUT_PROCESS_BY_VOUCHER('" & PType & "','" & RefVoucher & "')"
                        Case "PT"
                            SQL = "SELECT * FROM TAX_PREPAID_PROCESS_BY_VOUCHER('" & PType & "','" & RefVoucher & "')"
                    End Select
                    If SQL = "" Then
                        Throw New Exception("No Process type !")
                    End If
                    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL)
                    rsAcc = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from AccLink Where 1<>1")
                    Do While Not rs.Eof
                        If toval(rs.fields("Amount_1")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_2")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_3")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_4")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_5")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_6")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_7")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_8")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_9")) <> 0 Or toval(rs.fields("Amount_10")) <> 0 Then
                            rsAcc.AddNew()
                            If Not db.backup_record(rs, rsAcc) Then Throw New Exception(db.ErrDesc)
                            AccNo = db.GetIdentity("ACCLINK") + 1
                            rsAcc.fields("AccNo") = AccNo
                            rsAcc.fields("RefVoucher") = RefVoucher
                            rsAcc.fields("LUserID") = db_user
                            rsAcc.fields("LDate") = Today.Date
                            rsAcc.fields("LTime") = Time()
                            rsAcc.fields("PDate") = Today.Date
                            If Not rsAcc.Update Then Throw New Exception(rsAcc.ErrDesc)
                            If Not Create_AccLink_Memorial(AccNo, toval(rs.fields("Rate")), toval(rs.fields("Rate_RC"))) Then
                                Throw New Exception("Can't Create Memorial Journal !")
                            End If
                        End If
                        rs.MoveNext()
                    Loop
                End If
            Next

            Process_AccLink_By_Voucher = True
        Catch ex As Exception
            ErrMsg = ex.Message
            Process_AccLink_By_Voucher = False
        End Try
    End Function
    Private Function UnPosting_Journal(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal pdate1 As Date, ByVal pdate2 As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs_Voucher As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo ErrMsg
        If Not db.GetLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
            GoTo ErrMsg
        End If

        If Not db.Execute("Delete J From nJournal J, nVoucher V where V.Voucher=J.Voucher and SourceF=0 and V.Status='U' and V.SL_Cat NOT IN ('J', 'M') and V.Posting_Flag=1 and V.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and V.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and V.Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete J From pJournal J, pVoucher V where V.Voucher=J.Voucher and SourceF=0 and V.Status='U' and V.SL_Cat NOT IN ('J', 'M') and V.Posting_Flag=1 and V.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and V.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and V.Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete J From mJournal J, mVoucher V where V.Voucher=J.Voucher and SourceF=0 and V.Status='U' and V.SL_Cat NOT IN ('J', 'M') and V.Posting_Flag=1 and V.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and V.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and V.Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg

        If Not db.Execute("Delete S from Source S where S.Status='U' and S.SL_Cat NOT IN ('J', 'M') and S.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and S.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and S.Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg

        'Update Voucher
        If Not db.Execute("Update Voucher set Source='',Posting_Flag=0 from nVoucher Voucher Where BackDatedF<>1 and SourceF=0 and Voucher.Status='U' and Voucher.SL_Cat NOT IN ('J', 'M') and Voucher.Posting_Flag=1 and Voucher.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update Voucher set Source='',Posting_Flag=0 from pVoucher Voucher Where BackDatedF<>1 and SourceF=0 and Voucher.Status='U' and Voucher.SL_Cat NOT IN ('J', 'M') and Voucher.Posting_Flag=1 and Voucher.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update Voucher set Source='',Posting_Flag=0 from mVoucher Voucher Where BackDatedF<>1 and SourceF=0 and Voucher.Status='U' and Voucher.SL_Cat NOT IN ('J', 'M') and Voucher.Posting_Flag=1 and Voucher.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo ErrMsg

        If CBool(toval(GetAppSetting("GenerateAcclinkByVoucherF"))) Then
            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Distinct Acclink.Voucher from AccLink, pVoucher Voucher Where AccLink.VoucherF=1 and Acclink.RefVoucher=Voucher.Voucher and Voucher.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "'")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                If Not Cancel_AccLink_Memorial(rs.fields("Voucher")) Then
                    StrMsg = "Can't Cancel Acclink Memorial (Voucher : " & rs.fields("Voucher") & ") !"
                    GoTo ErrMsg
                End If
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            ' Delete AccLink
            If Not db.Execute("Delete AccLink from AccLink, pVoucher Voucher Where AccLink.VoucherF=0 and Acclink.RefVoucher=Voucher.Voucher and Voucher.Date>='" & Format(pdate1, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Date<='" & Format(pdate2, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then
                GoTo ErrMsg
            End If
        End If

        If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
            GoTo ErrMsg
        End If
        UnPosting_Journal = True

        Exit Function
ErrMsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch)
        UnPosting_Journal = False
    End Function

    Private Function Update_Budget_Balance(ByVal Account As String, ByVal Branch As String, ByVal Mth As Integer, ByVal Yr As Integer, ByVal nDebit As Double, ByVal nCredit As Double, ByVal nDebit_RC As Double, ByVal nCredit_RC As Double) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim i As Integer
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Do
            'Budget
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * From Budget Where Account='" & Account & "' and Branch='" & Branch & "' and [Year]=" & Yr, adOpenKeyset)
            If rs.Eof Then
                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("Branch") = Branch
                rs.fields("Year") = Yr
