                rs.fields("Account") = Account
            Else
                rs.Edit()
            End If
            rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth)) + nDebit - nCredit
            rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth)) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
            If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()

            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Parent from Account where Account ='" & Account & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
            rs_Account.CloseRemoteSet()
        Loop Until Account = "0"
        Update_Budget_Balance = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Budget_Balance = False
    End Function
    Private Function Update_Budget_TOC_Balance(ByVal Account As String, ByVal Branch As String, ByVal Mth As Integer, ByVal Yr As Integer, ByVal TOC As String, ByVal nDebit As Double, ByVal nCredit As Double, ByVal nDebit_RC As Double, ByVal nCredit_RC As Double) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim i As Integer
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Do
            'Budget
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * From Budget_TOC Where Account='" & Account & "' and Branch='" & Branch & "' and [Year]=" & Yr & " and TOC='" & TOC & "'", adOpenKeyset)
            If rs.Eof Then
                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("Branch") = Branch
                rs.fields("Year") = Yr
                rs.fields("Account") = Account
                rs.fields("TOC") = TOC
            Else
                rs.Edit()
            End If
            rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth)) + nDebit - nCredit
            rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth)) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
            If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()

            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Parent from Account where Account ='" & Account & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
            rs_Account.CloseRemoteSet()
        Loop Until Account = "0"
        Update_Budget_TOC_Balance = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Budget_TOC_Balance = False
    End Function

    Private Function Update_Ledger_Balance(ByVal Account As String, ByVal Branch As String, ByVal Mth As Integer, ByVal Yr As Integer, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal nDebit As Double, ByVal nCredit As Double, ByVal nDebit_RC As Double, ByVal nCredit_RC As Double) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim i As Integer
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Do
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL where Account ='" & Account & "' and Branch ='" & Branch & "' and [month] =" & Mth & " and [year] =" & Yr, adOpenKeyset)
            If rs.Eof Then
                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("Branch") = Branch
                rs.fields("month") = Mth
                rs.fields("year") = Yr
                rs.fields("Account") = Account
                rs.fields("starting") = 0
                rs.fields("ending") = 0
                rs.fields("starting_RC") = 0
                rs.fields("ending_RC") = 0
                rs.fields("Debit") = 0
                rs.fields("Credit") = 0
                rs.fields("Debit_RC") = 0
                rs.fields("Credit_RC") = 0
            Else
                rs.Edit()
            End If
            rs.fields("Ending") = toval(rs.fields("Ending")) + nDebit - nCredit
            rs.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Ending_RC")) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
            rs.fields("Debit") = toval(rs.fields("Debit")) + nDebit
            rs.fields("Credit") = toval(rs.fields("Credit")) + nCredit
            rs.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC")) + nDebit_RC
            rs.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC")) + nCredit_RC

            For i = PDate.Day To 31
                rs.fields("Balance_" & i) = toval(rs.fields("Balance_" & i)) + nDebit - nCredit
                rs.fields("Balance_RC_" & i) = toval(rs.fields("Balance_RC_" & i)) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
            Next i
            If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()

            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Parent from Account where Account ='" & Account & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
            rs_Account.CloseRemoteSet()
        Loop Until Account = "0"
        Update_Ledger_Balance = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Ledger_Balance = False
    End Function

    Private Function Update_Ledger_FX_Balance(ByVal Account As String, ByVal Branch As String, ByVal Mth As Integer, ByVal Yr As Integer, ByVal Currency As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal nDebit As Double, ByVal nCredit As Double, ByVal nDebit_OC As Double, ByVal nCredit_OC As Double, ByVal nDebit_RC As Double, ByVal nCredit_RC As Double) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim i As Integer
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Do
            'GL_FX
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * From GL_FX where Account ='" & Account & "' and Branch ='" & Branch & "' and [month] =" & Mth & " and [year] =" & Yr & " and [Currency]='" & Currency & "'", adOpenKeyset)
            If rs.Eof Then
                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("Branch") = Branch
                rs.fields("Currency") = Currency
                rs.fields("month") = Mth
                rs.fields("year") = Yr
                rs.fields("Account") = Account
                rs.fields("ending") = 0
                rs.fields("ending_bc") = 0
                rs.fields("ending_rc") = 0
                rs.fields("Debit") = 0
                rs.fields("Credit") = 0
                rs.fields("Debit_BC") = 0
                rs.fields("Credit_BC") = 0
                rs.fields("Debit_RC") = 0
                rs.fields("Credit_RC") = 0
            Else
                rs.Edit()
            End If
            rs.fields("Ending") = toval(rs.fields("Ending")) + nDebit_OC - nCredit_OC
            rs.fields("Debit") = toval(rs.fields("Debit")) + nDebit_OC
            rs.fields("Credit") = toval(rs.fields("Credit")) + nCredit_OC

            rs.fields("Ending_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Ending_BC")) + nDebit - nCredit
            rs.fields("Debit_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Debit_BC")) + nDebit
            rs.fields("Credit_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Credit_BC")) + nCredit

            rs.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Ending_RC")) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
            rs.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC")) + nDebit_RC
            rs.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC")) + nCredit_RC

            For i = PDate.Day To 31
                rs.fields("Balance_" & i) = toval(rs.fields("Balance_" & i)) + nDebit_OC - nCredit_OC
            Next i

            If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()

            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Parent from Account where Account ='" & Account & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
            rs_Account.CloseRemoteSet()
        Loop Until Account = "0"
        Update_Ledger_FX_Balance = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Ledger_FX_Balance = False
    End Function

    Private Function Posting_Process(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Journal As RemoteSet
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Branch As RemoteSet

        Dim Mth As Integer
        Dim Yr As Integer
        Dim Account As String
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_OC As Double
        Dim nCredit_OC As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double
        Dim VCurrency As String
        Dim sProAcc As String
        Dim sComAcc As String
        Dim CT As String
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim SDate As Date
        Dim Op_Flag As Boolean

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        Mth = Month(PDate)
        Yr = Year(PDate)

        ' Set Branch Posting Parameter First 
        Dim ContinueProcessFlag As Boolean
        rs_Branch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Month, Year, End_Date, Op_Flag, ShariaF from Branch Where Branch='" & br_branch & "'")
        If (rs_Branch.fields("Month") + rs_Branch.fields("Year") * 12 + 1) = (Mth + Yr * 12) And DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, rs_Branch.fields("End_Date"), PDate) >= 0 Then
            ContinueProcessFlag = True
        Else
            ContinueProcessFlag = False
        End If
        SDate = rs_Branch.fields("End_Date")
        Op_Flag = toval(rs_Branch.fields("OP_FLAG"))

        If ContinueProcessFlag AndAlso Op_Flag Then
            ' Posting to Ledger First
            If Not Posting_Journal(br_branch, PDate, True) Then
                StrMsg = "Posting To Ledger Failed !" & StrMsg
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
        End If

        If Not db.GetLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        If ContinueProcessFlag Then
            ' Update Branch Ending Date
            If Not db.Execute("Update Branch Set End_Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Where Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

            If Op_Flag Then
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select Top 1 Voucher.Voucher from pVoucher Voucher where Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Voucher.Status<>'U' and Voucher.Date Between '" & Format(DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, SDate), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    StrMsg = "Branch : " & br_branch & ", Please Check Payment/Receipt Voucher(s) with Not-Approved Status !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If

                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select Top 1 Voucher.Voucher from mVoucher Voucher where Voucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Voucher.Status<>'U' and Voucher.Date Between '" & Format(DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, SDate), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    StrMsg = "Branch : " & br_branch & ", Please Check Journal Memo Voucher(s) with Not-Approved Status !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If

                ' Create Journal Balance Before
                If Mth = 1 Then
                    If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, SDate, PDate) > 0 AndAlso DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, Last_date_of_period(PDate, 1)) = 0 Then
                        If Not Generate_Balance_Before(br_branch, Mth, Yr, PDate) Then
                            StrMsg = "Failed in Generate Balance Before !" & StrMsg
                            GoTo errmsg
                        End If
                    End If
                End If

                ' Create Recurring Memorial
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Voucher from mVoucher Voucher where RecurNo>0 and voucher.date between '" & Format(DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, SDate), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='U' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'")
                Do While Not rs.Eof
                    If Not Create_Recurring_Voucher(rs.fields("Voucher")) Then GoTo errmsg
                    rs.MoveNext()
                Loop

                ' Create Reversal Memorial
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Voucher from mVoucher Voucher where AutoReverseF=1 and voucher.date between '" & Format(DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, SDate), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='U' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'")
                Do While Not rs.Eof
                    If Not Create_Reversal_Voucher(rs.fields("Voucher")) Then GoTo errmsg
                    rs.MoveNext()
                Loop

                ' Execute Paid and Fund Elimination 
                PaidEliminationF = PaidEliminationF Or toval(rs_Branch.fields("ShariaF"))
                FundEliminationF = FundEliminationF Or toval(rs_Branch.fields("ShariaF"))

                If PaidEliminationF Then
                    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select top 1 AccFrom from CLProcess Where pElimination_Flag=1")
                    If Not rs.Eof Then
                        If Not Generate_Paid_Elimination(br_branch, DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, SDate), PDate) Then
                            StrMsg = "Generate Paid Elimination Failed !" & StrMsg
                            GoTo errmsg
                        End If
                    End If
                End If

                If FundEliminationF Then
                    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select top 1 AccFrom from CLProcess Where FElimination_Flag=1")
                    If Not rs.Eof Then
                        If Not Generate_Fund_Elimination(br_branch, DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, SDate), PDate) Then
                            StrMsg = "Generate Fund Elimination Failed !" & StrMsg
                            GoTo errmsg
                        End If
                    End If
                End If

                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 Branch from cstallocationH Where Branch='" & br_branch & "'")
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    If Not Generate_Cost_Allocation(br_branch, DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, SDate), PDate) Then
                        StrMsg = "Generate Cost Allocation Failed !" & StrMsg
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                End If

                'If Not Update_Installment(br_branch, PDate) Then GoTo errmsg
            End If

            Dim AnyTransactionF As Boolean
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 S.Source from Source S Where S.Status='U' and S.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and S.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            AnyTransactionF = (Not rs.Eof)

            If AnyTransactionF Then
                rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select J.Account, J.Currency, S.Date, sum(J.Debit_OC) as Debit_OC, sum(J.Credit_OC) as Credit_OC, sum(J.Debit) as Debit, sum(J.Credit) as Credit, sum(J.Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(J.Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from Journal_Account J, Source S Where S.Status='U' and J.Source=S.Source and S.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and S.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group By J.Account, J.Currency, S.Date", adOpenForwardonly)
                Do While Not rs_Journal.Eof
                    Account = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Account"))
                    VCurrency = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Currency"))
                    nDebit_OC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC"))
                    nCredit_OC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC"))
                    nDebit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit"))
                    nCredit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit"))
                    nDebit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC"))
                    nCredit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC"))
                    If Not Update_Ledger_Balance(Account, br_branch, Mth, Yr, rs_Journal.fields("Date"), nDebit, nCredit, nDebit_RC, nCredit_RC) Then GoTo errmsg
                    If Not Update_Ledger_FX_Balance(Account, br_branch, Mth, Yr, VCurrency, rs_Journal.fields("Date"), nDebit, nCredit, nDebit_OC, nCredit_OC, nDebit_RC, nCredit_RC) Then GoTo errmsg
                    If BudgetFeaturesF Then If Not Update_Budget_Balance(Account, br_branch, Mth, Yr, nDebit, nCredit, nDebit_RC, nCredit_RC) Then GoTo errmsg
                    rs_Journal.MoveNext()
                Loop
                rs_Journal.CloseRemoteSet()

                ' Process Sub Ledger (CA)
                rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select J.Account, S.ComAcc, J.Currency, sum(J.Debit_OC) as Debit_OC, sum(J.Credit_OC) as Credit_OC, sum(J.Debit) as Debit, sum(J.Credit) as Credit, sum(J.Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(J.Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from pJournal J, pVoucher S, ComAcc C Where C.ComAcc=S.ComAcc and C.Account=J.Account and S.SL_Cat='P' and S.Status='U' and J.Voucher=S.Voucher and S.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and S.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group By J.Account, S.ComAcc, J.Currency", adOpenForwardonly)
                Do While Not rs_Journal.Eof
                    Account = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Account"))
                    sComAcc = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("ComAcc"))
                    VCurrency = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Currency"))
                    nDebit_OC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC"))
                    nCredit_OC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC"))
                    nDebit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit"))
                    nCredit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit"))
                    nDebit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit_RC"))
                    nCredit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit_RC"))

                    'CA_FX
                    If sComAcc <> "" Then
                        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from CA_FX where Account ='" & Account & "' and Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and [month] =" & Mth & " and [year] =" & Yr & " and [Currency]='" & VCurrency & "' and ComAcc='" & sComAcc & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                        If rs.Eof Then
                            rs.AddNew()
                            rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                            rs.fields("Currency") = VCurrency
                            rs.fields("month") = Mth
                            rs.fields("year") = Yr
                            rs.fields("Account") = Account
                            rs.fields("ComAcc") = sComAcc
                            rs.fields("ending_bc") = 0
                            rs.fields("ending") = 0
                            rs.fields("Debit_BC") = 0
                            rs.fields("Credit_BC") = 0
                            rs.fields("Debit") = 0
                            rs.fields("Credit") = 0
                            rs.fields("Debit_RC") = 0
                            rs.fields("Credit_RC") = 0
                        Else
                            rs.Edit()
                        End If
                        rs.fields("Ending_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Ending_BC")) + nDebit - nCredit
                        rs.fields("Ending") = toval(rs.fields("Ending")) + nDebit_OC - nCredit_OC
                        rs.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Ending_RC")) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
                        rs.fields("Debit_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Debit_BC")) + nDebit
                        rs.fields("Credit_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Credit_BC")) + nCredit
                        rs.fields("Debit") = toval(rs.fields("Debit")) + nDebit_OC
                        rs.fields("Credit") = toval(rs.fields("Credit")) + nCredit_OC
                        rs.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC")) + nDebit_RC
                        rs.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC")) + nCredit_RC
                        If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                    rs_Journal.MoveNext()
                Loop
                rs_Journal.CloseRemoteSet()

                ' Process CT Ledger Only for PL Account 
                ' Original Version CT and Account Loop for Tree Structure
                'rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select J.Account, J.CT, sum(J.Debit) as Debit, sum(J.Credit) as Credit, sum(J.Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(J.Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from Journal_Account J, Source S, Account A, [Type] T , CT C Where C.CT=J.CT and T.Type=A.Type and T.PL_Flag=1 and A.Account=J.Account and S.Status='U' and J.Source=S.Source and S.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and S.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group By J.Account, J.CT", adOpenForwardonly)
                'Do While Not rs_Journal.Eof
                '    Account = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Account"))
                '    nDebit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit"))
                '    nCredit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit"))
                '    nDebit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit_RC"))
                '    nCredit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit_RC"))
                '    Do
                '        CT = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("CT"))
                '        Do
                '            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from CTL where Account ='" & Account & "' and Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and [month] =" & Mth & " and [year] =" & Yr & " and CT ='" & CT & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                '            If rs.Eof Then
                '                rs.AddNew()
                '                rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                '                rs.fields("Account") = Account
                '                rs.fields("month") = Mth
                '                rs.fields("year") = Yr
                '                rs.fields("CT") = CT
                '            Else
                '                rs.Edit()
                '            End If
                '            rs.fields("Balance") = toval(rs.fields("Balance")) + nDebit - nCredit
                '            rs.fields("Balance_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Balance_RC")) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
                '            If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg

                '            If BudgetFeaturesF Then
                '                'CTBudget
                '                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from CTBudget where Account='" & Account & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Year]=" & Yr & " and CT='" & CT & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                '                If rs.Eof Then
                '                    rs.AddNew()
                '                    rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                '                    rs.fields("Account") = Account
                '                    rs.fields("year") = Yr
                '                    rs.fields("CT") = CT
                '                Else
                '                    rs.Edit()
                '                End If
                '                rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth)) + nDebit - nCredit
                '                rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth)) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
                '                If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
                '            End If

                '            Dim rs_CT As RemoteSet
                '            rs_CT = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Parent from CT Where CT ='" & CT & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
                '            CT = tostr(rs_CT.fields("Parent"))
                '            rs_CT.CloseRemoteSet()
                '        Loop Until CT = "0"
                '        rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Parent from Account where Account ='" & Account & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
                '        Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                '        rs_Account.CloseRemoteSet()
                '    Loop Until Account = "0"
                '    rs_Journal.MoveNext()
                'Loop
                'rs_Journal.CloseRemoteSet()

                ' Process CT Ledger Only for PL Account 
                ' Simplifed Version CT and Account Update for Post Flag only
                'rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select J.Account, J.CT, sum(J.Debit) as Debit, sum(J.Credit) as Credit, sum(J.Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(J.Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from Journal_Account J, Source S, Account A, [Type] T , CT C Where C.CT=J.CT and T.Type=A.Type and T.PL_Flag=1 and A.Account=J.Account and S.Status='U' and J.Source=S.Source and S.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and S.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group By J.Account, J.CT", adOpenForwardonly)
                'Do While Not rs_Journal.Eof
                '    Account = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Account"))
                '    nDebit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit"))
                '    nCredit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit"))
                '    nDebit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit_RC"))
                '    nCredit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit_RC"))
                '    CT = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("CT"))
                '    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from CTL where Account ='" & Account & "' and Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and [month] =" & Mth & " and [year] =" & Yr & " and CT ='" & CT & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                '    If rs.Eof Then
                '        rs.AddNew()
                '        rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                '        rs.fields("Account") = Account
                '        rs.fields("month") = Mth
                '        rs.fields("year") = Yr
                '        rs.fields("CT") = CT
                '    Else
                '        rs.Edit()
                '    End If
                '    rs.fields("Balance") = toval(rs.fields("Balance")) + nDebit - nCredit
                '    rs.fields("Balance_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Balance_RC")) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
                '    If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg

                '    If BudgetFeaturesF Then
                '        'CTBudget
                '        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from CTBudget where Account='" & Account & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Year]=" & Yr & " and CT='" & CT & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                '        If rs.Eof Then
                '            rs.AddNew()
                '            rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                '            rs.fields("Account") = Account
                '            rs.fields("year") = Yr
                '            rs.fields("CT") = CT
                '        Else
                '            rs.Edit()
                '        End If
                '        rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth)) + nDebit - nCredit
                '        rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth)) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
                '        If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
                '    End If

                '    rs_Journal.MoveNext()
                'Loop
                'rs_Journal.CloseRemoteSet()
            End If

            If Op_Flag Then
                If CBool(toval(GetAppSetting("GenerateExchangeRateRevaluationDailyF"))) OrElse Day(PDate.AddDays(1)) = 1 Then
                    If Not Update_Exchange_Rate(br_branch, Month(PDate), Year(PDate), PDate.AddDays(1)) Then
                        StrMsg = "Failed in Exchange Rate (Revaluation) Process !" & StrMsg
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                End If

                If toval(rs_Branch.fields("ShariaF")) Then
                    If CBool(toval(GetAppSetting("GenerateGLCashF"))) AndAlso Day(PDate.AddDays(1)) = 1 Then
                        If Not Update_GL_Cash(br_branch, Month(PDate), Year(PDate)) Then
                            StrMsg = "Failed to Update GL Cash Process !" & StrMsg
                            GoTo errmsg
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            End If

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 S.Source from Source S Where S.Status='U' and S.SL_Cat='X' and S.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and S.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            AnyTransactionF = AnyTransactionF OrElse (Not rs.Eof)

            If AnyTransactionF Then
                ' Update Sub Budget Balance
                If BudgetFeaturesF Then
                    rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select B.Account, B.TOC, sum(J.Debit) as Debit, sum(J.Credit) as Credit, sum(J.Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(J.Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from admlink A, nVoucher V, nJournal J, Budget_TOC B Where B.Branch = A.Branch And B.Account = J.Account And B.Year = Year(A.Date) And B.TOC = A.Code and J.Voucher=A.Voucher and V.Status='U' and V.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and V.Voucher=A.Voucher and A.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group By B.Account, B.TOC")
                    Do While Not rs_Journal.Eof
                        Account = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Account"))
                        nDebit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit"))
                        nCredit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit"))
                        nDebit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit_RC"))
                        nCredit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit_RC"))
                        If Not Update_Budget_TOC_Balance(Account, br_branch, Mth, Yr, tostr(rs_Journal.fields("TOC")), nDebit, nCredit, nDebit_RC, nCredit_RC) Then GoTo errmsg
                        rs_Journal.MoveNext()
                    Loop
                    rs_Journal.CloseRemoteSet()

                    rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select B.Account, B.TOC, sum(J.Debit) as Debit, sum(J.Credit) as Credit, sum(J.Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(J.Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from acclink A, mVoucher V, mJournal J, Budget_TOC B Where B.Branch = A.Branch And B.Account = J.Account And B.Year = Year(A.Date) And B.TOC = A.Code and J.Voucher=A.Voucher and V.Status='U' and V.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and V.Voucher=A.Voucher and A.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group By B.Account, B.TOC")
                    Do While Not rs_Journal.Eof
                        Account = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Account"))
                        nDebit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit"))
                        nCredit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit"))
                        nDebit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit_RC"))
                        nCredit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit_RC"))
                        If Not Update_Budget_TOC_Balance(Account, br_branch, Mth, Yr, tostr(rs_Journal.fields("TOC")), nDebit, nCredit, nDebit_RC, nCredit_RC) Then GoTo errmsg
                        rs_Journal.MoveNext()
                    Loop
                    rs_Journal.CloseRemoteSet()
                End If

                ' Process CT Ledger Only for PL Account 
                ' Simplifed Version CT and Account Update for Post Flag only
                If BudgetFeaturesF Then
                    rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select J.Account, J.CT, sum(J.Debit) as Debit, sum(J.Credit) as Credit, sum(J.Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(J.Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from Journal_Account J, Source S, Account A, [Type] T , CT C Where C.CT=J.CT and T.Type=A.Type and T.PL_Flag=1 and A.Account=J.Account and S.Status='U' and J.Source=S.Source and S.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and S.Date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group By J.Account, J.CT", adOpenForwardonly)
                    Do While Not rs_Journal.Eof
                        Account = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Account"))
                        nDebit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit"))
                        nCredit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit"))
                        nDebit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("debit_RC"))
                        nCredit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("credit_RC"))
                        CT = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("CT"))

                        'CTBudget
                        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from CTBudget where Account='" & Account & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Year]=" & Yr & " and CT='" & CT & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                        If rs.Eof Then
                            rs.AddNew()
                            rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                            rs.fields("Account") = Account
                            rs.fields("year") = Yr
                            rs.fields("CT") = CT
                        Else
                            rs.Edit()
                        End If
                        rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_" & Mth)) + nDebit - nCredit
                        rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth) = toval(rs.fields("Actual_RC_" & Mth)) + nDebit_RC - nCredit_RC
                        If Not rs.Update() Then GoTo errmsg

                        rs_Journal.MoveNext()
                    Loop
                    rs_Journal.CloseRemoteSet()
                End If

                ' Set Voucher Status to Posted Whether Posting_Flag=1 or false
                If Not db.Execute("Update Source set Status='P' where Status='U' and date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
                If Not db.Execute("Update Voucher set status='P' from nVoucher Voucher where voucher.date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='U' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
                If Not db.Execute("Update Voucher set status='P' from pVoucher Voucher where voucher.date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='U' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
                If Not db.Execute("Update Voucher set status='P' from mVoucher Voucher where voucher.date between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='U' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
        End If

        If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        Posting_Process = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("POSTING_FA" & br_branch)
        Posting_Process = False
    End Function
    Private Function Delete_Memorial_Voucher(ByVal Voucher As String) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rsSrc As RemoteSet
        Dim Source As String

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rsSrc = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where Voucher='" & Voucher & "'")
        If Not rsSrc.Eof Then
            Voucher = rsSrc.fields("Voucher")
            Source = rsSrc.fields("Source")

            Dim Account As String
            Dim VCurrency As String
            Dim nDebit_OC As Double
            Dim nCredit_OC As Double
            Dim nDebit As Double
            Dim nCredit As Double
            Dim nDebit_RC As Double
            Dim nCredit_RC As Double
            Dim CT As String
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mJournal Where Voucher='" & Voucher & "'")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Account = rs.fields("Account")
                VCurrency = rs.fields("Currency")
                nDebit_OC = rs.fields("Debit_OC")
                nCredit_OC = rs.fields("Credit_OC")
                nDebit = rs.fields("Debit")
                nCredit = rs.fields("Credit")
                nDebit_RC = rs.fields("Debit_RC")
                nCredit_RC = rs.fields("Credit_RC")
                CT = rs.fields("CT")

                If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, Account, VCurrency, CT, -nDebit, -nCredit, -nDebit_OC, -nCredit_OC, -nDebit_RC, -nCredit_RC, StrMsg) Then GoTo errmsg
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop

            If Not rsSrc.Delete Then GoTo errmsg
        End If
        Delete_Memorial_Voucher = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Delete_Memorial_Voucher = False
    End Function
    Private Function Create_Recurring_Voucher(ByVal Voucher As String) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rsSrc As RemoteSet
        Dim rsDst As RemoteSet
        Dim VType As String
        Dim AutoNum As String
        Dim sAutoNum As String
        Dim Source As String

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rsSrc = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where Voucher='" & Voucher & "'")
        VType = rsSrc.fields("VType")
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, sAutoNum from VType Where VType='" & VType & "'")
        AutoNum = rs.fields("AutoNum")
        sAutoNum = rs.fields("sAutoNum")

        rsDst = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
        rsDst.AddNew()
        If Not rsDst.PutRecord(rsSrc.GetRecord()) Then GoTo errmsg
        rsDst.fields("Date") = DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, 1, rsSrc.fields("Date"))
        rsDst.fields("RecurNo") = rsDst.fields("RecurNo") - 1
        Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, rsDst.fields("Date"), rsDst.fields("Branch"))
        If Voucher = "" Then
            StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        rsDst.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
        rsDst.fields("DocNo") = rsSrc.fields("Voucher")
        rsDst.fields("CDate") = Today.Date
        rsDst.fields("CTime") = Time()
        rsDst.fields("UserID") = db_user
        rsDst.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
        rsDst.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
        rsDst.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
        Source = Get_Source_Doc(rsDst.fields("Branch"), "GJ", rsDst.fields("RefNo"), rsDst.fields("RefType"), "", "", "M", rsDst.fields("Date"), sAutoNum)
        If Source = "" Then
            StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        rsDst.fields("Source") = Source
        rsDst.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
        rsDst.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
        rsDst.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
        rsDst.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
        rsDst.fields("Status") = "U"
        If Not rsDst.Update Then GoTo errmsg

        Dim i As Integer
        Dim Account As String
        Dim VCurrency As String
        Dim nDebit_OC As Double
        Dim nCredit_OC As Double
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double
        Dim CT As String
        i = 0
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mJournal Where Voucher='" & rsSrc.fields("Voucher") & "'")
        rsDst = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mJournal Where 1<>1")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            Account = rs.fields("Account")
            VCurrency = rs.fields("Currency")
            nDebit_OC = rs.fields("Debit_OC")
            nCredit_OC = rs.fields("Credit_OC")
            nDebit = rs.fields("Debit")
            nCredit = rs.fields("Credit")
            nDebit_RC = rs.fields("Debit_RC")
            nCredit_RC = rs.fields("Credit_RC")
            CT = rs.fields("CT")

            rsDst.AddNew()
            rsDst.fields("SortNo") = i + 1
            rsDst.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
            rsDst.fields("Account") = Account
            rsDst.fields("Description") = rs.fields("Description")
            rsDst.fields("Currency") = VCurrency

            'If Not CalculationRoundingF Then
            '    ' nothing to do
            'Else
            '    nDebit_OC = RoundCCY(nDebit_OC, UCase(VCurrency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nCredit_OC = RoundCCY(nCredit_OC, UCase(VCurrency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nDebit = RoundCCY(nDebit, br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nCredit = RoundCCY(nCredit, br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nDebit_RC = RoundCCY(nDebit_RC, br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nCredit_RC = RoundCCY(nCredit_RC, br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            'End If

            rsDst.fields("Debit_OC") = nDebit_OC
            rsDst.fields("Credit_OC") = nCredit_OC
            rsDst.fields("Debit") = nDebit
            rsDst.fields("Credit") = nCredit
            rsDst.fields("Debit_RC") = nDebit_RC
            rsDst.fields("Credit_RC") = nCredit_RC
            rsDst.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
            rsDst.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
            rsDst.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
            rsDst.fields("CT") = CT

            If Not rsDst.UpdateRow Then GoTo errmsg

            'Updating Journal_Account
            If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, Account, VCurrency, CT, nDebit, nCredit, nDebit_OC, nCredit_OC, nDebit_RC, nCredit_RC, StrMsg) Then GoTo errmsg
            i = i + 1
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop
        'Finally
        If Not rsDst.Eof Then
            If Not rsDst.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        End If

        Create_Recurring_Voucher = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Create_Recurring_Voucher = False
    End Function

    Private Function Create_Reversal_Voucher(ByVal Voucher As String) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rsSrc As RemoteSet
        Dim rsDst As RemoteSet
        Dim VType As String
        Dim AutoNum As String
        Dim sAutoNum As String
        Dim Source As String

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rsSrc = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where Voucher='" & Voucher & "'")
        VType = rsSrc.fields("VType")
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, sAutoNum from VType Where VType='" & VType & "'")
        AutoNum = rs.fields("AutoNum")
        sAutoNum = rs.fields("sAutoNum")

        rsDst = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
        rsDst.AddNew()
        If Not rsDst.PutRecord(rsSrc.GetRecord()) Then GoTo errmsg
        rsDst.fields("Date") = DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, 1, rsSrc.fields("Date"))
        rsDst.fields("AutoReverseF") = False
        Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, rsDst.fields("Date"), rsDst.fields("Branch"))
        If Voucher = "" Then
            StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        rsDst.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
        rsDst.fields("DocNo") = rsSrc.fields("Voucher")
        rsDst.fields("CDate") = Today.Date
        rsDst.fields("CTime") = Time()
        rsDst.fields("UserID") = db_user
        rsDst.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
        rsDst.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
        rsDst.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
        Source = Get_Source_Doc(rsDst.fields("Branch"), "GJ", rsDst.fields("RefNo"), rsDst.fields("RefType"), "", "", "M", rsDst.fields("Date"), sAutoNum)
        If Source = "" Then
            StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        rsDst.fields("Source") = Source
        rsDst.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
        rsDst.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
        rsDst.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
        rsDst.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
        rsDst.fields("Status") = "U"
        If Not rsDst.Update Then GoTo errmsg

        Dim i As Integer
        Dim Account As String
        Dim VCurrency As String
        Dim nDebit_OC As Double
        Dim nCredit_OC As Double
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double
        Dim CT As String
        i = 0
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mJournal Where Voucher='" & rsSrc.fields("Voucher") & "'")
        rsDst = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mJournal Where 1<>1")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            Account = rs.fields("Account")
            VCurrency = rs.fields("Currency")
            nDebit_OC = rs.fields("Credit_OC")
            nCredit_OC = rs.fields("Debit_OC")
            nDebit = rs.fields("Credit")
            nCredit = rs.fields("Debit")
            nDebit_RC = rs.fields("Credit_RC")
            nCredit_RC = rs.fields("Debit_RC")
            CT = rs.fields("CT")

            rsDst.AddNew()
            rsDst.fields("SortNo") = i + 1
            rsDst.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
            rsDst.fields("Account") = Account
            rsDst.fields("Description") = rs.fields("Description")
            rsDst.fields("Currency") = VCurrency

            'If Not CalculationRoundingF Then
            '    ' nothing to do
            'Else
            '    nDebit_OC = RoundCCY(nDebit_OC, UCase(VCurrency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nCredit_OC = RoundCCY(nCredit_OC, UCase(VCurrency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nDebit = RoundCCY(nDebit, br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nCredit = RoundCCY(nCredit, br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nDebit_RC = RoundCCY(nDebit_RC, br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            '    nCredit_RC = RoundCCY(nCredit_RC, br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
            'End If

            rsDst.fields("Debit_OC") = nDebit_OC
            rsDst.fields("Credit_OC") = nCredit_OC
            rsDst.fields("Debit") = nDebit
            rsDst.fields("Credit") = nCredit
            rsDst.fields("Debit_RC") = nDebit_RC
            rsDst.fields("Credit_RC") = nCredit_RC
            rsDst.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
            rsDst.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
            rsDst.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
            rsDst.fields("CT") = CT

            If Not rsDst.UpdateRow Then GoTo errmsg

            'Updating Journal_Account
            If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, Account, VCurrency, CT, nDebit, nCredit, nDebit_OC, nCredit_OC, nDebit_RC, nCredit_RC, StrMsg) Then GoTo errmsg
            i = i + 1
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop
        'Finally
        If Not rsDst.Eof Then
            If Not rsDst.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        End If

        Create_Reversal_Voucher = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Create_Reversal_Voucher = False
    End Function
    Private Function Closing_Process_1(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Voucher As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        ' Check for Accounting Link
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Type, Remarks from MParam M1 Where M1.ExecCheckF=1 and Not Exists (Select 1 from AccLog Where AccLog.Type=M1.Type and AccLog.Date='" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            StrMsg = StrMsg & "Process " & rs.fields("Remarks") & "(" & rs.fields("Type") & ") Is Not Generated Yet !" & vbCrLf
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        ' Check for Monthly Process
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select ProcName from SysProcess S1 Where S1.ExecCheckF=1 and Not Exists (Select 1 from SysLogProcess S2 Where S2.ProcName=S1.ProcName and S2.PDate='" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            StrMsg = StrMsg & "Process " & rs.fields("ProcName") & " Is Not Executed Yet !" & vbCrLf
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        If StrMsg <> "" Then
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        If Not Posting_Process(br_branch, br_End_Date) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed in Posting Process !" & StrMsg
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        db.Committrans()

        ' Closing Process
        db.Begintrans()
        If Not db.GetLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        If Not db.Execute("Update branch set month=" & br_month & ", year=" & br_year & " where branch='" & br_branch & "' and ((year=" & br_year & " and month=" & br_month - 1 & ") or (year=" & br_year - 1 & " and month=12))") Then GoTo errmsg
        If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Update Branch Closing Period "
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        ' Update Exchange Rate Moved to Posting Process
        'If Not Update_Exchange_Rate(br_branch, br_month, br_year, br_End_Date.AddDays(1)) Then
        '    StrMsg = "Failed in Exchange Rate (Revaluation) Process !" & StrMsg
        '    GoTo errmsg
        'End If

        ' update closing balance
        If Not update_closing_balance(br_branch, br_month, br_year, br_End_Date) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed in Closing_Balance Process !" & StrMsg
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        ' Run Process
        If Not run_process(br_branch, br_month, br_year, br_End_Date) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed in Run (Closing Entries) Process !" & StrMsg
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        If Not ReConstruct_Fiscal(br_branch, br_month, br_year) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Re-Construct Fiscal !" & StrMsg
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        Closing_Process_1 = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch)
        Closing_Process_1 = False
        Exit Function
    End Function

    Private Function Closing_Process_2(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date, Optional ByVal GetLockedObjectF As Boolean = True, Optional ByVal OP_FLAG_Only As Boolean = False) As Boolean
        On Error GoTo errmsg

        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Branch As String
        Dim Mth As Integer
        Dim Yr As Integer
        Dim PDate As Date

        If GetLockedObjectF Then
            db.Committrans()

            ' Op Branch First
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, Op_Flag from Branch Where Parent='" & br_branch & "' and Month+Year*12<" & br_month + br_year * 12 & " order by Branch, OP_FLAG Desc")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Branch = rs.fields("Branch")

                Dim rsSys As RemoteSet
                Do While True
                    If rs.fields("Op_Flag") Then
                        rsSys = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 PID from sysproclib Where (Status='P' and ErrMsg='Asynchronous Process (Working...)') and ProcName='CLOSING_PROCESS_1' and Param1='" & Branch & "'")
                    Else
                        rsSys = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 PID from sysproclib Where (Status='P' and ErrMsg='Asynchronous Process (Working...)') and ProcName='CLOSING_PROCESS_2' and Param1='" & Branch & "'")
                    End If
                    If rsSys.Eof Then Exit Do
                    System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(10000)
                Loop

                Dim rsBranch As RemoteSet
                rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Month, Year from Branch with (readcommitted) Where Branch='" & Branch & "'")
                Mth = rsBranch.fields("Month")
                Yr = rsBranch.fields("Year")
                Do While Mth + Yr * 12 < br_month + br_year * 12
                    If Mth = 12 Then
                        Yr = Yr + 1
                        Mth = 1
                    Else
                        Mth = Mth + 1
                    End If
                    PDate = Last_date_of_period(DateSerial(Yr, Mth, 1), 1)

                    If rs.fields("Op_Flag") Then
                        Dim Result As Object = Nothing
                        Result = Me.Direct_Call_Ext_Process("ProcessFA.NetV3.ProcLib", "CLOSING_PROCESS_1", Branch, Mth, Yr, PDate)
                        If TypeName(Result).ToUpper = "BOOLEAN" Then
                            If Not Result Then
                                StrMsg = "Failed in Closing Branch '" & Branch & "' !" & Me.ErrDesc
                                GoTo errmsg
                            End If
                        End If
                    Else    ' Consolidation
                        db.Begintrans()
                        If Not Closing_Process_2(Branch, Mth, Yr, PDate, GetLockedObjectF, True) Then
                            StrMsg = "Failed in Closing Branch '" & Branch & "' !" & StrMsg
                            GoTo errmsg
                        End If
                        db.Committrans()
                    End If
                Loop
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop

            If Not OP_FLAG_Only Then
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, Op_Flag from Branch Where Parent='" & br_branch & "' and Month+Year*12<" & br_month + br_year * 12 & " order by Branch, OP_FLAG Desc")
                Do While Not rs.Eof
                    Branch = rs.fields("Branch")

                    Dim rsSys As RemoteSet
                    Do While True
                        If rs.fields("Op_Flag") Then
                            rsSys = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 PID from sysproclib Where (Status='P' and ErrMsg='Asynchronous Process (Working...)') and ProcName='CLOSING_PROCESS_1' and Param1='" & Branch & "'")
                        Else
                            rsSys = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 PID from sysproclib Where (Status='P' and ErrMsg='Asynchronous Process (Working...)') and ProcName='CLOSING_PROCESS_2' and Param1='" & Branch & "'")
                        End If
                        If rsSys.Eof Then Exit Do
                        System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(10000)
                    Loop

                    Dim rsBranch As RemoteSet
                    rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Month, Year from Branch with (readcommitted) Where Branch='" & Branch & "'")
                    Mth = rsBranch.fields("Month")
                    Yr = rsBranch.fields("Year")
                    Do While Mth + Yr * 12 < br_month + br_year * 12
                        If Mth = 12 Then
                            Yr = Yr + 1
                            Mth = 1
                        Else
                            Mth = Mth + 1
                        End If
                        PDate = Last_date_of_period(DateSerial(Yr, Mth, 1), 1)

                        db.Begintrans()
                        If rs.fields("Op_Flag") Then
                            If Not Closing_Process_1(Branch, Mth, Yr, PDate) Then
                                StrMsg = "Failed in Closing Branch '" & Branch & "' !" & StrMsg
                                GoTo errmsg
                            End If
                        Else
                            If Not Closing_Process_2(Branch, Mth, Yr, PDate, GetLockedObjectF) Then
                                StrMsg = "Failed in Closing Branch '" & Branch & "' !" & StrMsg
                                GoTo errmsg
                            End If
                        End If
                        db.Committrans()
                    Loop
                    rs.MoveNext()
                Loop
            End If

            db.Begintrans()
        End If

        If Not OP_FLAG_Only Then
            If Not db.GetLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then
                StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If

            If Not Combine_Process(br_branch, br_month, br_year, br_End_Date) Then
                StrMsg = "Failed in Combine Process !" & StrMsg
                GoTo errmsg
            End If

            If Not Generate_Elimination(br_branch, br_month, br_year, br_End_Date) Then
                StrMsg = "Failed in Generate Elimination Process !" & StrMsg
                GoTo errmsg
            End If

            'Posting any journal transaction
            If Not Posting_Process(br_branch, br_End_Date) Then
                StrMsg = "Failed in Posting Process !" & StrMsg
                GoTo errmsg
            End If

            If Not db.Execute("Update branch set cmonth=" & br_month & ", cyear=" & br_year & " where parent='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

            If Not db.Execute("Update branch set month=" & br_month & ", year=" & br_year & " where branch='" & br_branch & "' and ((year=" & br_year & " and month=" & br_month - 1 & ") or (year=" & br_year - 1 & " and month=12))") Then GoTo errmsg
            If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then
                StrMsg = "Failed To Update Branch Closing Period !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If

            ' update closing balance
            If Not update_closing_balance(br_branch, br_month, br_year, br_End_Date) Then
                StrMsg = "Failed in Closing Balance Process !" & StrMsg
                GoTo errmsg
            End If

            ' Run Process
            If Not run_process(br_branch, br_month, br_year, br_End_Date) Then
                StrMsg = "Failed in Run (Closing Entries) Process !" & StrMsg
                GoTo errmsg
            End If

            If Not ReConstruct_Fiscal(br_branch, br_month, br_year) Then
                StrMsg = "Failed To Re-Construct Fiscal !" & StrMsg
                GoTo errmsg
            End If

            If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then
                StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
        End If


        Closing_Process_2 = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch)
        Closing_Process_2 = False
        Exit Function
    End Function
    Private Function Combine_Process(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Dim rs_GL As RemoteSet
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim i As Integer
        rs = db.OpenQueryDef("GL_Consolidation", adOpenForwardonly, br_branch, br_month, br_year)
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL Where Account='" & rs.fields("Account") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Month=" & br_month & " and Year=" & br_year)
            If rs_GL.Eof Then
                rs_GL.AddNew()
                rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                rs_GL.fields("Account") = rs.fields("Account")
                rs_GL.fields("Starting") = toval(rs.fields("Starting"))
                rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Starting_RC"))
                rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs.fields("Ending"))
                rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Ending_RC"))
                rs_GL.fields("Closing") = toval(rs.fields("Closing"))
                rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Closing_RC"))

                rs_GL.fields("Closing_12") = toval(rs.fields("Closing_12"))
                rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC_12") = toval(rs.fields("Closing_RC_12"))

            Else
                rs_GL.Edit()
                rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit")) - toval(rs.fields("Credit"))
                rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC")) - toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC"))
            End If
            rs_GL.fields("Debit") = toval(rs.fields("Debit"))
            rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC"))
            rs_GL.fields("Credit") = toval(rs.fields("Credit"))
            rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC"))
            rs_GL.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs.fields("Revaluation"))
            rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Revaluation_RC"))
            For i = 1 To 31
                rs_GL.fields("Balance_" & i) = toval(rs.fields("Balance_" & i))
                rs_GL.fields("Balance_RC_" & i) = toval(rs.fields("Balance_RC_" & i))
            Next i
            If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        ' Based Currency
        'If Not db.Execute("Delete From GL Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        'If Not db.Execute("Update_Consolidation_GL '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg

        rs = db.OpenQueryDef("GL_FX_Consolidation", adOpenForwardonly, br_branch, br_month, br_year)
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL_FX Where Account='" & rs.fields("Account") & "' and Currency='" & rs.fields("Currency") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Month=" & br_month & " and Year=" & br_year)
            If rs_GL.Eof Then
                rs_GL.AddNew()
                rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                rs_GL.fields("Account") = rs.fields("Account")
                rs_GL.fields("Currency") = rs.fields("Currency")
                rs_GL.fields("Starting") = toval(rs.fields("Starting"))
                rs_GL.fields("Starting_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Starting_BC"))
                rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Starting_RC"))
                rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs.fields("Ending"))
                rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Ending_BC"))
                rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Ending_RC"))
                rs_GL.fields("Closing") = toval(rs.fields("Closing"))
                rs_GL.fields("Closing_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Closing_BC"))
                rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Closing_RC"))

                rs_GL.fields("Closing_12") = toval(rs.fields("Closing_12"))
                rs_GL.fields("Closing_BC_12") = toval(rs.fields("Closing_BC_12"))
                rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC_12") = toval(rs.fields("Closing_RC_12"))
            Else
                rs_GL.Edit()
                rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit")) - toval(rs.fields("Credit"))
                rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit_BC")) - toval(rs.fields("Credit_BC")) + toval(rs.fields("Revaluation"))
                rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC")) - toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Revaluation_RC"))
            End If
            rs_GL.fields("Debit") = toval(rs.fields("Debit"))
            rs_GL.fields("Debit_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Debit_BC"))
            rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC"))
            rs_GL.fields("Credit") = toval(rs.fields("Credit"))
            rs_GL.fields("Credit_BC") = toval(rs.fields("Credit_BC"))
            rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC"))
            rs_GL.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs.fields("Revaluation"))
            rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Revaluation_RC"))
            For i = 1 To 31
                rs_GL.fields("Balance_" & i) = toval(rs.fields("Balance_" & i))
            Next i
            If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        ' All currency
        'If Not db.Execute("Delete From GL_FX Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        'If Not db.Execute("Update_Consolidation_GL_FX '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg

        'rs = db.OpenQueryDef("CTL_Consolidation", adOpenForwardonly, br_branch, br_month, br_year)
        'Do While Not rs.Eof
        '    rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from CTL  Where Account='" & rs.fields("Account") & "' and CT='" & rs.fields("CT") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Month=" & br_month & " and Year=" & br_year)
        '    If rs_GL.Eof Then
        '        rs_GL.AddNew()
        '        rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
        '        rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
        '        rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
        '        rs_GL.fields("Account") = rs.fields("Account")
        '        rs_GL.fields("CT") = rs.fields("CT")
        '        rs_GL.fields("Balance") = toval(rs.fields("Balance"))
        '        rs_GL.fields("Balance_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Balance_RC"))
        '    Else
        '        rs_GL.Edit()
        '        rs_GL.fields("Balance") = toval(rs.fields("Balance"))
        '        rs_GL.fields("Balance_RC") = toval(rs.fields("Balance_RC"))
        '    End If
        '    If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        '    rs.MoveNext()
        'Loop

        ' Profit/Cost Center Ledger
        'If Not db.Execute("Delete From CTL Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        'If Not db.Execute("Update_Consolidation_CTL '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg

        If BudgetFeaturesF Then If Not Budget_Consolidation(br_branch, br_month, br_year, br_End_Date) Then GoTo errmsg

        'Update Combine Date
        If Not db.Execute("Update Branch set Combine_Date='" & Format(br_End_Date, "mm/dd/yyyy") & "' where Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        Combine_Process = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Combine_Process = False
    End Function
    Private Function Posting_to_Journal_Account(ByVal Source As String, ByVal Account As String, ByVal Ccy As String, ByVal CT As String, ByVal nDebit As Double, ByVal nCredit As Double, ByVal nDebit_OC As Double, ByVal nCredit_OC As Double, ByVal nDebit_RC As Double, ByVal nCredit_RC As Double, ByRef ErrMsg As String) As Boolean
        Dim rs_J_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim i As Integer

        On Error GoTo ErrMessage

        'Updating Journal_Account
        rs_J_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * From Journal_Account where Source='" & Source & "' and Account='" & Account & "' and [Currency]='" & Ccy & "' and CT='" & CT & "'")
        If rs_J_Account.Eof Then
            rs_J_Account.AddNew()
            rs_J_Account.fields("Source") = Source
            rs_J_Account.fields("Account") = Account
            rs_J_Account.fields("Currency") = Ccy
            rs_J_Account.fields("CT") = CT
        Else
            rs_J_Account.Edit()
        End If
        rs_J_Account.fields("Debit_OC") = toval(rs_J_Account.fields("Debit_OC")) + nDebit_OC
        rs_J_Account.fields("Credit_OC") = toval(rs_J_Account.fields("Credit_OC")) + nCredit_OC
        rs_J_Account.fields("Debit") = toval(rs_J_Account.fields("Debit")) + nDebit
        rs_J_Account.fields("Credit") = toval(rs_J_Account.fields("Credit")) + nCredit
        rs_J_Account.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs_J_Account.fields("Debit_RC")) + nDebit_RC
        rs_J_Account.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs_J_Account.fields("Credit_RC")) + nCredit_RC
        If Not rs_J_Account.Update() Then GoTo ErrMessage

        rs_J_Account.CloseRemoteSet()
        Posting_to_Journal_Account = True

        Exit Function
ErrMessage:
        ErrMsg = StrError(ErrMsg)
        Posting_to_Journal_Account = False
    End Function


    Private Function Create_Journal(ByVal Nvalue As Double, ByVal br_Currency As String, ByVal Account As String, ByRef i As Integer, ByRef NDebit As Double, ByRef NCredit As Double, ByRef Num_Debit As Double, ByRef Num_Credit As Double, ByRef BufferData As Object) As Boolean
        Dim j As Integer
        Dim sAccount As String
        Dim Description As String

        Description = ""
        sAccount = Account
        If Nvalue > 0 Then
            NDebit = Nvalue
            NCredit = 0
        Else
            NDebit = 0
            NCredit = -Nvalue
        End If
        j = Find_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, sAccount)
        If j < i Then
            ' Combine for the same account
            i = i - 1
        Else
            BufferData(0, j) = sAccount
            BufferData(1, j) = Description
            BufferData(2, j) = tostr(br_Currency)
            BufferData(3, j) = 0
            BufferData(4, j) = 0
            BufferData(5, j) = 0
            BufferData(6, j) = 0
            BufferData(7, j) = ""
            BufferData(8, j) = 0
            BufferData(9, j) = 0
        End If
        BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) + NDebit
        BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) + NCredit
        If BufferData(5, j) > BufferData(6, j) Then
            BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) - BufferData(6, j)
            NDebit = NDebit - BufferData(6, j)
            NCredit = NCredit - BufferData(6, j)
            BufferData(6, j) = 0
        Else
            BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - BufferData(5, j)
            NDebit = NDebit - BufferData(5, j)
            NCredit = NCredit - BufferData(5, j)
            BufferData(5, j) = 0
        End If

        ' Return Value
        Num_Debit = Num_Debit + NDebit
        Num_Credit = Num_Credit + NCredit
        i = i + 1

        Create_Journal = True
        Exit Function

ErrMsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Create_Journal = False
    End Function
    Private Function Create_Journal_RC(ByVal Nvalue As Double, ByVal br_Currency As String, ByVal Account As String, ByRef i As Integer, ByRef NDebit As Double, ByRef NCredit As Double, ByRef Num_Debit As Double, ByRef Num_Credit As Double, ByRef BufferData As Object) As Boolean
        Dim j As Integer
        Dim sAccount As String
        Dim Description As String

        Description = ""
        sAccount = Account
        If Nvalue > 0 Then
            NDebit = Nvalue
            NCredit = 0
        Else
            NDebit = 0
            NCredit = -Nvalue
        End If
        j = Find_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, sAccount)
        If j < i Then
            ' Combine for the same account
            i = i - 1
        Else
            BufferData(0, j) = sAccount
            BufferData(1, j) = Description
            BufferData(2, j) = tostr(br_Currency)
            BufferData(3, j) = 0
            BufferData(4, j) = 0
            BufferData(5, j) = 0
            BufferData(6, j) = 0
            BufferData(7, j) = ""
            BufferData(8, j) = 0
            BufferData(9, j) = 0
        End If
        BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) + NDebit
        BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) + NCredit
        If BufferData(8, j) > BufferData(9, j) Then
            BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) - BufferData(9, j)
            NDebit = NDebit - BufferData(9, j)
            NCredit = NCredit - BufferData(9, j)
            BufferData(9, j) = 0
        Else
            BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - BufferData(8, j)
            NDebit = NDebit - BufferData(8, j)
            NCredit = NCredit - BufferData(8, j)
            BufferData(8, j) = 0
        End If

        ' Return Value
        Num_Debit = Num_Debit + NDebit
        Num_Credit = Num_Credit + NCredit
        i = i + 1

        Create_Journal_RC = True
        Exit Function

ErrMsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Create_Journal_RC = False
    End Function
    Private Function Save_Journal(ByVal Voucher As String, ByVal Source As String, ByVal CT As String, ByVal BufferData As Object) As Boolean
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        Dim Account As String
        Dim Description As String
        Dim VCurrency As String
        Dim nDebit_OC As Double
        Dim nCredit_OC As Double
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double
        Dim sCT As String

        On Error GoTo ErrMsg

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from mJournal where 1<>1")
        Dim MaxRows As Integer = CType(BufferData, Object(,)).GetUpperBound(1) + 1
        For i = 0 To MaxRows - 1
            If Trim(BufferData(0, i)) <> "" And Trim(BufferData(2, i)) <> "" And (BufferData(5, i) <> 0 Or BufferData(6, i) <> 0 Or BufferData(8, i) <> 0 Or BufferData(9, i) <> 0) Then
                Account = tostr(BufferData(0, i))
                Description = tostr(BufferData(1, i))
                VCurrency = tostr(BufferData(2, i))
                nDebit_OC = toval(BufferData(3, i))
                nCredit_OC = toval(BufferData(4, i))
                nDebit = toval(BufferData(5, i))
                nCredit = toval(BufferData(6, i))
                sCT = tostr(BufferData(7, i))
                If sCT = "" Then sCT = CT
                nDebit_RC = toval(BufferData(8, i))
                nCredit_RC = toval(BufferData(9, i))

                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("SortNo") = i + 1
                rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                rs.fields("Account") = Account
                rs.fields("Description") = Description
                rs.fields("Currency") = VCurrency

                'If Not CalculationRoundingF Then
                '    ' nothing to do
                'Else
                '    nDebit_OC = RoundCCY(nDebit_OC, UCase(VCurrency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                '    nCredit_OC = RoundCCY(nCredit_OC, UCase(VCurrency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                '    nDebit = RoundCCY(nDebit, br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                '    nCredit = RoundCCY(nCredit, br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                '    nDebit_RC = RoundCCY(nDebit_RC, br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                '    nCredit_RC = RoundCCY(nCredit_RC, br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                'End If

                rs.fields("Debit_OC") = nDebit_OC
                rs.fields("Credit_OC") = nCredit_OC
                rs.fields("Debit") = nDebit
                rs.fields("Credit") = nCredit
                rs.fields("Debit_RC") = nDebit_RC
                rs.fields("Credit_RC") = nCredit_RC
                rs.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
                rs.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                rs.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                rs.fields("CT") = sCT

                If Not rs.UpdateRow Then GoTo ErrMsg

                'Updating Journal_Account
                If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, Account, VCurrency, sCT, nDebit, nCredit, nDebit_OC, nCredit_OC, nDebit_RC, nCredit_RC, StrMsg) Then GoTo ErrMsg
            End If
        Next i
        'Finally
        If Not rs.Eof Then
            If Not rs.Update Then GoTo ErrMsg
        End If
        rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        '=== End Creating Journal ===

        Save_Journal = True
        Exit Function
ErrMsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Save_Journal = False
    End Function

    Private Function Budget_Consolidation(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs_Budget As RemoteSet
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Actual As String
        Dim Actual_RC As String

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Budget.Account, sum(Budget.Actual_" & br_month & ") as CActual, sum(Budget.Actual_RC_" & br_month & ") as CActual_RC " & _
            "from Budget, Branch " & _
            "Where Budget.Branch=Branch.Branch and Branch.Parent='" & br_branch & "' " & _
            "and Budget.[Year]=" & br_year & " Group By Budget.Account")

        Actual = "Actual_" & br_month
        Actual_RC = "Actual_RC_" & br_month
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            rs_Budget = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * From Budget where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & tostr(rs.fields("Account")) & "'", adOpenKeyset)
            If rs_Budget.Eof Then
                rs_Budget.AddNew()
                rs_Budget.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                rs_Budget.fields("year") = br_year
                rs_Budget.fields("Account") = tostr(rs.fields("Account"))
            Else
                rs_Budget.Edit()
            End If
            rs_Budget.fields(Actual) = toval(rs_Budget.fields(Actual)) + toval(rs.fields("CActual"))
            rs_Budget.fields(Actual_RC) = toval(rs_Budget.fields(Actual_RC)) + toval(rs.fields("CActual_RC"))
            If Not rs_Budget.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop
        If TypeName(rs) <> "Nothing" Then rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        If TypeName(rs_Budget) <> "Nothing" Then rs_Budget.CloseRemoteSet()

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Budget.Account, Budget.TOC, sum(Budget.Actual_" & br_month & ") as CActual, sum(Budget.Actual_RC_" & br_month & ") as CActual_RC " & _
            "from Budget_TOC Budget, Branch " & _
            "Where Budget.Branch=Branch.Branch and Branch.Parent='" & br_branch & "' " & _
            "and Budget.[Year]=" & br_year & " Group By Budget.Account, Budget.TOC")

        Actual = "Actual_" & br_month
        Actual_RC = "Actual_RC_" & br_month
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            rs_Budget = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * From Budget_TOC where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & tostr(rs.fields("Account")) & "' and TOC='" & tostr(rs.fields("TOC")) & "'", adOpenKeyset)
            If rs_Budget.Eof Then
                rs_Budget.AddNew()
                rs_Budget.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                rs_Budget.fields("year") = br_year
                rs_Budget.fields("Account") = tostr(rs.fields("Account"))
                rs_Budget.fields("TOC") = tostr(rs.fields("TOC"))
            Else
                rs_Budget.Edit()
            End If
            rs_Budget.fields(Actual) = toval(rs_Budget.fields(Actual)) + toval(rs.fields("CActual"))
            rs_Budget.fields(Actual_RC) = toval(rs_Budget.fields(Actual_RC)) + toval(rs.fields("CActual_RC"))
            If Not rs_Budget.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop
        If TypeName(rs) <> "Nothing" Then rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        If TypeName(rs_Budget) <> "Nothing" Then rs_Budget.CloseRemoteSet()

        'rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select CTBudget.Account, CTBudget.CT, sum(CTBudget.Actual_" & br_month & ") as CActual, sum(CTBudget.Actual_RC_" & br_month & ") as CActual_RC " & _
        '    "from CTBudget, Branch " & _
        '    "Where CTBudget.Branch=Branch.Branch and Branch.Parent='" & br_branch & "' " & _
        '    "and CTBudget.[Year]=" & br_year & " Group By CTBudget.Account, CTBudget.CT")

        'Actual = "Actual_" & br_month
        'Actual_RC = "Actual_RC_" & br_month
        'Do While Not rs.Eof
        '    rs_Budget = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * From CTBudget Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & tostr(rs.fields("Account")) & "' and CT='" & tostr(rs.fields("CT")) & "'", adOpenKeyset)
        '    If rs_Budget.Eof Then
        '        rs_Budget.AddNew()
        '        rs_Budget.fields("Branch") = br_branch
        '        rs_Budget.fields("year") = br_year
        '        rs_Budget.fields("Account") = tostr(rs.fields("Account"))
        '        rs_Budget.fields("CT") = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
        '    Else
        '        rs_Budget.Edit()
        '    End If
        '    rs_Budget.fields(Actual) = toval(rs_Budget.fields(Actual)) + toval(rs.fields("CActual"))
