        '    rs_Budget.fields(Actual_RC) = toval(rs_Budget.fields(Actual_RC)) + toval(rs.fields("CActual_RC"))
        '    If Not rs_Budget.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
        '    rs.MoveNext()
        'Loop

        'rs.CloseRemoteSet()

        Budget_Consolidation = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Budget_Consolidation = False
    End Function


    Private Function Delete_Beginning_Balance(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer) As Boolean 'F&A
        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("Delete From GL Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete From GL_FX Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        ' SL_FX not Used 
        'If Not db.Execute("Delete From SL_FX Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete From CA_FX Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg

        Delete_Beginning_Balance = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Delete_Beginning_Balance = False
    End Function

    Private Function Undo_Closing_Process_1(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal PDate As Date, Optional ByVal CascadeF As Boolean = False) As Boolean

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.GetLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        'Delete GL Fiscal
        If Not db.Execute("Delete S from Source S, mVoucher V, GL_FiscalH G Where V.Voucher=G.Voucher and S.Source=V.Source and ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete V from mVoucher V, GL_FiscalH G Where V.Voucher=G.Voucher and ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        ' If Not db.Execute("Delete G from GL_FiscalH G Where ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("Update G Set Status='N', Voucher='', Starting=0, Ending=0 from GL_FiscalH G Where ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update G Set Starting=0, Ending=0 from GL_Fiscal G Where ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg


        'Delete GL Audit
        If Not db.Execute("Delete GL_AuditH Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Year>=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg

        'Delete Budget
        If Not db.Execute("Delete_Budget_TOC '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete_Budget '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete_CTBudget '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg

        'Delete Journal and Voucher for Revaluation or Elimination
        If Not db.Execute("Delete from mVoucher where SL_Cat='X' and Date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete from Source where SL_Cat='X' and Source.Date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("delete from CTL   where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("delete from GL    where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("delete from GL_FX where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("delete from GL_CASH where (([Month]>=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        ' SL_FX not Used 
        'If Not db.Execute("delete from SL_FX where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("delete from CA_FX where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("delete from CTL where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update GL set ending=starting, Debit=0, Credit=0, Revaluation=0, ending_RC=starting_RC, Debit_RC=0, Credit_RC=0, Revaluation_RC=0, Balance_1=0, Balance_2=0, Balance_3=0, Balance_4=0, Balance_5=0, Balance_6=0, Balance_7=0, Balance_8=0, Balance_9=0, Balance_10=0, Balance_11=0, Balance_12=0, Balance_13=0, Balance_14=0, Balance_15=0, Balance_16=0, Balance_17=0, Balance_18=0, Balance_19=0, Balance_20=0, Balance_21=0, Balance_22=0, Balance_23=0, Balance_24=0, Balance_25=0, Balance_26=0, Balance_27=0, Balance_28=0, Balance_29=0, Balance_30=0, Balance_31=0, Balance_RC_1=0, Balance_RC_2=0, Balance_RC_3=0, Balance_RC_4=0, Balance_RC_5=0, Balance_RC_6=0, Balance_RC_7=0, Balance_RC_8=0, Balance_RC_9=0, Balance_RC_10=0, Balance_RC_11=0, Balance_RC_12=0, Balance_RC_13=0, Balance_RC_14=0, Balance_RC_15=0, Balance_RC_16=0, Balance_RC_17=0, Balance_RC_18=0, Balance_RC_19=0, Balance_RC_20=0, Balance_RC_21=0, Balance_RC_22=0, Balance_RC_23=0, Balance_RC_24=0, Balance_RC_25=0, Balance_RC_26=0, Balance_RC_27=0, Balance_RC_28=0, Balance_RC_29=0, Balance_RC_30=0, Balance_RC_31=0 where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update GL_FX set ending=starting, Debit=0, Credit=0, ending_BC=starting_BC, Debit_BC=0, Credit_BC=0, Revaluation=0, ending_RC=starting_RC, Debit_RC=0, Credit_RC=0, Revaluation_RC=0, Balance_1=0, Balance_2=0, Balance_3=0, Balance_4=0, Balance_5=0, Balance_6=0, Balance_7=0, Balance_8=0, Balance_9=0, Balance_10=0, Balance_11=0, Balance_12=0, Balance_13=0, Balance_14=0, Balance_15=0, Balance_16=0, Balance_17=0, Balance_18=0, Balance_19=0, Balance_20=0, Balance_21=0, Balance_22=0, Balance_23=0, Balance_24=0, Balance_25=0, Balance_26=0, Balance_27=0, Balance_28=0, Balance_29=0, Balance_30=0, Balance_31=0 where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        ' SL_FX not Used 
        'If Not db.Execute("Update SL_FX set ending=starting, Debit=0, Credit=0, ending_BC=starting_BC, Debit_BC=0, Credit_BC=0, Revaluation=0, ending_RC=starting_RC, Debit_RC=0, Credit_RC=0, Revaluation_RC=0, Balance_1=0, Balance_2=0, Balance_3=0, Balance_4=0, Balance_5=0, Balance_6=0, Balance_7=0, Balance_8=0, Balance_9=0, Balance_10=0, Balance_11=0, Balance_12=0, Balance_13=0, Balance_14=0, Balance_15=0, Balance_16=0, Balance_17=0, Balance_18=0, Balance_19=0, Balance_20=0, Balance_21=0, Balance_22=0, Balance_23=0, Balance_24=0, Balance_25=0, Balance_26=0, Balance_27=0, Balance_28=0, Balance_29=0, Balance_30=0, Balance_31=0 where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update CA_FX set ending=starting, Debit=0, Credit=0, ending_BC=starting_BC, Debit_BC=0, Credit_BC=0, Revaluation=0, ending_RC=starting_RC, Debit_RC=0, Credit_RC=0, Revaluation_RC=0, Balance_1=0, Balance_2=0, Balance_3=0, Balance_4=0, Balance_5=0, Balance_6=0, Balance_7=0, Balance_8=0, Balance_9=0, Balance_10=0, Balance_11=0, Balance_12=0, Balance_13=0, Balance_14=0, Balance_15=0, Balance_16=0, Balance_17=0, Balance_18=0, Balance_19=0, Balance_20=0, Balance_21=0, Balance_22=0, Balance_23=0, Balance_24=0, Balance_25=0, Balance_26=0, Balance_27=0, Balance_28=0, Balance_29=0, Balance_30=0, Balance_31=0 where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("Update Source set status='U' where Source.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Source.status='P' and Source.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update voucher set status='U' from nVoucher Voucher where BackDatedF<>1 and voucher.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='P' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update voucher set status='U' from pVoucher Voucher where BackDatedF<>1 and voucher.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='P' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update voucher set status='U' from mVoucher Voucher where BackDatedF<>1 and voucher.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='P' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        ' Delete Recurring Voucher
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select V2.Voucher from mVoucher V1 Inner Join mVoucher V2 on (V2.DocNo=V1.Voucher and V2.Date=dateadd(month,1,V1.Date)) where V1.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and V1.RecurNo>0 and V1.branch='" & br_branch & "'")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            If Not Delete_Memorial_Voucher(rs.fields("Voucher")) Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        'Delete Auto Reversal Voucher
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select V2.Voucher from mVoucher V1 Inner Join mVoucher V2 on (V2.DocNo=V1.Voucher and V2.Date=dateadd(month,1,V1.Date)) where V1.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and V1.AutoReverseF=1 and V1.branch='" & br_branch & "'")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            If Not Delete_Memorial_Voucher(rs.fields("Voucher")) Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        Dim br_End_Date As Date
        Dim br_Last_Month As Integer
        Dim br_Last_Year As Integer
        br_End_Date = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, -1, DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1))
        br_Last_Month = Month(br_End_Date)
        br_Last_Year = Year(br_End_Date)
        If Not db.Execute("Update branch set End_Date='" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "', month=" & br_Last_Month & ", year=" & br_Last_Year & " where branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then
            StrMsg = "Failed In Undo-Closing Process "
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        If Not Posting_Process(br_branch, PDate) Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        Undo_Closing_Process_1 = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch)
        Undo_Closing_Process_1 = False
    End Function


    Private Function Undo_Closing_Process_2(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal PDate As Date, Optional ByVal CascadeF As Boolean = False) As Boolean
        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.GetLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        'Delete GL Fiscal
        If Not db.Execute("Delete S from Source S, mVoucher V, GL_FiscalH G Where V.Voucher=G.Voucher and S.Source=V.Source and ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete V from mVoucher V, GL_FiscalH G Where V.Voucher=G.Voucher and ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        ' If Not db.Execute("Delete G from GL_FiscalH G Where ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("Update G Set Status='N', Voucher='', Starting=0, Ending=0 from GL_FiscalH G Where ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update G Set Starting=0, Ending=0 from GL_Fiscal G Where ((G.Month>=" & br_month & " and G.Year=" & br_year & ") or G.Year>" & br_year & ") and G.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        'Delete GL Audit
        If Not db.Execute("Delete GL_AuditH Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Year>=" & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg

        'Delete Budget
        If Not db.Execute("Delete_Budget_TOC '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete_Budget '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete_CTBudget '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year) Then GoTo errmsg

        'Delete Journal and Voucher for Revaluation or Elimination
        If Not db.Execute("Delete from mVoucher where SL_Cat='X' and Date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Delete from Source where SL_Cat='X' and Source.Date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("delete from CTL   where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("delete from GL    where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("delete from GL_FX where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        ' SL_FX not Used 
        'If Not db.Execute("delete from SL_FX where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("delete from CA_FX where (([Month]>" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & ") or [Year]>" & br_year & ") and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("delete from CTL where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update GL set ending=starting, Debit=0, Credit=0, Revaluation=0, ending_RC=starting_RC, Debit_RC=0, Credit_RC=0, Revaluation_RC=0, Balance_1=0, Balance_2=0, Balance_3=0, Balance_4=0, Balance_5=0, Balance_6=0, Balance_7=0, Balance_8=0, Balance_9=0, Balance_10=0, Balance_11=0, Balance_12=0, Balance_13=0, Balance_14=0, Balance_15=0, Balance_16=0, Balance_17=0, Balance_18=0, Balance_19=0, Balance_20=0, Balance_21=0, Balance_22=0, Balance_23=0, Balance_24=0, Balance_25=0, Balance_26=0, Balance_27=0, Balance_28=0, Balance_29=0, Balance_30=0, Balance_31=0, Balance_RC_1=0, Balance_RC_2=0, Balance_RC_3=0, Balance_RC_4=0, Balance_RC_5=0, Balance_RC_6=0, Balance_RC_7=0, Balance_RC_8=0, Balance_RC_9=0, Balance_RC_10=0, Balance_RC_11=0, Balance_RC_12=0, Balance_RC_13=0, Balance_RC_14=0, Balance_RC_15=0, Balance_RC_16=0, Balance_RC_17=0, Balance_RC_18=0, Balance_RC_19=0, Balance_RC_20=0, Balance_RC_21=0, Balance_RC_22=0, Balance_RC_23=0, Balance_RC_24=0, Balance_RC_25=0, Balance_RC_26=0, Balance_RC_27=0, Balance_RC_28=0, Balance_RC_29=0, Balance_RC_30=0, Balance_RC_31=0 where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update GL_FX set ending=starting, Debit=0, Credit=0, ending_BC=starting_BC, Debit_BC=0, Credit_BC=0, Revaluation=0, ending_RC=starting_RC, Debit_RC=0, Credit_RC=0, Revaluation_RC=0, Balance_1=0, Balance_2=0, Balance_3=0, Balance_4=0, Balance_5=0, Balance_6=0, Balance_7=0, Balance_8=0, Balance_9=0, Balance_10=0, Balance_11=0, Balance_12=0, Balance_13=0, Balance_14=0, Balance_15=0, Balance_16=0, Balance_17=0, Balance_18=0, Balance_19=0, Balance_20=0, Balance_21=0, Balance_22=0, Balance_23=0, Balance_24=0, Balance_25=0, Balance_26=0, Balance_27=0, Balance_28=0, Balance_29=0, Balance_30=0, Balance_31=0 where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        ' SL_FX not Used 
        'If Not db.Execute("Update SL_FX set ending=starting, Debit=0, Credit=0, ending_BC=starting_BC, Debit_BC=0, Credit_BC=0, Revaluation=0, ending_RC=starting_RC, Debit_RC=0, Credit_RC=0, Revaluation_RC=0, Balance_1=0, Balance_2=0, Balance_3=0, Balance_4=0, Balance_5=0, Balance_6=0, Balance_7=0, Balance_8=0, Balance_9=0, Balance_10=0, Balance_11=0, Balance_12=0, Balance_13=0, Balance_14=0, Balance_15=0, Balance_16=0, Balance_17=0, Balance_18=0, Balance_19=0, Balance_20=0, Balance_21=0, Balance_22=0, Balance_23=0, Balance_24=0, Balance_25=0, Balance_26=0, Balance_27=0, Balance_28=0, Balance_29=0, Balance_30=0, Balance_31=0 where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update CA_FX set ending=starting, Debit=0, Credit=0, ending_BC=starting_BC, Debit_BC=0, Credit_BC=0, Revaluation=0, ending_RC=starting_RC, Debit_RC=0, Credit_RC=0, Revaluation_RC=0, Balance_1=0, Balance_2=0, Balance_3=0, Balance_4=0, Balance_5=0, Balance_6=0, Balance_7=0, Balance_8=0, Balance_9=0, Balance_10=0, Balance_11=0, Balance_12=0, Balance_13=0, Balance_14=0, Balance_15=0, Balance_16=0, Balance_17=0, Balance_18=0, Balance_19=0, Balance_20=0, Balance_21=0, Balance_22=0, Balance_23=0, Balance_24=0, Balance_25=0, Balance_26=0, Balance_27=0, Balance_28=0, Balance_29=0, Balance_30=0, Balance_31=0 where [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("Update Source set status='U' where Source.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Source.status='P' and Source.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update voucher set status='U' from nVoucher Voucher where BackDatedF<>1 and voucher.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='P' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update voucher set status='U' from pVoucher Voucher where BackDatedF<>1 and voucher.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='P' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update voucher set status='U' from mVoucher Voucher where BackDatedF<>1 and voucher.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and voucher.status='P' and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        ' Delete Recurring Voucher
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Voucher from mVoucher Voucher where BackDatedF<>1 and voucher.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.RecurNo>0 and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            If Not Delete_Memorial_Voucher(rs.fields("Voucher")) Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        'Delete Auto Reversal Voucher
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Voucher from mVoucher Voucher where BackDatedF<>1 and voucher.date>='" & Format(DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Voucher.AutoReverseF=1 and voucher.branch='" & br_branch & "'")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            If Not Delete_Memorial_Voucher(rs.fields("Voucher")) Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        Dim br_End_Date As Date
        Dim br_Last_Month As Integer
        Dim br_Last_Year As Integer
        br_End_Date = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, -1, DateSerial(br_year, br_month, 1))
        br_Last_Month = Month(br_End_Date)
        br_Last_Year = Year(br_End_Date)

        If Not db.Execute("Update branch set End_Date='" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "', month=" & br_Last_Month & ", year=" & br_Last_Year & " where branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then
            StrMsg = "Failed In Undo-Closing Process "
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        If Not Posting_Process(br_branch, PDate) Then GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("Update branch set cmonth=" & br_Last_Month & ", cyear=" & br_Last_Year & " where parent='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        If CascadeF Then
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, Start_Date, End_Date, Op_Flag from Branch Where Parent='" & br_branch & "'")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, PDate), todate(rs.fields("start_date"))) > 0 Or DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, PDate), todate(rs.fields("end_date"))) < 0 Then
                    StrMsg = "Branch Start Date and Ending Date Contraint !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If

                Dim BBalF As Boolean
                BBalF = False
                If Month(DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, -1, todate(rs.fields("start_date")))) = br_month And Year(DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, -1, todate(rs.fields("start_date")))) = br_year And rs.fields("Op_Flag") Then
                    BBalF = True
                End If

                If rs.fields("Op_Flag") Then
                    If Not Undo_Closing_Process_1(rs.fields("Branch"), br_month, br_year, PDate, CascadeF) Then
                        StrMsg = "Failed in Closing Branch : '" & rs.fields("Branch") & "'"
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If

                    If BBalF Then
                        If Not Delete_Beginning_Balance(rs.fields("Branch"), br_month, br_year) Then
                            StrMsg = "Failed Deleting Beginning Balance, Branch : '" & rs.fields("Branch") & "'"
                            GoTo errmsg
                        End If
                    End If
                Else
                    If Not Undo_Closing_Process_2(rs.fields("Branch"), br_month, br_year, PDate, CascadeF) Then
                        StrMsg = "Failed in Closing Branch : '" & rs.fields("Branch") & "'"
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                End If
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
        End If

        If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        Undo_Closing_Process_2 = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("CLOSING_FA" & br_branch)
        Undo_Closing_Process_2 = False
    End Function


    Private Function Depreciation_Process_All(ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rsBranch As RemoteSet
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, End_Date, Month, Year from Branch Where OP_Flag=1 and Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
        Do While Not rsBranch.Eof

            If (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, rsBranch.fields("End_Date")) = 0 And rsBranch.fields("Month") = Month(PDate) And rsBranch.fields("Year") = Year(PDate)) Or (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, rsBranch.fields("End_Date")) > 0) Then
                StrMsg = StrMsg & "Branch : '" & rsBranch.fields("Branch") & "' Closing Month & Ending Date Constraint !" & vbCrLf
                GoTo errmsg
            Else
                If Not Depreciation_Process(rsBranch.fields("Branch"), PDate) Then
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
            End If
            rsBranch.MoveNext()
        Loop
        Depreciation_Process_All = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Depreciation_Process_All = False
    End Function
    Private Function Depreciation_Process(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean 'F&A
        Dim rs_asset As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Voucher As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_SL As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_AssType As RemoteSet
        Dim DocNo As String
        Dim depValue As Double
        Dim depValueFiscal As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.GetLockedObject("DEPRECIATION" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        rs_Voucher = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from pVoucher Where 1<>1")
        rs_SL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from pSL Where 1<>1")
        rs_asset = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from Asset where WriteF=0 and Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Booked<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and (BookValue_CC<>0 or BookValueFiscal_CC<>0)")
        Do While Not rs_asset.Eof
            rs_AssType = db.OpenRemoteSet("select AssType.Method, AssType.Depreciation, AssType.Age, AssType.AgeFiscal, AssType.Residual, AssType.FiscalF, VType.VType, VType.AutoNum  from AssType, VType where AssType='" & tostr(rs_asset.fields("AssType")) & "' and VType.VType=AssType.VType", adOpenForwardonly)
            If Not rs_AssType.Eof And (toval(rs_asset.fields("BookValue")) > toval(rs_AssType.fields("Residual")) Or toval(rs_asset.fields("BookValueFiscal")) > toval(rs_AssType.fields("Residual"))) Then

                depValue = RoundCCY(toval(rs_asset.fields("QTY")) * Calc_Dep(toval(rs_asset.fields("Nominal_CC")), toval(rs_asset.fields("Accumulation_CC")), tostr(rs_AssType.fields("Method")), toval(rs_AssType.fields("Depreciation")), todate(rs_asset.fields("Date")), PDate, toval(rs_asset.fields("age")), toval(rs_AssType.fields("Residual")), toval(rs_AssType.fields("FiscalF"))), br_currency, 0)
                depValueFiscal = RoundCCY(toval(rs_asset.fields("QTY")) * Calc_Dep(toval(rs_asset.fields("Nominal_CC")), toval(rs_asset.fields("AccumulationFiscal_CC")), tostr(rs_AssType.fields("Method")), toval(rs_AssType.fields("Depreciation")), todate(rs_asset.fields("Date")), PDate, toval(rs_asset.fields("ageFiscal")), toval(rs_AssType.fields("Residual")), True), br_currency, 0)

                DocNo = ""
                If depValue <> 0 Or depValueFiscal <> 0 Then
                    If Trim(rs_AssType.fields("Autonum")) <> "" Then
                        DocNo = db.GetNextDocNo(tostr(rs_AssType.fields("AutoNum")), PDate, br_branch)
                    End If
                    If DocNo = "" Then
                        StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                End If

                If DocNo <> "" Then
                    rs_Voucher.AddNew()
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Voucher") = DocNo
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Date") = PDate
                    rs_Voucher.fields("vType") = tostr(rs_AssType.fields("vType"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Cat") = "D"  ' Depreciation Category
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Currency") = br_currency
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Rate") = 1
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Rate_RC") = exchange_rate_RC(br_currency, PDate, br_currency_1)
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Qty") = toval(rs_asset.fields("Qty"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal_CC") = depValue
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal") = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal_CC")) * toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Rate"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Diff_CC") = depValue - depValueFiscal
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Diff") = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Diff_CC")) * toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Rate"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Asset") = tostr(rs_asset.fields("Asset"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("AssetF") = 1
                    rs_Voucher.fields("SL_FLag") = 1
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Status") = "U"
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                    rs_Voucher.fields("CT") = tostr(rs_asset.fields("CT"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("RefDate") = todate(rs_asset.fields("Booked"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("RefNo") = tostr(rs_asset.fields("RefNo"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Posting_Flag") = 0
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Remarks") = tostr(rs_asset.fields("Description"))
                    rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Tag") = 0
                    rs_Voucher.fields("PDate") = PDate
                    rs_Voucher.fields("CDate") = Today.Date
                    rs_Voucher.fields("CTime") = Time()
                    rs_Voucher.fields("UserID") = db_user
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                    rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                    rs_Voucher.fields("ProAcc") = Get_ProAcc(rs_asset.fields("ID"), rs_Voucher.fields("Currency"))
                    If Not rs_Voucher.UpdateRow() Then GoTo errmsg

                    rs_SL.AddNew()
                    rs_SL.fields("Voucher") = DocNo
                    rs_SL.fields("Doc_No") = "DEPRECIATION"
                    rs_SL.fields("Doc_Date") = todate(rs_Voucher.fields("Date"))
                    rs_SL.fields("Remarks") = "Depreciation of asset"
                    rs_SL.fields("Nominal") = toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal"))
                    rs_SL.fields("Doc_CCY") = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Currency"))
                    rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC") = toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal"))
                    rs_SL.fields("Rate") = 1
                    rs_SL.fields("Nominator") = 1
                    rs_SL.fields("Denominator") = 1
                    rs_SL.fields("Hint") = -1
                    If Not rs_SL.UpdateRow() Then GoTo errmsg

                    rs_SL.AddNew()
                    rs_SL.fields("Voucher") = DocNo
                    rs_SL.fields("Doc_No") = "FISCAL DIFF."
                    rs_SL.fields("Doc_Date") = todate(rs_Voucher.fields("Date"))
                    rs_SL.fields("Remarks") = "Fiscal Depreciation Difference"
                    rs_SL.fields("Nominal") = -toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Diff"))
                    rs_SL.fields("Doc_CCY") = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Currency"))
                    rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC") = rs_SL.fields("Nominal")
                    rs_SL.fields("Rate") = 1
                    rs_SL.fields("Nominator") = 1
                    rs_SL.fields("Denominator") = 1
                    rs_SL.fields("Hint") = -2
                    If Not rs_SL.UpdateRow() Then GoTo errmsg


                    rs_asset.Edit()
                    rs_asset.fields("Booked") = todate(rs_Voucher.fields("Date"))
                    rs_asset.fields("RefNo") = tostr(rs_Voucher.fields("Voucher"))
                    rs_asset.fields("BookValue_CC") = toval(rs_asset.fields("BookValue_CC")) - toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal")) / toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Qty"))
                    rs_asset.fields("Accumulation_CC") = toval(rs_asset.fields("Accumulation_CC")) + toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal")) / toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Qty"))

                    rs_asset.fields("BookValue") = toval(rs_asset.fields("BookValue")) - toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal"))
                    rs_asset.fields("Accumulation") = toval(rs_asset.fields("Accumulation")) + toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal"))

                    ' Fiscal
                    rs_asset.fields("BookValueFiscal_CC") = toval(rs_asset.fields("BookValueFiscal_CC")) - (toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal")) - toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Diff"))) / toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Qty"))
                    rs_asset.fields("AccumulationFiscal_CC") = toval(rs_asset.fields("AccumulationFiscal_CC")) + (toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal")) - toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Diff"))) / toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Qty"))
                    rs_asset.fields("BookValueFiscal") = toval(rs_asset.fields("BookValueFiscal")) - (toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal")) - toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Diff")))
                    rs_asset.fields("AccumulationFiscal") = toval(rs_asset.fields("AccumulationFiscal")) + (toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal")) - toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Diff")))

                    If Not rs_asset.Update() Then GoTo errmsg

                End If
            End If
            rs_AssType.CloseRemoteSet()
            rs_asset.MoveNext()
        Loop

        ' Finally
        If Not rs_Voucher.Eof Then
            If Not rs_Voucher.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        End If
        If Not rs_SL.Eof Then
            If Not rs_SL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        End If
        rs_asset.CloseRemoteSet()
        rs_Voucher.CloseRemoteSet()
        If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("DEPRECIATION" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        Depreciation_Process = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("DEPRECIATION" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("DEPRECIATION" & br_branch)
        Depreciation_Process = False
    End Function

    Private Function Calc_Dep(ByVal Nominal As Double, ByVal Accumulation As Double, ByVal Method As String, ByVal Depreciation As Double, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date, ByVal Age As Integer, ByVal residual As Double, Optional ByVal FiscalF As Boolean = False) As Double
        Dim tMonth As Integer
        Dim BaseCalc As Double
        Dim TotalDep As Double

        BaseCalc = Nominal

        If Depreciation = 0 And Age <> 0 Then
            Depreciation = 100 / Age
        End If

        If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, SDate, EDate) >= 0 Then
            If Method = "SF" Or Method = "DF" Then ' Tax
                SDate = DateSerial(Year(SDate), 1, 1)
            Else
                If SDate.Day > 15 And Not FiscalF Then
                    ' skip one Month
                    SDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, 1, SDate)
                Else
                    SDate = SDate
                End If
            End If
        Else
            If Method = "SF" Or Method = "DF" Then ' Tax
                SDate = DateSerial(Year(SDate), 1, 1)
            Else
                SDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, SDate)
            End If
        End If

        Dim yDepreciation As Double    ' Yearly Depreciation
        If EDate > SDate Then
            ' Monthly Basis
            tMonth = DateDiff(DateInterval.Month, SDate, EDate) + 1

            If (tMonth / 12) >= Age And Age <> 0 Then
                TotalDep = BaseCalc
            Else
                If UCase(Method) = "DC" Or UCase(Method) = "DF" Then ' Double Declining
                    Depreciation = Depreciation * 2
                    TotalDep = 0
                    yDepreciation = (BaseCalc - TotalDep) * Depreciation / 100
                    If Year(SDate) = Year(EDate) Then
                        TotalDep = TotalDep + yDepreciation / 12 * tMonth
                    Else
                        TotalDep = yDepreciation / 12 * (12 - Month(SDate) + 1) ' First Year Depreciaton
                        TotalDep = TotalDep + (BaseCalc - TotalDep) - (BaseCalc - TotalDep) * (1 - (Depreciation / 100)) ^ (Year(EDate) - Year(SDate) - 1)
                        yDepreciation = (BaseCalc - TotalDep) * Depreciation / 100
                        TotalDep = TotalDep + yDepreciation / 12 * Month(EDate)
                    End If
                Else
                    ' Monthly Basis
                    TotalDep = (BaseCalc * Depreciation / 100 / 12) * tMonth
                End If
            End If
            TotalDep = TotalDep - Accumulation
        Else
            TotalDep = 0
        End If

        If TotalDep < 0 Then
            TotalDep = 0
        End If

        If TotalDep + Accumulation > Nominal - residual Then
            TotalDep = Nominal - residual - Accumulation
            If TotalDep < 0 Then TotalDep = 0
        End If

        Calc_Dep = TotalDep
    End Function
    Private Function Get_Source_Doc(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal sType As String, ByVal RefNo As String, ByVal RefType As String, ByVal ProAcc As String, ByVal ComAcc As String, ByVal SL_Cat As String, ByVal tdate As Date, ByVal AutoNum As String, Optional ByVal Source As String = "") As String
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim sfilter As String
        On Error GoTo ErrMsg
        If Source = "" Then
            sfilter = "Date='" & Format(tdate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Branch='" & br_branch & "' and "
            'If RefNo <> "" Then
            '    sfilter = sfilter & "RefNo='" & RefNo & "' and "
            'End If
            'If RefType <> "" Then
            '    sfilter = sfilter & "RefType='" & RefType & "' and "
            'End If
            'If ProAcc <> "" Then
            '    sfilter = sfilter & "ProAcc='" & ProAcc & "' and "
            'End If
            'If ComAcc <> "" Then
            '    sfilter = sfilter & "ComAcc='" & ComAcc & "' and "
            'End If
            If SL_Cat <> "" Then
                sfilter = sfilter & "SL_Cat='" & SL_Cat & "' and "
            End If
            sfilter = sfilter & "SType='" & sType & "'"

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from Source Where Status='U' and " & sfilter)
            If rs.Eof Then
                Source = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, tdate, br_branch)
                If Source = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "Can't Create Source Number"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
                'If Not db.Execute("Insert Into Source(Source,[Date],Branch,RefNo,RefType, ProAcc, ComAcc, SL_Cat,SType,Status) Values('" & Source & "','" & Format(tdate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & br_branch & "','" & RefNo & "','" & RefType & "','" & ProAcc & "','" & ComAcc & "','" & SL_Cat & "','" & sType & "','U')") Then GoTo ErrMsg
                If Not db.Execute("Insert Into Source(Source,[Date],Branch,RefNo,RefType, ProAcc, ComAcc, SL_Cat,SType,Status) Values('" & Source & "','" & Format(tdate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & br_branch & "','','','','','" & SL_Cat & "','" & sType & "','U')") Then GoTo ErrMsg
            Else
                Source = tostr(rs.fields("Source"))
            End If
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        End If
        Get_Source_Doc = Source

        Exit Function
ErrMsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Get_Source_Doc = ""
    End Function

    Private Function Update_GL_Tree(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_GL As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Branch As RemoteSet
        Dim Account As String

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rs_Branch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch from Branch Where Parent='" & br_branch & "' or Branch='" & br_branch & "'")
        Do While Not rs_Branch.Eof
            br_branch = rs_Branch.fields("Branch")
            If Not db.Execute("Delete from GL_FX Where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Exists (Select Account from Account Where Account.Account=GL_FX.Account and Account.Post_Flag=0)") Then GoTo errmsg

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select GL_FX.*, Account.Parent from GL_FX, Account Where GL_FX.Account=Account.Account and Account.Post_Flag=1 and GL_FX.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and GL_FX.Month=" & br_month & " and GL_FX.Year=" & br_year)
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Account = tostr(rs.fields("Parent"))
                Do
                    rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL_FX where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "' and Currency='" & rs.fields("Currency") & "'")
                    If rs_GL.Eof Then
                        rs_GL.AddNew()
                        rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                        rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                        rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                        rs_GL.fields("Currency") = tostr(rs.fields("Currency"))
                        rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                    Else
                        rs_GL.Edit()
                    End If
                    rs_GL.fields("Starting") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting")) + toval(rs.fields("Starting"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending")) + toval(rs.fields("Ending"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Debit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Credit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit")) + toval(rs.fields("Credit"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Starting_BC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting_BC")) + toval(rs.fields("Starting_BC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC")) + toval(rs.fields("Ending_BC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Debit_BC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit_BC")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit_BC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Credit_BC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit_BC")) + toval(rs.fields("Credit_BC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation")) + toval(rs.fields("Revaluation"))

                    rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Starting_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Ending_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Revaluation_RC"))

                    rs_GL.fields("Closing") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Closing_BC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing_BC")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing_BC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing_RC"))

                    rs_GL.fields("Closing_12") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing_12")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing_12"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Closing_BC_12") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing_BC_12")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing_BC_12"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC_12") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC_12")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing_RC_12"))
                    If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                    rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'")
                    Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                Loop Until Account = "0"
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop


            If Not db.Execute("Delete from GL Where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Exists (Select Account from Account Where Account.Account=GL.Account and Account.Post_Flag=0)") Then GoTo errmsg
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select GL.*, Account.Parent from GL, Account Where GL.Account=Account.Account and Account.Post_Flag=1 and GL.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and GL.Month=" & br_month & " and GL.Year=" & br_year)
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Account = tostr(rs.fields("Parent"))
                Do
                    rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "'")
                    If rs_GL.Eof Then
                        rs_GL.AddNew()
                        rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                        rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                        rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                        rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                    Else
                        rs_GL.Edit()
                    End If
                    rs_GL.fields("Starting") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting")) + toval(rs.fields("Starting"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending")) + toval(rs.fields("Ending"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Debit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Credit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit")) + toval(rs.fields("Credit"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Closing") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Closing_12") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing_12")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing_12"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation")) + toval(rs.fields("Revaluation"))

                    rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Starting_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Ending_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Debit_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Credit_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing_RC"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC_12") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Closing_RC_12")) + toval(rs.fields("Closing_RC_12"))
                    rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC")) + toval(rs.fields("Revaluation_RC"))
                    If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                    rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'")
                    Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                Loop Until Account = "0"
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            rs_Branch.MoveNext()
        Loop

        Update_GL_Tree = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_GL_Tree = False
    End Function

    Private Function Update_GL_Cash(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_GL As RemoteSet
        Dim Account As String
        Dim Debit As Double
        Dim Credit As Double

        On Error GoTo errmsg


        If Not db.Execute("Delete from GL_Cash Where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " ") Then GoTo errmsg
        rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL_Cash Where 1<>1")
        rs = db.OpenQueryDef("Get_GLCash", adOpenKeyset, br_branch, br_month, br_year)
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            Account = rs.fields("Account")
            Debit = rs.fields("Debit")
            Credit = rs.fields("Credit")
            rs_GL.AddNew()
            rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
            rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
            rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
            rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
            rs_GL.fields("Debit") = Debit
            rs_GL.fields("Credit") = Credit
            If Not rs_GL.UpdateRow Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        If Not rs_GL.Eof Then
            If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        End If

        Dim dataset As DataSet
        dataset = rs_GL.GetDataSet

        For i = 0 To dataset.Tables(0).Rows.Count - 1
            Account = dataset.Tables(0).Rows(i)("Account")
            Debit = dataset.Tables(0).Rows(i)("Debit")
            Credit = dataset.Tables(0).Rows(i)("Credit")

            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Parent from Account Where Account='" & Account & "'")
            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
            Do
                rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL_Cash where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "'")
                If rs_GL.Eof Then
                    rs_GL.AddNew()
                    rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                    rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                    rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                    rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                Else
                    rs_GL.Edit()
                End If
                rs_GL.fields("Debit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit")) + Debit
                rs_GL.fields("Credit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit")) + Credit
                If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'")
                Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
            Loop Until Account = "0"
        Next

        Update_GL_Cash = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_GL_Cash = False
    End Function
    Private Function Update_Exchange_Rate(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs_CL As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_FX As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_GL As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim BufferData As Object
        Dim nDiff As Double
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim Rate As Double
        Dim Nvalue As Double
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim Num_Debit As Double
        Dim Num_Credit As Double
        Dim Account As String
        Dim AccTo As String
        On Error GoTo errmsg

        ' Base Currency #1
        Dim RevalRate As New Collection_Class
        AccTo = ""
        rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select CLProcess.AccFrom, CLProcess.AccTO from ClProcess where CLProcess.effective<='" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and CLProcess.FX_FLag=1 Order by CLProcess.AccTO", adOpenForwardonly)
        Do While Not rs_CL.Eof
            AccTo = rs_CL.fields("AccTo")
            ' Initialize BufferData
            ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
            For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                BufferData(7, i) = ""      ' CT
                BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
            Next i

            i = 0
            Num_Debit = 0
            Num_Credit = 0

            Do While Not rs_CL.Eof AndAlso AccTo = rs_CL.fields("AccTo")
                'GL_FX
                Nvalue = 0
                rs_FX = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL_FX where Currency<>'" & br_currency & "' and branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & rs_CL.fields("AccFrom") & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                Do While Not rs_FX.Eof
                    Rate = toval(RevalRate(rs_FX.fields("Currency")))
                    If Rate = 0 Then
                        Rate = Revaluation_Rate(tostr(rs_FX.fields("currency")), br_End_Date, br_currency)
                        RevalRate.Add(Rate, rs_FX.fields("Currency"))
                    End If

                    nDiff = Math.Round(toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending") * Rate) - toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending_BC")), 4, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
                    If CalculationRoundingF Then
                        nDiff = RoundCCY(nDiff, UCase(br_currency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    End If

                    If nDiff <> 0 Then
                        ' Update GL_FX
                        Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                        Do
                            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'")
                            rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL_FX where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "' and Currency='" & rs_FX.fields("Currency") & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                            If rs_GL.Eof Then
                                rs_GL.AddNew()
                                rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                                rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                                rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                                rs_GL.fields("Currency") = tostr(rs_FX.fields("Currency"))
                                rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                            Else
                                rs_GL.Edit()
                            End If
                            rs_GL.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation")) + nDiff
                            rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC")) + nDiff
                            If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
                            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                        Loop Until Account = "0"

                        Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                        Do
                            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'")
                            rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL_FX where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "' and Currency='" & rs_FX.fields("Currency") & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                            If rs_GL.Eof Then
                                rs_GL.AddNew()
                                rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                                rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                                rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                                rs_GL.fields("Currency") = tostr(rs_FX.fields("Currency"))
                                rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                            Else
                                rs_GL.Edit()
                            End If
                            rs_GL.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation")) - nDiff
                            rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_BC")) - nDiff
                            If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
                            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                        Loop Until Account = "0"

                        Nvalue = Nvalue + nDiff
                    End If

                    If Not Update_Exchange_Rate_CA_FX(br_branch, br_month, br_year, rs_FX.fields("Account"), rs_FX.fields("Currency"), Rate) Then GoTo errmsg

                    rs_FX.MoveNext()
                Loop
                rs_FX.CloseRemoteSet()

                If Nvalue <> 0 Then
                    Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                    If Not Create_Journal(Nvalue, br_currency, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
                    Do
                        rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                        rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                        If rs_GL.Eof Then
                            rs_GL.AddNew()
                            rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                            rs_GL.fields("month") = br_month
                            rs_GL.fields("year") = br_year
                            rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                        Else
                            rs_GL.Edit()
                        End If
                        rs_GL.fields("Debit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit")) + nDebit
                        rs_GL.fields("Credit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit")) + nCredit
                        rs_GL.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation")) + Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending")) + Nvalue

                        If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
                        Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                    Loop Until Account = "0"

                    Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                    If Not Create_Journal(-Nvalue, br_currency, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
                    If BudgetFeaturesF Then If Not Update_Budget_Balance(Account, br_branch, br_month, br_year, nDebit, nCredit, 0, 0) Then GoTo errmsg
                    Do
                        rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                        rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                        If rs_GL.Eof Then
                            rs_GL.AddNew()
                            rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                            rs_GL.fields("month") = br_month
                            rs_GL.fields("year") = br_year
                            rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                        Else
                            rs_GL.Edit()
                        End If

                        rs_GL.fields("Debit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit")) + nDebit
                        rs_GL.fields("Credit") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit")) + nCredit
                        rs_GL.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation")) + (-Nvalue)
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending")) + (-Nvalue)
                        If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                        Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                    Loop Until Account = "0"
                End If
                '=== End Buffering Journal ===

                rs_CL.MoveNext()
            Loop

            If Num_Debit <> 0 Or Num_Credit <> 0 Then
                Dim rs As RemoteSet
                Dim AutoNum As String
                Dim sAutoNum As String
                Dim CT As String
                Dim Source As String
                Dim VType As String
                Dim Voucher As String

                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
                AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
                sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
                CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
                VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
                rs.AddNew()

                Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, br_End_Date.AddDays(-1), br_branch)
                If Voucher = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
                rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                rs.fields("VType") = VType
                rs.fields("Date") = br_End_Date.AddDays(-1)
                rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency
                rs.fields("Nominal_CC") = Num_Debit
                rs.fields("Nominal") = Num_Debit
                rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
                rs.fields("RefType") = "Revaluation"
                rs.fields("Rate") = 1
                rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 0
                rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
                rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
                rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
                rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
                rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
                rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Revaluation)"
                rs.fields("Status") = "U"
                rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                rs.fields("CT") = CT
                Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Revaluation", "", "", "X", br_End_Date.AddDays(-1), sAutoNum)
                If Source = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
                rs.fields("Source") = Source
                If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                'Keep the Source Status = 'U'
                'If Not db.Execute("Update Source Set Status='P' Where Status='U' and Source='" & Source & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

                '=== Creating Voucher ===
                If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
            End If

        Loop

        RevalRate.Clear()

        ' Base Currency #2
        If br_currency_1 <> "" AndAlso br_currency_1.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper Then
            rs_CL.MoveFirst()
            AccTo = ""
            Do While Not rs_CL.Eof
                AccTo = rs_CL.fields("AccTo")
                ' Initialize BufferData
                ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
                For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                    BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                    BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                    BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                    BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                    BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                    BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                    BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                    BufferData(7, i) = ""      ' CT
                    BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                    BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
                Next i

                i = 0
                Num_Debit = 0
                Num_Credit = 0

                Do While Not rs_CL.Eof AndAlso AccTo = rs_CL.fields("AccTo")
                    'GL_FX
                    Nvalue = 0
                    rs_FX = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL_FX where Currency<>'" & br_currency_1 & "' and branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & rs_CL.fields("AccFrom") & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                    Do While Not rs_FX.Eof
                        Rate = toval(RevalRate(rs_FX.fields("Currency")))
                        If Rate = 0 Then
                            Rate = Revaluation_Rate_RC(tostr(rs_FX.fields("currency")), br_End_Date, br_currency_1)
                            RevalRate.Add(Rate, rs_FX.fields("Currency"))
                        End If

                        nDiff = Math.Round(toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending") * Rate) - toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending_RC")), 4, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
                        If CalculationRoundingF Then
                            nDiff = RoundCCY(nDiff, UCase(br_currency_1), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                        End If

                        If nDiff <> 0 Then
                            ' Update GL_FX
                            Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                            Do
                                rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'")
                                rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL_FX where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "' and Currency='" & rs_FX.fields("Currency") & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                                If rs_GL.Eof Then
                                    rs_GL.AddNew()
                                    rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                                    rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                                    rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                                    rs_GL.fields("Currency") = tostr(rs_FX.fields("Currency"))
                                    rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                                Else
                                    rs_GL.Edit()
                                End If
                                rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC")) + nDiff
                                rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) + nDiff
                                If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
                                Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                            Loop Until Account = "0"

                            Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                            Do
                                rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'")
                                rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from GL_FX where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "' and Currency='" & rs_FX.fields("Currency") & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                                If rs_GL.Eof Then
                                    rs_GL.AddNew()
                                    rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                                    rs_GL.fields("Month") = br_month
                                    rs_GL.fields("Year") = br_year
                                    rs_GL.fields("Currency") = tostr(rs_FX.fields("Currency"))
                                    rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                                Else
                                    rs_GL.Edit()
                                End If
                                rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC")) - nDiff
                                rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) - nDiff
                                If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
                                Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                            Loop Until Account = "0"

                            Nvalue = Nvalue + nDiff
                        End If

                        If Not Update_Exchange_Rate_CA_FX_RC(br_branch, br_month, br_year, rs_FX.fields("Account"), rs_FX.fields("Currency"), Rate) Then GoTo errmsg

                        rs_FX.MoveNext()
                    Loop
                    rs_FX.CloseRemoteSet()

                    If Nvalue <> 0 Then
                        Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                        If Not Create_Journal_RC(Nvalue, br_currency, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
                        Do
                            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                            rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                            If rs_GL.Eof Then
                                rs_GL.AddNew()
                                rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                                rs_GL.fields("month") = br_month
                                rs_GL.fields("year") = br_year
                                rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                            Else
                                rs_GL.Edit()
                            End If
                            rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC")) + nDebit
                            rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC")) + nCredit
                            rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC")) + Nvalue
                            rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) + Nvalue
                            If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg
                            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                        Loop Until Account = "0"

                        Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                        If Not Create_Journal_RC(-Nvalue, br_currency, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
                        Do
                            rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                            rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL where branch='" & br_branch & "' and [Month]=" & br_month & " and [Year]=" & br_year & " and Account='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                            If rs_GL.Eof Then
                                rs_GL.AddNew()
                                rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                                rs_GL.fields("month") = br_month
                                rs_GL.fields("year") = br_year
                                rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                            Else
                                rs_GL.Edit()
                            End If

                            rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Debit_RC")) + nDebit
                            rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Credit_RC")) + nCredit
                            rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Revaluation_RC")) + (-Nvalue)
                            rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) + (-Nvalue)
                            If Not rs_GL.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                            Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                        Loop Until Account = "0"
                    End If
                    '=== End Buffering Journal ===

                    rs_CL.MoveNext()
                Loop

                If Num_Debit <> 0 Or Num_Credit <> 0 Then
                    Dim rs As RemoteSet
                    Dim AutoNum As String
                    Dim sAutoNum As String
                    Dim CT As String
                    Dim Source As String
                    Dim VType As String
                    Dim Voucher As String

                    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
                    AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
                    sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
                    CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
                    VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

                    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
                    rs.AddNew()

                    Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, br_End_Date.AddDays(-1), br_branch)
                    If Voucher = "" Then
                        StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                    rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                    rs.fields("VType") = VType
                    rs.fields("Date") = br_End_Date.AddDays(-1)
                    rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency_1
                    rs.fields("Nominal_CC") = Num_Debit
                    rs.fields("Nominal") = Num_Debit
                    rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
                    rs.fields("RefType") = "Revaluation"
                    rs.fields("Rate") = 0
                    rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 1
                    rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
                    rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
                    rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
                    rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
                    rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
                    rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Revaluation)"
                    rs.fields("Status") = "U"
                    rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                    rs.fields("CT") = CT
                    Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Revaluation", "", "", "X", br_End_Date.AddDays(-1), sAutoNum)
                    If Source = "" Then
                        StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                    rs.fields("Source") = Source
                    If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                    'Keep the Source Status = 'U'
                    'If Not db.Execute("Update Source Set Status='P' Where Status='U' and Source='" & Source & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

                    '=== Creating Voucher ===
                    If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
                End If
            Loop
        End If
        RevalRate.Clear()

        rs_CL.CloseRemoteSet()
        Update_Exchange_Rate = True
        Exit Function

errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Exchange_Rate = False
    End Function


    Private Function update_closing_balance(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim Mth As Integer
        Dim Yr As Integer
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        Mth = ((br_month + br_year * 12) Mod 12) + 1
        Yr = ((br_month + br_year * 12) \ 12)

        If Not db.Execute("Update_Closing_Balance_GL '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year & "," & Mth & "," & Yr) Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update_Closing_Balance_GL_FX '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year & "," & Mth & "," & Yr) Then GoTo errmsg
        ' SL_FX not Used 
        'If Not db.Execute("Update_Closing_Balance_SL_FX '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year & "," & Mth & "," & Yr) Then GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Update_Closing_Balance_CA_FX '" & br_branch & "'," & br_month & "," & br_year & "," & Mth & "," & Yr) Then GoTo errmsg

        update_closing_balance = True
        Exit Function

errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        update_closing_balance = False
    End Function

    Private Function run_process(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs_CL As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_GL As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Account As RemoteSet
        Dim Nvalue As Double
        Dim Nvalue_RC As Double
        Dim Account As String
        Dim Mth As Integer
        Dim Yr As Integer

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Mth = ((Month(br_End_Date) + Year(br_End_Date) * 12) Mod 12) + 1
        Yr = ((Month(br_End_Date) + Year(br_End_Date) * 12) \ 12)

        ' based currency

        ' Only PL Account
        rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet("select CLProcess.AccFrom, CLProcess.AccTO, GL.ending, GL.ending_RC from ClProcess, GL, Account AccFrom, Type where CLProcess.AccFrom=gl.account and AccFrom.Account=CLProcess.AccFrom and Type.Type=AccFrom.Type and Type.PL_Flag=1 and CLProcess.Closing_Flag=1 and GL.branch='" & br_branch & "' and GL.Month=" & Mth & " and GL.Year=" & Yr, adOpenForwardonly)

        Do While Not rs_CL.Eof
            Nvalue = toval(rs_CL.fields("Ending"))
            Nvalue_RC = toval(rs_CL.fields("Ending_RC"))
            If Nvalue <> 0 Or Nvalue_RC <> 0 Then
                Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                Do
                    rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account Where Account ='" & Account & "'", adOpenKeyset)
                    If rs_Account.Eof Then
                        StrMsg = "Account : " & Account & " Not Found !"
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                    rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL where Account ='" & Account & "' and Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and [month] =" & Mth & " and [year] =" & Yr, adOpenKeyset)
                    If rs_GL.Eof Then
                        rs_GL.AddNew()
                        rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                        rs_GL.fields("month") = Mth
                        rs_GL.fields("year") = Yr
                        rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                        rs_GL.fields("starting") = -Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending") = -Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("starting_RC") = -Nvalue_RC
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = -Nvalue_RC
                    Else
                        rs_GL.Edit()
                        rs_GL.fields("Starting") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting")) - Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending")) - Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC")) - Nvalue_RC
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) - Nvalue_RC
                    End If
                    If Not rs_GL.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
                    Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                Loop Until Account = "0"

                Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                Do
                    rs_Account = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Account, Parent from Account where Account ='" & Account & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
                    If rs_Account.Eof Then
                        StrMsg = "Account : " & Account & " Not Found !"
                        GoTo errmsg
                    End If
                    rs_GL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL where Account ='" & Account & "' and Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and [month] =" & Mth & " and [year] =" & Yr, adOpenKeyset)
                    If rs_GL.Eof Then
                        rs_GL.AddNew()
                        rs_GL.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                        rs_GL.fields("month") = Mth
                        rs_GL.fields("year") = Yr
                        rs_GL.fields("Account") = Account
                        rs_GL.fields("starting") = Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending") = Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("starting_RC") = Nvalue_RC
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = Nvalue_RC
                    Else
                        rs_GL.Edit()
                        rs_GL.fields("Starting") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting")) + Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("Ending") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending")) + Nvalue
                        rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Starting_RC")) + Nvalue_RC
