                        rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_GL.fields("Ending_RC")) + Nvalue_RC
                    End If

                    If Not rs_GL.Update() Then GoTo errmsg
                    Account = tostr(rs_Account.fields("Parent"))
                Loop Until Account = "0"
            End If
            rs_CL.MoveNext()
        Loop
        rs_CL.CloseRemoteSet()

        ' Others currencies

        'GL_FX

        If Not db.Execute("Update GL_FX set starting=0, Ending=0, Starting_BC=0, Ending_BC=0, Starting_RC=0, Ending_RC=0 From GL_FX Where Branch='" & br_branch & "' and Month=" & Mth & " and Year=" & Yr & " and Exists (Select Account From Account, Type Where Type.Type=Account.Type and Account.Account=GL_FX.Account and Type.PL_Flag=1)") Then GoTo errmsg

        run_process = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        run_process = False
    End Function

    Private Function Update_Installment(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("Update_Installment '" & br_branch & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        Update_Installment = True
        Exit Function

errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Installment = False
    End Function

    Private Function exchange_rate(ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal br_Currency As String) As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If CCode.ToUpper <> br_Currency.ToUpper And IsDate(PDate) Then
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Rate from Currency Where Currency='" & CCode & "' and Effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            If Not rs.Eof Then
                exchange_rate = rs.fields("Rate")
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select top 1 exchange.rate from exchange where exchange.currency='" & CCode & "' and exchange.effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Order by exchange.effective desc", adOpenForwardonly)
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    exchange_rate = rs.fields("Rate")
                Else
                    exchange_rate = 1
                End If
            End If
            If exchange_rate <= 0 Then exchange_rate = 1
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        Else
            exchange_rate = 1
        End If
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        exchange_rate = 1
    End Function
    Private Function exchange_rate_RC(ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal br_Currency As String) As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If CCode.ToUpper <> br_Currency.ToUpper And IsDate(PDate) Then
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Rate_RC from Currency Where Currency='" & CCode & "' and Effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            If Not rs.Eof Then
                exchange_rate_RC = rs.fields("Rate_RC")
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select top 1 exchange.Rate_RC from exchange where exchange.currency='" & CCode & "' and exchange.effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Order by exchange.effective desc", adOpenForwardonly)
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    exchange_rate_RC = rs.fields("Rate_RC")
                Else
                    exchange_rate_RC = 1
                End If
            End If
            If exchange_rate_RC <= 0 Then exchange_rate_RC = 1
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        Else
            exchange_rate_RC = 1
        End If
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        exchange_rate_RC = 1
    End Function
    Private Function exchange_rate_RC(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal br_Currency As String) As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

                If Not rs.Eof Then

errmsg:

    End Function
    Private Function Revaluation_Rate(ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal br_Currency As String) As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If CCode.ToUpper <> br_Currency.ToUpper And IsDate(PDate) Then
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select VRate, Rate from Currency Where Currency='" & CCode & "' and Effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            If Not rs.Eof Then
                Revaluation_Rate = IIf(rs.fields("VRate") <> 0, rs.fields("VRate"), rs.fields("Rate"))
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select Top 1 exchange.VRate, exchange.rate from exchange where exchange.currency='" & CCode & "' and exchange.effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Order by exchange.effective desc", adOpenForwardonly)
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    Revaluation_Rate = IIf(rs.fields("VRate") <> 0, rs.fields("VRate"), rs.fields("Rate"))
                Else
                    Revaluation_Rate = 1
                End If
            End If
            If Revaluation_Rate <= 0 Then Revaluation_Rate = 1
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        Else
            Revaluation_Rate = 1
        End If
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Revaluation_Rate = 1
    End Function
    Private Function Revaluation_Rate_RC(ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal br_Currency As String) As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If CCode.ToUpper <> br_Currency.ToUpper And IsDate(PDate) Then
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select VRate_RC, Rate_RC from Currency Where Currency='" & CCode & "' and Effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            If Not rs.Eof Then
                Revaluation_Rate_RC = IIf(rs.fields("VRate_RC") <> 0, rs.fields("VRate_RC"), rs.fields("Rate_RC"))
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select Top 1 exchange.VRate_RC, exchange.Rate_RC from exchange where exchange.currency='" & CCode & "' and exchange.effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Order by exchange.effective desc", adOpenForwardonly)
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    Revaluation_Rate_RC = IIf(rs.fields("VRate_RC") <> 0, rs.fields("VRate_RC"), rs.fields("Rate_RC"))
                Else
                    Revaluation_Rate_RC = 1
                End If
            End If
            If Revaluation_Rate_RC <= 0 Then Revaluation_Rate_RC = 1
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        Else
            Revaluation_Rate_RC = 1
        End If
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Revaluation_Rate_RC = 1
    End Function
    Private Function Generate_Balance_Before(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_CL As RemoteSet
        Dim BufferData As Object
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim Num_Debit As Double
        Dim Num_Credit As Double

        Dim Nvalue As Double
        Dim Account As String

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        ' Based Currency #1 
        Dim SQL As String
        SQL = "Select C.AccFrom, C.AccTO, GL.Starting as Starting"
        SQL = SQL & " from ClProcess C, GL, Type, Account"
        SQL = SQL & " where C.Closing_Flag = 1 And Account.Account=C.AccFrom and Type.Type=Account.Type and Type.BS_Flag=1"
        SQL = SQL & " and GL.Account = C.AccFrom And GL.Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and GL.Month=" & br_month & " and GL.Year=" & br_year

        rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL)

        If Not rs_CL.Eof Then
            ' Initialize BufferData
            ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
            For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                BufferData(7, i) = ""    'CT
                BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
            Next i
        End If
        i = 0
        Num_Debit = 0
        Num_Credit = 0
        Do While Not rs_CL.Eof
            Nvalue = rs_CL.fields("Starting")
            '=== Creating Journal ===
            If Nvalue <> 0 Then
                Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                If Not Create_Journal(-Nvalue, br_currency, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg

                Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                If Not Create_Journal(Nvalue, br_currency, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
            '=== End Creating Journal ===
            rs_CL.MoveNext()
        Loop
        rs_CL.CloseRemoteSet()

        If Num_Debit <> 0 Or Num_Credit <> 0 Then
            Dim AutoNum As String
            Dim sAutoNum As String
            Dim CT As String
            Dim Source As String
            Dim VType As String
            Dim Voucher As String

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
            AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
            sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
            CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
            VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

            Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, br_End_Date, br_branch)
            If Voucher = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
            rs.AddNew()
            rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
            rs.fields("VType") = VType
            rs.fields("Date") = br_End_Date
            rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency
            rs.fields("Nominal_CC") = Num_Debit
            rs.fields("Nominal") = Num_Debit
            rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
            rs.fields("RefType") = "Balance Before"
            rs.fields("Rate") = 1
            rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 0
            rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
            rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
            rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
            rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
            rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
            rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Balance Before)"
            rs.fields("Status") = "U"
            rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
            rs.fields("CT") = CT
            Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Balance Before", "", "", "X", br_End_Date, sAutoNum)
            If Source = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs.fields("Source") = Source
            If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

            '=== Creating Voucher ===
            If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
        End If

        ' Based Currency #2 
        If br_currency_1 <> "" AndAlso br_currency_1.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper Then
            SQL = "Select C.AccFrom, C.AccTO, GL.Starting_RC as Starting"
            SQL = SQL & " from ClProcess C, GL, Type, Account"
            SQL = SQL & " where C.Closing_Flag = 1 And Account.Account=C.AccFrom and Type.Type=Account.Type and Type.BS_Flag=1"
            SQL = SQL & " and GL.Account = C.AccFrom And GL.Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and GL.Month=" & br_month & " and GL.Year=" & br_year

            rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL)

            If Not rs_CL.Eof Then
                ' Initialize BufferData
                ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
                For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                    BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                    BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                    BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                    BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                    BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                    BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                    BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                    BufferData(7, i) = ""    'CT
                    BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                    BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
                Next i
            End If

            i = 0
            Num_Debit = 0
            Num_Credit = 0
            Do While Not rs_CL.Eof
                Nvalue = rs_CL.fields("Starting")

                '=== Creating Journal ===
                If Nvalue <> 0 Then
                    Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                    If Not Create_Journal_RC(-Nvalue, br_currency_1, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg

                    Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                    If Not Create_Journal_RC(Nvalue, br_currency_1, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
                End If
                '=== End Creating Journal ===
                rs_CL.MoveNext()
            Loop
            rs_CL.CloseRemoteSet()

            If Num_Debit <> 0 Or Num_Credit <> 0 Then
                Dim AutoNum As String
                Dim sAutoNum As String
                Dim CT As String
                Dim Source As String
                Dim VType As String
                Dim Voucher As String

                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
                AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
                sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
                CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
                VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

                Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, br_End_Date, br_branch)
                If Voucher = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                rs.fields("VType") = VType
                rs.fields("Date") = br_End_Date
                rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency_1
                rs.fields("Nominal_CC") = Num_Debit
                rs.fields("Nominal") = Num_Debit
                rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
                rs.fields("RefType") = "Balance Before"
                rs.fields("Rate") = 0
                rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 1
                rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
                rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
                rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
                rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
                rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
                rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Balance Before)"
                rs.fields("Status") = "U"
                rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                rs.fields("CT") = CT
                Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Balance Before", "", "", "X", br_End_Date, sAutoNum)
                If Source = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
                rs.fields("Source") = Source
                If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                '=== Creating Voucher ===
                If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
        End If

        Generate_Balance_Before = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Generate_Balance_Before = False
    End Function
    Private Function Generate_Elimination(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal br_month As Integer, ByVal br_year As Integer, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_CL As RemoteSet
        Dim BufferData As Object
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim Num_Debit As Double
        Dim Num_Credit As Double

        Dim nValue1 As Double
        Dim nValue2 As Double
        Dim Nvalue As Double
        Dim Account As String

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        ' Based Currency #1 
        Dim SQL As String
        SQL = "Select C.AccFrom, C.AccTO, GLFrom.Ending as EndingFrom, GLTo.Ending as EndingTo into #GL"
        SQL = SQL & " from ClProcess C, GL GLFrom, GL GLTo"
        SQL = SQL & " where GLFrom.Account = C.AccFrom And GLTo.Account = C.AccTo And C.Elimination_Flag = 1 And GLFrom.Branch = GLTo.Branch And GLFrom.Month = GLTo.Month And GLFrom.Year = GLTo.Year"
        SQL = SQL & " and GLFrom.Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and GLFrom.Month=" & br_month & " and GLFrom.Year=" & br_year
        db.Execute("Drop Table #GL")
        If Not db.Execute(SQL) Then GoTo errmsg

        rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from #GL", adOpenForwardonly)

        If Not rs_CL.Eof Then
            ' Initialize BufferData
            ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
            For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                BufferData(7, i) = ""    'CT
                BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
            Next i
        End If
        i = 0
        Num_Debit = 0
        Num_Credit = 0
        Dim Balance As New Collection
        Do While Not rs_CL.Eof

            On Error Resume Next
            Balance.Add(tostr(rs_CL.fields("EndingFrom")), tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
            Balance.Add(tostr(rs_CL.fields("EndingTo")), tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))
            On Error GoTo errmsg

            nValue1 = Balance(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
            nValue2 = Balance(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))

            If nValue1 <> 0 And nValue2 <> 0 Then
                If Abs(nValue1) < Abs(nValue2) Then
                    Nvalue = Abs(nValue1)
                    If nValue1 > 0 Then Nvalue = -Nvalue Else Nvalue = Nvalue
                Else
                    Nvalue = Abs(nValue2)
                    If nValue2 > 0 Then Nvalue = Nvalue Else Nvalue = -Nvalue
                End If
            Else
                Nvalue = 0
            End If

            '=== Creating Journal ===
            If Nvalue <> 0 Then
                nValue1 = Balance(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
                nValue2 = Balance(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))
                Balance.Remove(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
                Balance.Remove(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))
                nValue1 = nValue1 + Nvalue
                nValue2 = nValue2 - Nvalue

                Balance.Add(nValue1, tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
                Balance.Add(nValue2, tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))

                Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                If Not Create_Journal(Nvalue, br_currency, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg

                Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                If Not Create_Journal(-Nvalue, br_currency, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
            '=== End Creating Journal ===
            rs_CL.MoveNext()
        Loop
        rs_CL.CloseRemoteSet()
        If Not db.Execute("Drop Table #GL") Then GoTo errmsg

        If Num_Debit <> 0 Or Num_Credit <> 0 Then
            Dim AutoNum As String
            Dim sAutoNum As String
            Dim CT As String
            Dim Source As String
            Dim VType As String
            Dim Voucher As String

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
            AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
            sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
            CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
            VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

            Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, br_End_Date, br_branch)
            If Voucher = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
            rs.AddNew()
            rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
            rs.fields("VType") = VType
            rs.fields("Date") = br_End_Date
            rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency
            rs.fields("Nominal_CC") = Num_Debit
            rs.fields("Nominal") = Num_Debit
            rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
            rs.fields("RefType") = "Elimination"
            rs.fields("Rate") = 1
            rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 0
            rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
            rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
            rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
            rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
            rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
            rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Elimination)"
            rs.fields("Status") = "U"
            rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
            rs.fields("CT") = CT
            Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Elimination", "", "", "X", br_End_Date, sAutoNum)
            If Source = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs.fields("Source") = Source
            If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

            '=== Creating Voucher ===
            If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
        End If

        ' Based Currency #2 
        If br_currency_1 <> "" AndAlso br_currency_1.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper Then
            SQL = "Select C.AccFrom, C.AccTO, GLFrom.Ending_RC as EndingFrom, GLTo.Ending_RC as EndingTo into #GL"
            SQL = SQL & " from ClProcess C, GL GLFrom, GL GLTo"
            SQL = SQL & " where GLFrom.Account = C.AccFrom And GLTo.Account = C.AccTo And C.Elimination_Flag = 1 And GLFrom.Branch = GLTo.Branch And GLFrom.Month = GLTo.Month And GLFrom.Year = GLTo.Year"
            SQL = SQL & " and GLFrom.Branch ='" & br_branch & "' and GLFrom.Month=" & br_month & " and GLFrom.Year=" & br_year
            db.Execute("Drop Table #GL")
            If Not db.Execute(SQL) Then GoTo errmsg

            rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from #GL", adOpenForwardonly)

            If Not rs_CL.Eof Then
                ' Initialize BufferData
                ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
                For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                    BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                    BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                    BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                    BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                    BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                    BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                    BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                    BufferData(7, i) = ""    'CT
                    BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                    BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
                Next i
            End If

            i = 0
            Num_Debit = 0
            Num_Credit = 0
            Balance = New Collection
            Do While Not rs_CL.Eof

                On Error Resume Next
                Balance.Add(tostr(rs_CL.fields("EndingFrom")), tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
                Balance.Add(tostr(rs_CL.fields("EndingTo")), tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))
                On Error GoTo errmsg

                nValue1 = Balance(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
                nValue2 = Balance(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))

                If nValue1 <> 0 And nValue2 <> 0 Then
                    If Abs(nValue1) < Abs(nValue2) Then
                        Nvalue = Abs(nValue1)
                        If nValue1 > 0 Then Nvalue = -Nvalue Else Nvalue = Nvalue
                    Else
                        Nvalue = Abs(nValue2)
                        If nValue2 > 0 Then Nvalue = Nvalue Else Nvalue = -Nvalue
                    End If
                Else
                    Nvalue = 0
                End If

                '=== Creating Journal ===
                If Nvalue <> 0 Then
                    nValue1 = Balance(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
                    nValue2 = Balance(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))
                    Balance.Remove(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
                    Balance.Remove(tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))
                    nValue1 = nValue1 + Nvalue
                    nValue2 = nValue2 - Nvalue

                    Balance.Add(nValue1, tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")))
                    Balance.Add(nValue2, tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTo")))

                    Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccFrom"))
                    If Not Create_Journal_RC(Nvalue, br_currency_1, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg

                    Account = tostr(rs_CL.fields("AccTO"))
                    If Not Create_Journal_RC(-Nvalue, br_currency_1, Account, i, nDebit, nCredit, Num_Debit, Num_Credit, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
                End If
                '=== End Creating Journal ===
                rs_CL.MoveNext()
            Loop
            rs_CL.CloseRemoteSet()
            If Not db.Execute("Drop Table #GL") Then GoTo errmsg

            If Num_Debit <> 0 Or Num_Credit <> 0 Then
                Dim AutoNum As String
                Dim sAutoNum As String
                Dim CT As String
                Dim Source As String
                Dim VType As String
                Dim Voucher As String

                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
                AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
                sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
                CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
                VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

                Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, br_End_Date, br_branch)
                If Voucher = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                rs.fields("VType") = VType
                rs.fields("Date") = br_End_Date
                rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency_1
                rs.fields("Nominal_CC") = Num_Debit
                rs.fields("Nominal") = Num_Debit
                rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
                rs.fields("RefType") = "Elimination"
                rs.fields("Rate") = 0
                rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 1
                rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
                rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
                rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
                rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
                rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
                rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Elimination)"
                rs.fields("Status") = "U"
                rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
                rs.fields("CT") = CT
                Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Elimination", "", "", "X", br_End_Date, sAutoNum)
                If Source = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
                rs.fields("Source") = Source
                If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                '=== Creating Voucher ===
                If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
        End If

        'Update Elimination Date
        If Not db.Execute("Update Branch set Elimination_Date='" & Format(br_End_Date, "mm/dd/yyyy") & "' where Branch='" & br_branch & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        Generate_Elimination = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Generate_Elimination = False
    End Function
    Private Function Generate_Paid_Elimination(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs_CL As RemoteSet
        Dim BufferData As Object

        Dim nDebit_OC As Double
        Dim nCredit_OC As Double
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double

        Dim Account As String
        Dim Currency As String
        Dim CT As String
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim j As Integer

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Dim SQL As String
        Dim PDate As Date

        db.Execute("Drop Table #GL")
        SQL = ""
        SQL = SQL & " Select pVoucher.Date, CLProcess.AccFrom, CLProcess.AccTo, nVoucher.Currency, nVoucher.CT,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(pSL.Nominal_OC, pVoucher.SL_Cat, pVoucher.DebtorF, nVoucher.DebtorF)/Case When nVoucher.DebtorF=1 Then nVoucher.Nominal_CC-nVoucher.Diff_CC else -nVoucher.Nominal_CC+nVoucher.Diff_CC end) as Debit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(pSL.Nominal_OC, pVoucher.SL_Cat, pVoucher.DebtorF, nVoucher.DebtorF)/Case When nVoucher.DebtorF=1 Then nVoucher.Nominal_CC-nVoucher.Diff_CC else -nVoucher.Nominal_CC+nVoucher.Diff_CC end) as Credit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*nVoucher.Rate*dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(pSL.Nominal_OC, pVoucher.SL_Cat, pVoucher.DebtorF, nVoucher.DebtorF)/Case When nVoucher.DebtorF=1 Then nVoucher.Nominal_CC-nVoucher.Diff_CC else -nVoucher.Nominal_CC+nVoucher.Diff_CC end) as Debit,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*nVoucher.Rate*dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(pSL.Nominal_OC, pVoucher.SL_Cat, pVoucher.DebtorF, nVoucher.DebtorF)/Case When nVoucher.DebtorF=1 Then nVoucher.Nominal_CC-nVoucher.Diff_CC else -nVoucher.Nominal_CC+nVoucher.Diff_CC end) as Credit,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*nVoucher.Rate_RC*dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(pSL.Nominal_OC, pVoucher.SL_Cat, pVoucher.DebtorF, nVoucher.DebtorF)/Case When nVoucher.DebtorF=1 Then nVoucher.Nominal_CC-nVoucher.Diff_CC else -nVoucher.Nominal_CC+nVoucher.Diff_CC end) as Debit_RC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*nVoucher.Rate_RC*dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(pSL.Nominal_OC, pVoucher.SL_Cat, pVoucher.DebtorF, nVoucher.DebtorF)/Case When nVoucher.DebtorF=1 Then nVoucher.Nominal_CC-nVoucher.Diff_CC else -nVoucher.Nominal_CC+nVoucher.Diff_CC end) as Credit_RC"
        SQL = SQL & " into #GL"
        SQL = SQL & " from nVoucher, nSL, vPattern, Account, pSL, pVoucher, CLProcess"
        SQL = SQL & " Where nSL.Voucher = nVoucher.Voucher And vPattern.VType = nVoucher.VType And vPattern.Debit + vPattern.Credit = nSL.Hint"
        SQL = SQL & " and Account.Account=vPattern.Account"
        SQL = SQL & " and CLProcess.pElimination_Flag=1 and CLProcess.AccFrom=Account.Account"
        SQL = SQL & " and Round(nVoucher.Nominal_CC-nVoucher.Diff_CC,4)<>0"
        SQL = SQL & " and pSL.PaymentF=1 and pSL.Doc_No=nVoucher.Voucher and pSL.Voucher=pVoucher.Voucher and pVoucher.Posting_Flag=1"
        SQL = SQL & " and nVoucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and pVoucher.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
        SQL = SQL & " Group By pVoucher.Date, CLProcess.AccFrom, CLProcess.AccTo, nVoucher.Currency, nVoucher.CT"

        SQL = SQL & " Union All"

        SQL = SQL & " Select nVoucher.Date, CLProcess.AccFrom, CLProcess.AccTo, nVoucher.Currency, nVoucher.CT,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal else 0 end)) as Debit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal else 0 end)) as Credit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*nVoucher.Rate) as Debit,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*nVoucher.Rate) as Credit,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*nVoucher.Rate_RC) as Debit_RC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum((Case When vPattern.Debit<>0 and nSL.Nominal>0 then nSL.Nominal When vPattern.Credit<>0 and nSL.Nominal<0 then -nSL.Nominal else 0 end)*nVoucher.Rate_RC) as Credit_RC"
        SQL = SQL & " from nVoucher, nSL, vPattern, Account, CLProcess"
        SQL = SQL & " Where nSL.Voucher = nVoucher.Voucher And vPattern.VType = nVoucher.VType And vPattern.Debit + vPattern.Credit = nSL.Hint"
        SQL = SQL & " and Account.Account=vPattern.Account"
        SQL = SQL & " and CLProcess.pElimination_Flag=1 and CLProcess.AccFrom=Account.Account"
        SQL = SQL & " and Round(nVoucher.Nominal_CC-nVoucher.Diff_CC,4)=0"
        SQL = SQL & " and nVoucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and nVoucher.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
        SQL = SQL & " Group By nVoucher.Date, CLProcess.AccFrom, CLProcess.AccTo, nVoucher.Currency, nVoucher.CT"
        SQL = SQL & " Order By Date, AccFrom, AccTo, Currency, CT"

        If Not db.Execute(SQL) Then GoTo errmsg

        rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from #GL Order By Date, AccFrom, AccTo, Currency, CT", adOpenForwardonly)
        Do While Not rs_CL.Eof
            ' Initialize BufferData
            ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
            For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                BufferData(7, i) = ""      ' CT
                BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
            Next i

            i = 0
            PDate = rs_CL.fields("Date")
            Do While Not rs_CL.Eof AndAlso PDate = rs_CL.fields("Date")
                Account = rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")
                Currency = rs_CL.fields("Currency")
                CT = rs_CL.fields("CT")
                nDebit_OC = rs_CL.fields("Debit_OC")
                nCredit_OC = rs_CL.fields("Credit_OC")
                nDebit = rs_CL.fields("Debit")
                nCredit = rs_CL.fields("Credit")
                nDebit_RC = rs_CL.fields("Debit_RC")
                nCredit_RC = rs_CL.fields("Credit_RC")

                j = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, Account, 2, Currency, 7, CT)
                If j < i Then
                    ' Combine for the same account
                    i = i - 1
                Else
                    BufferData(0, j) = Account
                    BufferData(1, j) = ""
                    BufferData(2, j) = Currency
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                    BufferData(7, j) = CT
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                End If

                BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) + nDebit_OC
                BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) + nCredit_OC
                BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) + nDebit
                BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) + nCredit
                BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) + nDebit_RC
                BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) + nCredit_RC

                If BufferData(3, j) > BufferData(4, j) Then
                    BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) - BufferData(4, j)
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) - BufferData(3, j)
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(5, j) > BufferData(6, j) Then
                    BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) - BufferData(6, j)
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - BufferData(5, j)
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(8, j) > BufferData(9, j) Then
                    BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) - BufferData(9, j)
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - BufferData(8, j)
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                End If

                i = i + 1
                rs_CL.MoveNext()
            Loop

            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AccTo, sum(Debit) as Debit, sum(Credit) as Credit, sum(Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from #GL Where Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group by AccTo")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Account = rs.fields("AccTo")
                Currency = br_currency
                CT = ""
                nDebit_OC = rs.fields("Credit")
                nCredit_OC = rs.fields("Debit")
                nDebit = rs.fields("Credit")
                nCredit = rs.fields("Debit")
                nDebit = rs.fields("Credit")
                nCredit = rs.fields("Debit")
                nDebit_RC = rs.fields("Credit_RC")
                nCredit_RC = rs.fields("Debit_RC")

                j = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, Account, 2, Currency, 7, CT)
                If j < i Then
                    ' Combine for the same account
                    i = i - 1
                Else
                    BufferData(0, j) = Account
                    BufferData(1, j) = ""
                    BufferData(2, j) = Currency
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                    BufferData(7, j) = CT
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                End If
                BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) + nDebit_OC
                BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) + nCredit_OC
                BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) + nDebit
                BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) + nCredit
                BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) + nDebit_RC
                BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) + nCredit_RC

                If BufferData(3, j) > BufferData(4, j) Then
                    BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) - BufferData(4, j)
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) - BufferData(3, j)
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(5, j) > BufferData(6, j) Then
                    BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) - BufferData(6, j)
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - BufferData(5, j)
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(8, j) > BufferData(9, j) Then
                    BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) - BufferData(9, j)
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - BufferData(8, j)
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                End If

                i = i + 1
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()

            Dim AutoNum As String
            Dim sAutoNum As String
            Dim Source As String
            Dim VType As String
            Dim Voucher As String

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
            AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
            sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
            CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
            VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

            Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, PDate, br_branch)
            If Voucher = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
            rs.AddNew()
            rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
            rs.fields("VType") = VType
            rs.fields("Date") = PDate
            rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency
            rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
            rs.fields("RefType") = "Paid Elimination"
            rs.fields("Rate") = 1
            rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 0
            rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
            rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
            rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
            rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
            rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
            rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Paid Elimination)"
            rs.fields("Status") = "U"
            rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
            rs.fields("CT") = CT
            Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Paid Elimination", "", "", "X", PDate, sAutoNum)
            If Source = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs.fields("Source") = Source
            If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

            '=== Creating Voucher ===
            If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg

        Loop
        If Not db.Execute("Drop Table #GL") Then GoTo errmsg

        Generate_Paid_Elimination = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Generate_Paid_Elimination = False
    End Function
    Private Function Generate_Fund_Elimination(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs_CL As RemoteSet
        Dim BufferData As Object

        Dim nDebit_OC As Double
        Dim nCredit_OC As Double
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double
        Dim Account As String
        Dim Currency As String
        Dim CT As String
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim j As Integer

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Dim SQL As String
        Dim PDate As Date

        db.Execute("Drop Table #GL")
        SQL = ""
        SQL = SQL & " Select Voucher.Date, "
        SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Account_N as AccFrom,"
        SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Account_C as AccTo,"
        SQL = SQL & " SysTab.Currency,"
        SQL = SQL & " '' as CT,"
        SQL = SQL & " Case When Voucher.Nominal_N<0 Then -Voucher.Nominal_N else 0 end as Debit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " Case When Voucher.Nominal_N>0 Then Voucher.Nominal_N else 0 end as Credit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " Case When Voucher.Nominal_N<0 Then -Voucher.Nominal_N else 0 end as Debit,"
        SQL = SQL & " Case When Voucher.Nominal_N>0 Then Voucher.Nominal_N else 0 end as Credit,"
        SQL = SQL & " Case When Voucher.Nominal_N_RC<0 Then -Voucher.Nominal_N_RC else 0 end as Debit_RC,"
        SQL = SQL & " Case When Voucher.Nominal_N_RC>0 Then Voucher.Nominal_N_RC else 0 end as Credit_RC"
        SQL = SQL & " into #GL"
        SQL = SQL & " from "
        SQL = SQL & " ("
        SQL = SQL & " Select pVoucher.Voucher, pVoucher.Date,"
        SQL = SQL & " Max(Case When Account.AccGroup='C' Then CLProcess.AccTo else '' end) as Account_C,"
        SQL = SQL & " Max(Case When Account.AccGroup='N' Then CLProcess.AccTo else '' end) as Account_N,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(case When Account.AccGroup='N' Then pJournal.debit-pJournal.Credit else 0 end) as Nominal_N,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(case When Account.AccGroup='C' Then pJournal.debit-pJournal.Credit else 0 end) as Nominal_C,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(case When Account.AccGroup='N' Then pJournal.debit_RC-pJournal.Credit_RC else 0 end) as Nominal_N_RC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(case When Account.AccGroup='C' Then pJournal.debit_RC-pJournal.Credit_RC else 0 end) as Nominal_C_RC"
        SQL = SQL & " from pVoucher, pJournal, Account, CLProcess"
        SQL = SQL & " Where pJournal.Voucher = pVoucher.Voucher And Account.Account = pJournal.Account And CLProcess.AccFrom = Account.Account And CLProcess.FElimination_Flag = 1"
        SQL = SQL & " and pVoucher.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and pVoucher.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
        SQL = SQL & " Group by pVoucher.Voucher, pVoucher.Date"
        SQL = SQL & " having "
        SQL = SQL & " round(sum(case When Account.AccGroup='N' Then pJournal.debit-pJournal.Credit else 0 end),4)<>0 and "
        SQL = SQL & " round(sum(case When Account.AccGroup='C' Then pJournal.debit-pJournal.Credit else 0 end),4)<>0"
        SQL = SQL & " ) Voucher, SysTab"
        SQL = SQL & " Order By Voucher.Date, Voucher.Account_N, Voucher.Account_C"

        If Not db.Execute(SQL) Then GoTo errmsg

        rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from #GL Order By Date, AccFrom, AccTo, Currency, CT", adOpenForwardonly)
        Do While Not rs_CL.Eof
            ' Initialize BufferData
            ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
            For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                BufferData(7, i) = ""      ' CT
                BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
            Next i

            i = 0
            PDate = rs_CL.fields("Date")
            Do While Not rs_CL.Eof AndAlso PDate = rs_CL.fields("Date")
                Account = rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")
                Currency = rs_CL.fields("Currency")
                CT = rs_CL.fields("CT")
                nDebit_OC = rs_CL.fields("Debit_OC")
                nCredit_OC = rs_CL.fields("Credit_OC")
                nDebit = rs_CL.fields("Debit")
                nCredit = rs_CL.fields("Credit")
                nDebit_RC = rs_CL.fields("Debit_RC")
                nCredit_RC = rs_CL.fields("Credit_RC")

                j = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, Account, 2, Currency, 7, CT)
                If j < i Then
                    ' Combine for the same account
                    i = i - 1
                Else
                    BufferData(0, j) = Account
                    BufferData(1, j) = ""
                    BufferData(2, j) = Currency
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                    BufferData(7, j) = CT
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                End If
                BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) + nDebit_OC
                BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) + nCredit_OC
                BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) + nDebit
                BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) + nCredit
                BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) + nDebit_RC
                BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) + nCredit_RC

                If BufferData(3, j) > BufferData(4, j) Then
                    BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) - BufferData(4, j)
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) - BufferData(3, j)
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(5, j) > BufferData(6, j) Then
                    BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) - BufferData(6, j)
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - BufferData(5, j)
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(8, j) > BufferData(9, j) Then
                    BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) - BufferData(9, j)
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - BufferData(8, j)
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                End If

                i = i + 1
                rs_CL.MoveNext()
            Loop

            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AccTo, sum(Debit) as Debit, sum(Credit) as Credit, sum(Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from #GL Where Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group by AccTo")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Account = rs.fields("AccTo")
                Currency = br_currency
                CT = ""
                nDebit_OC = rs.fields("Credit")
                nCredit_OC = rs.fields("Debit")
                nDebit = rs.fields("Credit")
                nCredit = rs.fields("Debit")
                nDebit_RC = rs.fields("Credit_RC")
                nCredit_RC = rs.fields("Debit_RC")

                j = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, Account, 2, Currency, 7, CT)
                If j < i Then
                    ' Combine for the same account
                    i = i - 1
                Else
                    BufferData(0, j) = Account
                    BufferData(1, j) = ""
                    BufferData(2, j) = Currency
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                    BufferData(7, j) = CT
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                End If
                BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) + nDebit_OC
                BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) + nCredit_OC
                BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) + nDebit
                BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) + nCredit
                BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) + nDebit_RC
                BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) + nCredit_RC

                If BufferData(3, j) > BufferData(4, j) Then
                    BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) - BufferData(4, j)
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) - BufferData(3, j)
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(5, j) > BufferData(6, j) Then
                    BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) - BufferData(6, j)
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - BufferData(5, j)
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(8, j) > BufferData(9, j) Then
                    BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) - BufferData(9, j)
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - BufferData(8, j)
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                End If

                i = i + 1
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()

            Dim AutoNum As String
            Dim sAutoNum As String
            Dim Source As String
            Dim VType As String
            Dim Voucher As String

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
            AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
            sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
            CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
            VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

            Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, PDate, br_branch)
            If Voucher = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
            rs.AddNew()
            rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
            rs.fields("VType") = VType
            rs.fields("Date") = PDate
            rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency
            rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
            rs.fields("RefType") = "Fund Elimination"
            rs.fields("Rate") = 1
            rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 0
            rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
            rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
            rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
            rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
            rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
            rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Fund Elimination)"
            rs.fields("Status") = "U"
            rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
            rs.fields("CT") = CT
            Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Fund Elimination", "", "", "X", PDate, sAutoNum)
            If Source = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs.fields("Source") = Source
            If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

            '=== Creating Voucher ===
            If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg

        Loop
        If Not db.Execute("Drop Table #GL") Then GoTo errmsg

        Generate_Fund_Elimination = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Generate_Fund_Elimination = False
    End Function

    Private Function Generate_Cost_Allocation(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal br_End_Date As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs_CL As RemoteSet
        Dim BufferData As Object

        Dim nDebit_OC As Double
        Dim nCredit_OC As Double
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double
        Dim Account As String
        Dim Currency As String
        Dim CT As String
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim j As Integer

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Dim SQL As String
        Dim PDate As Date

        db.Execute("Drop Table #GL")
        SQL = ""


        SQL = SQL & " Select Voucher.* "
        SQL = SQL & " into #GL"
        SQL = SQL & " from"
        SQL = SQL & " ("
        SQL = SQL & " Select Voucher.Date, "
        SQL = SQL & " Journal.Currency, "
        SQL = SQL & " Journal.Account as AccFrom, "
        SQL = SQL & " Journal.CT as CTFrom,"
        SQL = SQL & " C2.AccTo as AccTo,"
        SQL = SQL & " Case When C2.CT='<Any>' Then Journal.CT Else C2.CT End as CTTo,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit-Journal.Credit)<0 Then -(Journal.Debit-Journal.Credit) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Debit,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit-Journal.Credit)>0 Then (Journal.Debit-Journal.Credit) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Credit,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit_OC-Journal.Credit_OC)<0 Then -(Journal.Debit_OC-Journal.Credit_OC) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Debit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit_OC-Journal.Credit_OC)>0 Then (Journal.Debit_OC-Journal.Credit_OC) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Credit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit_RC-Journal.Credit_RC)<0 Then -(Journal.Debit_RC-Journal.Credit_RC) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Debit_RC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit_RC-Journal.Credit_RC)>0 Then (Journal.Debit_RC-Journal.Credit_RC) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Credit_RC"
        SQL = SQL & " from CSTALLOCATIONH C1"
        SQL = SQL & " Inner Join CSTALLOCATIOND C2 on (C2.Branch=C1.Branch and C2.Account=C1.Account)"
        SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Voucher on (Voucher.Branch=C2.Branch)"
        SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Journal on (Journal.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Journal.Account=C1.Account)"
        SQL = SQL & " Where C1.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and C1.AMethod in ('BYPCTSQM','BYHEADCNT') and C1.TotalUnit<>0"
        SQL = SQL & " and Voucher.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
        SQL = SQL & " Group by Voucher.Date, Journal.Account, Journal.CT, C2.AccTo, Case When C2.CT='<Any>' Then Journal.CT Else C2.CT End, Journal.Currency"
        SQL = SQL & " "
        SQL = SQL & " Union All"
        SQL = SQL & " "
        SQL = SQL & " Select Voucher.Date, "
        SQL = SQL & " Journal.Currency, "
        SQL = SQL & " AcD.MAccount as AccFrom, "
        SQL = SQL & " Journal.CT as CTFrom,"
        SQL = SQL & " C2.AccTo as AccTo,"
        SQL = SQL & " Case When C2.CT='<Any>' Then Journal.CT Else C2.CT End as CTTo,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit-Journal.Credit)<0 Then -(Journal.Debit-Journal.Credit) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Debit,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit-Journal.Credit)>0 Then (Journal.Debit-Journal.Credit) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Credit,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit_OC-Journal.Credit_OC)<0 Then -(Journal.Debit_OC-Journal.Credit_OC) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Debit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit_OC-Journal.Credit_OC)>0 Then (Journal.Debit_OC-Journal.Credit_OC) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Credit_OC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit_RC-Journal.Credit_RC)<0 Then -(Journal.Debit_RC-Journal.Credit_RC) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Debit_RC,"
        SQL = SQL & " sum(Case When (Journal.Debit_RC-Journal.Credit_RC)>0 Then (Journal.Debit_RC-Journal.Credit_RC) else 0 end*C2.NumUnit/C1.TotalUnit) as Credit_RC"
        SQL = SQL & " from CSTALLOCATIONH C1"
        SQL = SQL & " Inner Join CSTALLOCATIOND C2 on (C2.Branch=C1.Branch and C2.Account=C1.Account)"
        SQL = SQL & " Cross Apply ("
        SQL = SQL & "       Select AcD.Account, AcD.MAccount"
        SQL = SQL & "       from AccClass Ac"
        SQL = SQL & "       Inner Join AccClassDet AcD on (AcD.AccClass=Ac.AccClass)"
        SQL = SQL & "       Inner Join Account AccTo on (AccTo.Account=AcD.MAccount and AccTo.AccReport in (2))"
        SQL = SQL & "       Where Ac.AcGroup='IFRS17' and Acd.MAccount=C1.Account"
        SQL = SQL & " ) AcD"
        SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Voucher on (Voucher.Branch=C2.Branch)"
        SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Journal on (Journal.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Journal.Account=AcD.Account and Journal.Account<>C1.Account)"
        SQL = SQL & " Where C1.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and C1.AMethod in ('BYPCTSQM','BYHEADCNT') and C1.TotalUnit<>0"
        SQL = SQL & " and Voucher.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(br_End_Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
        SQL = SQL & " Group by Voucher.Date, AcD.MAccount, Journal.CT, C2.AccTo, Case When C2.CT='<Any>' Then Journal.CT Else C2.CT End, Journal.Currency"
        SQL = SQL & " ) Voucher"
        SQL = SQL & " Order By Voucher.Date, Voucher.AccFrom, Voucher.CTFrom, Voucher.AccTo, Voucher.CTTo, Voucher.Currency"

        If Not db.Execute(SQL) Then GoTo errmsg

        rs_CL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from #GL Order By Date, AccFrom, CTFrom,  AccTo, CTTo, Currency", adOpenForwardonly)
        Do While Not rs_CL.Eof
            ' Initialize BufferData
            ReDim BufferData(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
            For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                BufferData(0, i) = ""   'Account
                BufferData(1, i) = ""   'Description
                BufferData(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                BufferData(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                BufferData(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                BufferData(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                BufferData(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                BufferData(7, i) = ""      ' CT
                BufferData(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                BufferData(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
            Next i

            i = 0
            PDate = rs_CL.fields("Date")
            Do While Not rs_CL.Eof AndAlso PDate = rs_CL.fields("Date")
                Account = rs_CL.fields("AccFrom")
                Currency = rs_CL.fields("Currency")
                CT = rs_CL.fields("CTFrom")
                nDebit_OC = rs_CL.fields("Debit_OC")
                nCredit_OC = rs_CL.fields("Credit_OC")
                nDebit = rs_CL.fields("Debit")
                nCredit = rs_CL.fields("Credit")
                nDebit_RC = rs_CL.fields("Debit_RC")
                nCredit_RC = rs_CL.fields("Credit_RC")

                j = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, Account, 2, Currency, 7, CT)
                If j < i Then
                    ' Combine for the same account
                    i = i - 1
                Else
                    BufferData(0, j) = Account
                    BufferData(1, j) = ""
                    BufferData(2, j) = Currency
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                    BufferData(7, j) = CT
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                End If
                BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) + nDebit_OC
                BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) + nCredit_OC
                BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) + nDebit
                BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) + nCredit
                BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) + nDebit_RC
                BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) + nCredit_RC

                If BufferData(3, j) > BufferData(4, j) Then
                    BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) - BufferData(4, j)
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) - BufferData(3, j)
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(5, j) > BufferData(6, j) Then
                    BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) - BufferData(6, j)
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - BufferData(5, j)
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(8, j) > BufferData(9, j) Then
                    BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) - BufferData(9, j)
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - BufferData(8, j)
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                End If

                i = i + 1
                rs_CL.MoveNext()
            Loop

            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AccTo, CTTo, Currency, sum(Debit) as Debit, sum(Credit) as Credit, sum(Debit_OC) as Debit_OC, sum(Credit_OC) as Credit_OC, sum(Debit_RC) as Debit_RC, sum(Credit_RC) as Credit_RC from #GL Where Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Group by AccTo, CTTo, Currency")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Account = rs.fields("AccTo")
                Currency = rs.fields("Currency")
                CT = rs.fields("CTTo")
                nDebit_OC = rs.fields("Credit_OC")
                nCredit_OC = rs.fields("Debit_OC")
                nDebit = rs.fields("Credit")
                nCredit = rs.fields("Debit")
                nDebit_RC = rs.fields("Credit_RC")
                nCredit_RC = rs.fields("Debit_RC")

                j = Find_3_Element(BufferData, 0, i - 1, 0, Account, 2, Currency, 7, CT)
                If j < i Then
                    ' Combine for the same account
                    i = i - 1
                Else
                    BufferData(0, j) = Account
                    BufferData(1, j) = ""
                    BufferData(2, j) = Currency
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                    BufferData(7, j) = CT
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                End If
                BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) + nDebit_OC
                BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) + nCredit_OC
                BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) + nDebit
                BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) + nCredit
                BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) + nDebit_RC
                BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) + nCredit_RC

                If BufferData(3, j) > BufferData(4, j) Then
                    BufferData(3, j) = BufferData(3, j) - BufferData(4, j)
                    BufferData(4, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(4, j) = BufferData(4, j) - BufferData(3, j)
                    BufferData(3, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(5, j) > BufferData(6, j) Then
                    BufferData(5, j) = BufferData(5, j) - BufferData(6, j)
                    BufferData(6, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(6, j) = BufferData(6, j) - BufferData(5, j)
                    BufferData(5, j) = 0
                End If

                If BufferData(8, j) > BufferData(9, j) Then
                    BufferData(8, j) = BufferData(8, j) - BufferData(9, j)
                    BufferData(9, j) = 0
                Else
                    BufferData(9, j) = BufferData(9, j) - BufferData(8, j)
                    BufferData(8, j) = 0
                End If

                i = i + 1
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()

            Dim AutoNum As String
            Dim sAutoNum As String
            Dim Source As String
            Dim VType As String
            Dim Voucher As String

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AutoNum, SAutoNum, CT, VType from VType Where SL_Cat='X'")
            AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
            sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("SAutoNum"))
            CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
            VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))

            Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, PDate, br_branch)
            If Voucher = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
            rs.AddNew()
            rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
            rs.fields("VType") = VType
            rs.fields("Date") = PDate
            rs.fields("Currency") = br_currency
            rs.fields("SL_Cat") = "X"
            rs.fields("RefType") = "Cost Allocation"
            rs.fields("Rate") = 1
            rs.fields("Rate_RC") = 0
            rs.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
            rs.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
            rs.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
            rs.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
            rs.fields("SL_Flag") = False
            rs.fields("Remarks") = "System Journal (Cost Allocation)"
            rs.fields("Status") = "U"
            rs.fields("Branch") = br_branch
            rs.fields("CT") = CT
            Source = Get_Source_Doc(br_branch, "GJ", "", "Cost Allocation", "", "", "X", PDate, sAutoNum)
            If Source = "" Then
                StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs.fields("Source") = Source
            If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg

            '=== Creating Voucher ===
            If Not Save_Journal(Voucher, Source, CT, BufferData) Then GoTo errmsg

        Loop
        If Not db.Execute("Drop Table #GL") Then GoTo errmsg

        Generate_Cost_Allocation = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Generate_Cost_Allocation = False
    End Function

    Private Function Update_Exchange_Rate_CA_FX(ByVal Branch As String, ByVal Mth As Integer, ByVal Yr As Integer, ByVal Account As String, ByVal Ccy As String, ByVal Rate As Double) As Boolean
        Dim nDiff As Double
        Dim rs_FX As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rs_FX = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from CA_FX where branch='" & Branch & "' and [Month]=" & Mth & " and [Year]=" & Yr & " and Account='" & Account & "' and Currency='" & Ccy & "'", adOpenKeyset)
        Do While Not rs_FX.Eof
            nDiff = Math.Round(toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending") * Rate) - toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending_BC")), 4, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
            If nDiff <> 0 Then
                rs_FX.Edit()
                rs_FX.fields("Revaluation") = toval(rs_FX.fields("Revaluation")) + nDiff
                rs_FX.fields("Ending_BC") = toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending_BC")) + nDiff
                If Not rs_FX.Update Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs_FX.MoveNext()
        Loop
        Update_Exchange_Rate_CA_FX = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Exchange_Rate_CA_FX = False
    End Function

    Private Function Update_Exchange_Rate_CA_FX_RC(ByVal Branch As String, ByVal Mth As Integer, ByVal Yr As Integer, ByVal Account As String, ByVal Ccy As String, ByVal Rate_RC As Double) As Boolean
        Dim nDiff As Double
        Dim rs_FX As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rs_FX = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from CA_FX where branch='" & Branch & "' and [Month]=" & Mth & " and [Year]=" & Yr & " and Account='" & Account & "' and Currency='" & Ccy & "'", adOpenKeyset)
        Do While Not rs_FX.Eof
            nDiff = Math.Round(toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending") * Rate_RC) - toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending_RC")), 4, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
            If nDiff <> 0 Then
                rs_FX.Edit()
                rs_FX.fields("Revaluation_RC") = toval(rs_FX.fields("Revaluation_RC")) + nDiff
                rs_FX.fields("Ending_RC") = toval(rs_FX.fields("Ending_RC")) + nDiff
                If Not rs_FX.Update Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs_FX.MoveNext()
        Loop
        Update_Exchange_Rate_CA_FX_RC = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Exchange_Rate_CA_FX_RC = False
    End Function
    Private Function Calc_Tax_Amount(ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal Amount As Double, ByVal TaxPCT As Double, Optional ByVal TaxCCYRate As Double = 0) As Double
        Dim Amount_BC As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Difference As Double
        Dim TAX As Double
        Dim sign As Integer

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        If Amount <> 0 Then
            If TaxCCYRate = 0 Then TaxCCYRate = Tax_Rate(CCode, PDate)
            Amount_BC = TaxCCYRate * Amount
            TAX = 0
            sign = Abs(Amount_BC) / Amount_BC
            Amount_BC = Abs(Amount_BC)

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Lower, Upper, Tax from ProgTab Where [Lower]<=" & tovalSF(Amount_BC) & " Order By lower")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Difference = toval(rs.fields("Upper")) - toval(rs.fields("Lower"))
                If Difference > Amount_BC Then Difference = Amount_BC
                TAX = TAX + toval(rs.fields("Tax")) / 100 * TaxPCT / 100 * Difference
                Amount_BC = Amount_BC - Difference
                If Amount_BC = 0 Then Exit Do
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            Calc_Tax_Amount = -TAX / TaxCCYRate * sign
        Else
            Calc_Tax_Amount = 0
        End If
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Calc_Tax_Amount = 0
    End Function
    Private Function Calc_Tax_Amount(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal Amount As Double, ByVal TaxPCT As Double, Optional ByVal TaxCCYRate As Double = 0) As Double
        Dim Amount_BC As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Difference As Double
        Dim TAX As Double
        Dim sign As Integer

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        If Amount <> 0 Then
            If TaxCCYRate = 0 Then TaxCCYRate = Tax_Rate(db, CCode, PDate)
            Amount_BC = TaxCCYRate * Amount
            TAX = 0
            sign = Abs(Amount_BC) / Amount_BC
            Amount_BC = Abs(Amount_BC)

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Lower, Upper, Tax from ProgTab Where [Lower]<=" & tovalSF(Amount_BC) & " Order By lower")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                Difference = toval(rs.fields("Upper")) - toval(rs.fields("Lower"))
                If Difference > Amount_BC Then Difference = Amount_BC
                TAX = TAX + toval(rs.fields("Tax")) / 100 * TaxPCT / 100 * Difference
                Amount_BC = Amount_BC - Difference
                If Amount_BC = 0 Then Exit Do
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            Calc_Tax_Amount = -TAX / TaxCCYRate * sign
        Else
            Calc_Tax_Amount = 0
        End If
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Calc_Tax_Amount = 0
    End Function

    Private Function Tax_Rate(ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Object) As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        If CCode.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper And IsDate(PDate) Then
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select TRate from Currency Where Currency='" & CCode & "' and Effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            If Not rs.Eof Then
                Tax_Rate = rs.fields("TRate")
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select Top 1 exchange.TRate from exchange where exchange.currency='" & CCode & "' and exchange.effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Order by exchange.effective desc", adOpenForwardonly)
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    Tax_Rate = rs.fields("TRate")
                Else
                    Tax_Rate = 1
                End If
            End If
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        Else
            Tax_Rate = 1
        End If
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Tax_Rate = 1
    End Function
    Private Function Tax_Rate(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Object) As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        If CCode.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper And IsDate(PDate) Then
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select TRate from Currency Where Currency='" & CCode & "' and Effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            If Not rs.Eof Then
                Tax_Rate = rs.fields("TRate")
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select Top 1 exchange.TRate from exchange where exchange.currency='" & CCode & "' and exchange.effective<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Order by exchange.effective desc", adOpenForwardonly)
                If Not rs.Eof Then
                    Tax_Rate = rs.fields("TRate")
                Else
                    Tax_Rate = 1
                End If
            End If
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()
        Else
            Tax_Rate = 1
        End If
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Tax_Rate = 1
    End Function
    Private Function SQLObjectExists(ByVal ObjName As String) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select top 1 * from sysobjects where name='" & ObjName & "'")
        SQLObjectExists = Not rs.Eof
    End Function
    Private Sub Insert_Into_BPP_Record(ByVal Branch As String, ByVal Tariff As Double, ByVal PNominal As Double, ByVal tax As Double, ByVal Voucher As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal ID As String, ByVal TaxType As String, ByVal Code As String)
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Percentage As Double
        Dim Description As String

        Percentage = 0

        On Error GoTo ErrMsg
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from BPP Where 1<>1")
        rs.AddNew()
        rs.fields("Branch") = Branch
        rs.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
        rs.fields("Date") = PDate
        rs.fields("ID") = ID
        rs.fields("TaxType") = TaxType
        rs.fields("Tax") = RoundCCY(tax, br_currency, 0.01)
        rs.fields("TaxCode") = TaxCode_Map(tostr(TaxType), Code, Description)
        If rs.fields("TaxCode") = "PH2308" Then
            rs.fields("PNominal") = rs.fields("Tax") / 7.5 * 100
        Else
            If rs.fields("TaxCode") = "PH2309a" Or rs.fields("TaxCode") = "PH2309b" Then
                rs.fields("PNominal") = rs.fields("Tax") / 6 * 100
            Else
                rs.fields("PNominal") = PNominal
            End If
            rs.fields("PNominal") = PNominal
        End If

        rs.fields("Percentage") = Percentage
        rs.fields("Tariff") = Tariff
        rs.fields("Description") = Description
        rs.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
        rs.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
        rs.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
        If Not rs.Update Then GoTo ErrMsg
ErrMsg:
    End Sub

    Private Function TaxCode_Map(ByVal TaxType As String, ByVal Code As String, ByRef Description As String) As String
        Dim TaxCode As String
        Select Case Code
            Case "BA"
                If TaxType = "PASAL21" Then
                    TaxCode = "PH2109"
                    Description = ""
                Else
                    TaxCode = "PH2309a"
                    Description = "Jasa Perantara"
                End If
            Case "BB"
                If TaxType = "PASAL21" Then
                    TaxCode = "PH2109"
                    Description = ""
                Else
                    TaxCode = "PH2309a"
                    Description = "Jasa Perantara"
                End If
            Case "BO"
                If TaxType = "PASAL21" Then
                    TaxCode = "PH2109"
                    Description = ""
                Else
                    TaxCode = "PH2309a"
                    Description = "Jasa Perantara"
                End If
            Case "DI"
                If TaxType = "PASAL21" Then
                    TaxCode = "PH2112"
                    Description = ""
                Else
