                    TaxCode = "PH2309b"
                    Description = "Jasa Perbaikan alat-alat Transportasi"
                End If
            Case "OP"
                If TaxType = "PASAL21" Then
                    TaxCode = "PH2112"
                    Description = ""
                Else
                    TaxCode = "PH2308"
                    Description = ""
                End If
        End Select
        TaxCode_Map = TaxCode
    End Function

    Private Function GetSystemParameter() As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, Currency, Currency_1, CalculationRoundingF, WithHoldingTaxF, CheckMonthlyExchangeRateF, CheckWeeklyExchangeRateF, LockedDate, TaxTablePaidF, BudgetFeaturesF, PaidEliminationF, FundEliminationF from SysTab", adOpenForwardonly)
        If Not rs.Eof Then
            br_currency = tostr(rs.fields("Currency"))
            br_currency_1 = tostr(rs.fields("Currency_1"))
            If br_currency_1 = "" Then br_currency_1 = br_currency
            br_branch = tostr(rs.fields("Branch"))
            br_WithHoldingTax = toval(rs.fields("WithHoldingTaxF"))
            CheckMonthlyExchangeRateF = toval(rs.fields("CheckMonthlyExchangeRateF"))
            CheckWeeklyExchangeRateF = toval(rs.fields("CheckWeeklyExchangeRateF"))
            LockedDate = todate(rs.fields("LockedDate"))
            TaxTableF = toval(rs.fields("TaxTablePaidF"))
            BudgetFeaturesF = toval(rs.fields("BudgetFeaturesF"))
            PaidEliminationF = toval(rs.fields("PaidEliminationF"))
            FundEliminationF = toval(rs.fields("FundEliminationF"))
            CalculationRoundingF = toval(rs.fields("CalculationRoundingF"))
        End If
        rs.CloseRemoteSet()
    End Function
    Private Function Create_Admlink_Voucher_All(ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim SDate As Date
        Dim EDate As Date
        Dim rsBranch As RemoteSet
        SDate = DateSerial(Year(PDate), Month(PDate), 1)
        EDate = Last_date_of_period(PDate, 1)

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch from Branch Where OP_Flag=1 and Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and End_Date<'" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
        Do While Not rsBranch.Eof
            If Not Create_Admlink_Voucher(rsBranch.fields("Branch"), SDate, EDate) Then GoTo errmsg
            rsBranch.MoveNext()
        Loop
        Create_Admlink_Voucher_All = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Create_Admlink_Voucher_All = False
    End Function

    Private Function Create_Admlink_Voucher(ByVal Branch As String, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date, Optional ByVal CheckLockedDateF As Boolean = False) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AdmNo from Admlink Where Branch='" & Branch & "' and Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and VoucherF=0 and WriteF=0 and Amount<>0 ", adOpenForwardonly)
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            If Me.Direct_Call_Process_Execute("Process.NetV3.Proclib", "Create_Voucher", rs.fields("AdmNo"), CheckLockedDateF) = "" Then
                StrMsg = Me.ErrDesc
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop
        Create_Admlink_Voucher = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Create_Admlink_Voucher = False
    End Function
    Private Function Create_Proacc(ByVal ID As String, ByVal CCY As String) As Boolean
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        If Not db.Execute("Insert into ProAcc(ProAcc,[Currency],Description, ID, RestrictedF, PUserDate, PUserID, Purpose) Select ID+'-'+ left([Currency].[Currency],3), [Currency].[Currency], Profile.Name, Profile.ID, 0, '" & Format(Today.Date, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & db_user & "','' from Profile, [Currency] Where Profile.ID='" & ID & "' and [Currency].[Currency]='" & CCY & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then GoTo errmsg
        Create_Proacc = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        Create_Proacc = False
    End Function

    Private Function Check_Monthly_Exchange_Rate(ByVal CCY As String, ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim SDate As Date
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        If br_currency <> CCY Then
            SDate = DateSerial(Year(PDate), Month(PDate), 1)
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select top 1 Currency from Exchange Where Currency='" & CCY & "' and Effective Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            Check_Monthly_Exchange_Rate = (Not rs.Eof)
        Else
            Check_Monthly_Exchange_Rate = True
        End If
    End Function
    Private Function Check_Weekly_Exchange_Rate(ByVal CCY As String, ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim SDate As Date
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        If br_currency <> CCY Then
            If Weekday(PDate) >= 2 Then
                SDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, -(Weekday(PDate) - 2), PDate)
            Else
                SDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, -(7 - Weekday(PDate)), PDate)
            End If
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select top 1 Currency from Exchange Where Currency='" & CCY & "' and Effective Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            Check_Weekly_Exchange_Rate = (Not rs.Eof)
        Else
            Check_Weekly_Exchange_Rate = True
        End If
    End Function
    Private Function StrError(Optional ByVal errmsg As String = "") As String
        Dim str As String
        Dim ErrDesc As String
        ErrDesc = Err.Description
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        str = errmsg
        If str = "" Then str = ErrDesc
        If str = "" Then str = db.ErrDesc
        StrError = str
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrError = "Connection is Broken !"
    End Function
    Private Function Get_ProAcc(ByVal ID As String, ByVal CCY As String) As String
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select ProAcc from ProAcc Where ID='" & ID & "' and Currency='" & CCY & "'")
        If rs.Eof Then
            If Not Create_Proacc(ID, CCY) Then GoTo errmsg
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select ProAcc from ProAcc Where ID='" & ID & "' and Currency='" & CCY & "'")
        End If
        Get_ProAcc = tostr(rs.fields("ProAcc"))
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Get_ProAcc = ""
    End Function
    Private Function ReConstruct_Fiscal(ByVal Branch As String, ByVal Mth As Integer, ByVal Yr As Integer) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rsF As RemoteSet
        Dim rsH As RemoteSet
        Dim CumulativeF As Boolean


        Dim TStarting As Double
        Dim TDebit As Double
        Dim TCredit As Double
        Dim Tax_Amount As Double
        Dim Pre_Amount As Double

        On Error GoTo ErrMsg

        rsH = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL_FiscalH Where Branch='" & Branch & "' and Month=" & Mth & " and Year=" & Yr)
        If Not rsH.Eof Then
            CumulativeF = toval(rsH.fields("CumulativeF"))
            If CumulativeF Then   ' Cumulative
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select A.Account, A.Description, GL.Closing+GL.Ending as Ending From GL, Account A, Type T, Branch B where B.Branch=GL.Branch and B.TX_Account<>GL.Account and GL.Account=A.Account and A.Type=T.Type and T.PL_Flag=1 and GL.Branch='" & Branch & "' and GL.Month=" & Mth & " and GL.Year=" & Yr & " and A.Post_Flag=1 order by GL.Account")
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select A.Account, A.Description, GL.Ending as Ending From GL, Account A, Type T, Branch B where B.Branch=GL.Branch and B.TX_Account<>GL.Account and GL.Account=A.Account and A.Type=T.Type and T.PL_Flag=1 and GL.Branch='" & Branch & "' and GL.Month=" & Mth & " and GL.Year=" & Yr & " and A.Post_Flag=1 order by GL.Account")
            End If
            TStarting = 0
            TDebit = 0
            TCredit = 0
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                rsF = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL_Fiscal where Branch='" & Branch & "' and Month=" & Mth & " and Year=" & Yr & " and Account='" & rs.fields("Account") & "'")
                If rsF.Eof Then
                    rsF.AddNew()
                    rsF.fields("Branch") = Branch
                    rsF.fields("Month") = Mth
                    rsF.fields("Year") = Yr
                    rsF.fields("Account") = rs.fields("Account")
                    rsF.fields("Remarks") = ""
                    rsF.fields("Debit") = 0
                    rsF.fields("Credit") = 0
                Else
                    rsF.Edit()
                End If
                rsF.fields("Starting") = toval(rs.fields("Ending"))
                rsF.fields("Ending") = toval(rsF.fields("Starting")) + toval(rsF.fields("Debit")) - toval(rsF.fields("Credit"))
                If Not rsF.Update Then GoTo ErrMsg
                TStarting = TStarting + toval(rsF.fields("Starting"))
                TDebit = TDebit + toval(rsF.fields("Debit"))
                TCredit = TCredit + toval(rsF.fields("Credit"))
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            rs.CloseRemoteSet()


            'PPH 29
            If -(TStarting + TDebit - TCredit) > 0 Then
                Tax_Amount = Calculate_Progress(br_currency, Today.Date, (TStarting + TDebit - TCredit))
            Else
                Tax_Amount = 0
            End If


            'Prepaid Tax
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select A.Account, A.Description, GL.Ending as Ending From Account A Left Join GL On (GL.Account=A.Account and GL.Branch='" & Branch & "' and GL.Month=" & Mth & " and GL.Year=" & Yr & "),Type T where A.Type=T.Type and A.Post_Flag=1 and A.PT_Flag=1")
            Pre_Amount = 0
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                rsF = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from GL_FiscalP where Branch='" & Branch & "' and Month=" & Mth & " and Year=" & Yr & " and Account='" & rs.fields("Account") & "'")
                If rsF.Eof Then
                    rsF.AddNew()
                    rsF.fields("Branch") = Branch
                    rsF.fields("Month") = Mth
                    rsF.fields("Year") = Yr
                    rsF.fields("Account") = rs.fields("Account")
                Else
                    rsF.Edit()
                End If
                rsF.fields("Amount") = toval(rs.fields("Ending"))
                If Not rsF.Update Then GoTo ErrMsg
                Pre_Amount = Pre_Amount + toval(rs.fields("Ending"))
                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop

            rsH.Edit()
            rsH.fields("Starting") = TStarting
            rsH.fields("Debit") = TDebit
            rsH.fields("Credit") = TCredit
            rsH.fields("Ending") = TStarting + TDebit - TCredit
            rsH.fields("Tax_Amount") = Tax_Amount
            rsH.fields("Pre_Amount") = Pre_Amount
            rsH.fields("Pay_Amount") = Tax_Amount - Pre_Amount
            If Not rsH.Update Then GoTo ErrMsg
        End If

        ReConstruct_Fiscal = True
        Exit Function
ErrMsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        ReConstruct_Fiscal = False
    End Function


    Private Function Calculate_Progress(ByVal CCode As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal Amount As Double) As Double
        Dim TaxRate As Double
        Dim Amount_BC As Double
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Difference As Double
        Dim TAX As Double
        Dim Sign As Integer

        On Error GoTo ErrMsg
        TaxRate = Tax_Rate(CCode, PDate)
        Amount_BC = TaxRate * Amount
        TAX = 0
        Sign = Abs(Amount_BC) / Amount_BC
        Amount_BC = Abs(Amount_BC)

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from ProgressTab Where LRange<=" & tovalSF(Amount_BC) & " Order By LRange")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            Difference = toval(rs.fields("URange")) - toval(rs.fields("LRange"))
            If Difference > Amount_BC Then Difference = Amount_BC
            TAX = TAX + toval(rs.fields("Tax")) / 100 * Difference
            Amount_BC = Amount_BC - Difference
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop
        Calculate_Progress = -TAX / TaxRate * Sign
        Exit Function
ErrMsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Calculate_Progress = 0
    End Function
    Private Function Get_Open_Item(ByVal Voucher As String) As RemoteSet
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select * from get_open_Item_N('" & Voucher & "')")
        Get_Open_Item = rs
    End Function
    Private Function Process_AccLink_All(ByVal PDate As Date, Optional ByVal PEDate As Object = Nothing) As Boolean
        Try
            db.Committrans()
            Dim rsParam As RemoteSet
            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            Dim PType As String

            rsParam = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Type from Mparam Where MemorialF=1 order by ExecOrderNo")
            Do While Not rsParam.Eof
                PType = rsParam.fields("Type")
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 Date from AccLog Where Type='" & PType & "' Order by Date Desc")
                If Not rs.Eof Then PEDate = rs.fields("Date")
                If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PEDate, PDate) > 0 Then
                    db.Begintrans()
                    If Not Process_AccLink(PType, PDate, PEDate) Then
                        StrMsg = "Can't Generate Accounting Link for Type : " & PType & "-" & StrMsg
                        Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                    End If
                    db.Committrans()
                End If
                rsParam.MoveNext()
            Loop
            Process_AccLink_All = True
            db.Begintrans()
        Catch ex As Exception
            StrMsg = ex.Message
            Process_AccLink_All = False
        End Try
    End Function
    Private Function Process_AccLink(ByVal PType As String, ByVal PDate As Date, Optional ByVal PEDate As Object = Nothing) As Boolean
        Try
            Dim EDate As Date
            Dim SQL As String = ""
            Dim AccNo As Long
            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            Dim rsAcc As RemoteSet

            ' Process Accounting Link Must Create Admlink
            If PType <> "PL" And PType <> "PB" And PType <> "CL" And PType <> "CB" Then
                If Not Create_Admlink_Voucher_All(PDate) Then
                    Throw New Exception("Can't Create Admlink Voucher !" & StrMsg)
                End If
            End If

            ' Save to Log
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from AccLog Where Type='" & PType & "' and Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            If Not rs.Eof Then
                Throw New Exception("Accounting Log for Type : " & PType & ", Date : " & Format(PDate, "d") & " Already exists !")
            End If
            rs.AddNew()
            rs.fields("Type") = PType
            rs.fields("Date") = PDate
            rs.fields("db_User") = db_user
            rs.fields("PDate") = PDate
            rs.fields("PEDate") = todate(PEDate)
            rs.fields("PTime") = Time()
            If Not rs.Update Then Throw New Exception(rs.ErrDesc)

            EDate = PDate
            If PEDate Is Nothing Then
                PEDate = Last_date_of_period(DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, -1, PDate), 1)
            End If
            ' Preliminary Process
            Select Case PType
                Case "IF"   ' IFRS Monthly Process
                    Dim Dataset As DataSet
                    If Not db.Execute("exec IFRS_Data_Create_Temp  '" & Format(DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, PEDate), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'") Then
                        Throw New Exception(db.ErrDesc)
                    End If

                    rs = db.OpenQueryDef("IFRS_Data", adOpenKeyset, DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, PEDate), EDate)
                    Dataset = rs.GetDataSet
                    If Not Me.Direct_Call_Process_Execute("ProcessIFRS.NetV3.Proclib", "IFRS_PROCESS", PDate, Dataset) Then
                        Throw New Exception(Me.ErrDesc)
                    End If
            End Select
            Select Case PType
                Case "DJ"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM DEFERRED_PREMIUM_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "PR", "PC"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM RESERVED_PREMIUM_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "CC"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM RESERVED_CLAIM_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "PL", "PB"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM RESERVED_PREMIUM_BEFORE('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "CB"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM RESERVED_CLAIM_BEFORE('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "CR"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM IBNR_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "CL"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM IBNR_BEFORE('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "XA"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM XOL_AMORTIZATION_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "SD"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM POLICY_STAMPDUTY_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "SI"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM SUM_INSURED_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "JA"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM JOURNAL_ADJUSTMENT_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "VI"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM VAT_IN_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "VO"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM VAT_OUT_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "AD"   'Allowance For DoubtFul Account
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM AFDA_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "TD"   'Treaty Not Yet Due
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM TNYD_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "PT"   ' Prepaid Tax
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM TAX_PREPAID_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "IF"   ' IFRS
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM IFRS_Movement_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
                Case "LC"   ' Loss (Recovery) Component
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM IFRS_LOSSCOMPONENT_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "')"
            End Select

            If SQL <> "" Then
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL)
                rsAcc = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from AccLink Where 1<>1")
                Do While Not rs.Eof
                    Dim ContinueProcessF As Boolean = False
                    For i = 1 To 35
                        If toval(rs.fields("Amount_" & i)) <> 0 Then
                            ContinueProcessF = True
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next
                    If ContinueProcessF Then
                        rsAcc.AddNew()
                        If Not db.backup_record(rs, rsAcc) Then Throw New Exception(db.ErrDesc)
                        AccNo = db.GetIdentity("ACCLINK") + 1
                        rsAcc.fields("AccNo") = AccNo
                        rsAcc.fields("LUserID") = db_user
                        rsAcc.fields("LDate") = Today.Date
                        rsAcc.fields("LTime") = Time()
                        rsAcc.fields("PDate") = PDate
                        If Not rsAcc.Update Then Throw New Exception(rsAcc.ErrDesc)
                        If Not Create_AccLink_Memorial(AccNo, toval(rs.fields("Rate")), toval(rs.fields("Rate_RC"))) Then
                            Throw New Exception("Can't Create Memorial Journal !")
                        End If

                        If InStr("IFIB|IFRB|IFIP|IFRP", rsAcc.fields("Type")) <> 0 AndAlso toval(rsAcc.fields("Amount_27")) = 0 Then
                            SQL = "Update I1 Set AccNo=" & AccNo
                            SQL = SQL & " from IFRS_Movement I1 "
                            SQL = SQL & " Inner join IFRS_Calculation I2 on (I2.GroupID=I1.GroupID)"
                            SQL = SQL & " Where I1.ProcessDate='" & Format(rsAcc.fields("PDate"), "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                            If toval(rsAcc.fields("GroupID")) <> 0 Then
                                SQL = SQL & " and I2.GroupID=" & toval(rsAcc.fields("GroupID"))
                            End If
                            SQL = SQL & " and I2.Branch='" & rsAcc.fields("Branch") & "' and I2.TOC='" & rsAcc.fields("Code") & "' and I2.Currency='" & rsAcc.fields("Currency") & "' "
                            SQL = SQL & " and "
                            SQL = SQL & " ("
                            SQL = SQL & " Case "
                            SQL = SQL & " When I1.GroupType='I' and I2.Method='B' then 'IFIB'"
                            SQL = SQL & " When I1.GroupType='R' and I2.Method='B' then 'IFRB'"
                            SQL = SQL & " When I1.GroupType='I' and I2.Method='P' then 'IFIP'"
                            SQL = SQL & " Else 'IFRP'"
                            SQL = SQL & " End "
                            SQL = SQL & " ) = '" & rsAcc.fields("Type") & "'"
                            If Not db.Execute(SQL) Then Throw New Exception(db.ErrDesc)
                        End If
                    End If
                    rs.MoveNext()
                Loop
            End If
            Process_AccLink = True
        Catch ex As Exception
            StrMsg = ex.Message
            Process_AccLink = False
        End Try
    End Function
    Private Function Create_AccLink_Memorial(ByVal AccNo As Long, Optional ByVal Rate As Double = 0, Optional ByVal Rate_RC As Double = 0) As Boolean
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Amount(35) As Double
        Dim Nominal As Double
        Dim Diff As Double
        Dim bufferdata As Object

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        Dim SL_Cat As String
        Dim Ccy As String
        Dim CT As String
        Dim Branch As String
        Dim RefNo As String
        Dim DocNo As String
        Dim Vat As Double
        Dim TAX As Double
        Dim VoucherDate As Date
        Dim BillDate As Date
        Dim Source As String
        Dim Preference As String
        Dim Remarks As String
        Dim Voucher As String

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Acclink.* from Acclink where Acclink.AccNo=" & AccNo & " and VoucherF=0")
        If rs.Eof Then
            StrMsg = "No Acclink Data !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        ' Initialize BufferData
        ReDim bufferdata(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
        For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
            bufferdata(0, i) = ""   'Account
            bufferdata(1, i) = ""   'Description
            bufferdata(2, i) = ""   'Currency
            bufferdata(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
            bufferdata(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
            bufferdata(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
            bufferdata(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
            bufferdata(7, i) = ""    'CT
            bufferdata(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
            bufferdata(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
        Next i

        Nominal = 0
        Diff = 0

        For i = 1 To 35
            Amount(i) = toval(rs.fields("Amount_" & i))
            If Amount(i) > 0 Then
                Nominal = Nominal + Amount(i)
            Else
                Diff = Diff - Amount(i)
            End If
        Next i

        Voucher = tostr(rs.fields("Voucher"))
        Ccy = tostr(rs.fields("Currency"))
        CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
        Branch = tostr(rs.fields("Branch"))
        RefNo = tostr(rs.fields("RefNo"))
        DocNo = tostr(rs.fields("DocNo"))
        Vat = toval(Amount(13))
        TAX = toval(Amount(14))
        VoucherDate = todate(rs.fields("Date"))
        BillDate = todate(rs.fields("Date"))
        Remarks = tostr(rs.fields("Remarks"))

        SL_Cat = "M"

        Preference = tostr(rs.fields("Type") & "-" & rs.fields("Code"))
        rs.CloseRemoteSet()

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("SELECT Top 1 VType.vType, VType.Description, VType.AutoNum, VType.DebtorF, VType.CreditorF, VType.sAutoNum From VType WHERE AllowedF=1 and '" & Preference & "' like VType.Code+'%' and VType.SL_Cat='" & SL_Cat & "' Order by VType.CodeExt Desc, Len(VType.Code) Desc, difference('" & Preference & "',VType.Code) Desc")
        If rs.Eof Then
            StrMsg = "No Voucher Type for Preference : " & Preference & " !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        Dim VType As String
        Dim CreditorF As Boolean
        Dim DebtorF As Boolean
        Dim AutoNum As String
        Dim sAutoNum As String

        VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))
        DebtorF = toval(rs.fields("DebtorF"))
        CreditorF = toval(rs.fields("CreditorF"))
        AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
        sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("sAutoNum"))
        If Remarks = "" Then
            Remarks = tostr(rs.fields("Description"))
        Else
            Remarks = Remarks + " - " + tostr(rs.fields("Description"))
        End If

        If Rate = 0 Then Rate = exchange_rate(Ccy, VoucherDate, br_currency)
        If Rate = 0 Then
            StrMsg = "No Exchange Rate for Base Currency !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        If br_currency_1 <> "" AndAlso br_currency_1.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper Then
            If Rate_RC = 0 Then Rate_RC = exchange_rate_RC(Ccy, VoucherDate, br_currency_1)
            If Rate_RC = 0 Then
                StrMsg = "No Exchange Rate for 2nd Base Currency !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
        Else
            Rate_RC = Rate
        End If

        Dim DataMaster As RemoteSet
        DataMaster = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
        DataMaster.AddNew()
        If Voucher = "" Then Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, VoucherDate, Branch)
        If Voucher = "" Then
            StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        DataMaster.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
        DataMaster.fields("VType") = VType
        DataMaster.fields("Date") = VoucherDate
        DataMaster.fields("PDate") = VoucherDate
        DataMaster.fields("Rate") = Rate
        DataMaster.fields("Rate_RC") = Rate_RC
        DataMaster.fields("DueDate") = BillDate
        DataMaster.fields("CT") = CT
        DataMaster.fields("CreditorF") = toval(CreditorF)
        DataMaster.fields("DebtorF") = toval(DebtorF)
        DataMaster.fields("SL_Flag") = True
        DataMaster.fields("Status") = "U"
        DataMaster.fields("Currency") = Ccy
        DataMaster.fields("Nominal_CC") = Nominal
        DataMaster.fields("Diff_CC") = Diff
        DataMaster.fields("Nominal") = Nominal * Rate
        DataMaster.fields("Diff") = Diff * Rate
        DataMaster.fields("Branch") = Branch
        DataMaster.fields("SL_Cat") = SL_Cat
        DataMaster.fields("RefNo") = RefNo
        DataMaster.fields("DocNo") = DocNo
        DataMaster.fields("Remarks") = Remarks
        DataMaster.fields("NDueDate") = todate(DataMaster.fields("DueDate"))
        DataMaster.fields("CDate") = Today.Date
        DataMaster.fields("CTime") = Time()
        DataMaster.fields("UserID") = db_user
        DataMaster.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
        DataMaster.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
        DataMaster.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
        DataMaster.fields("nPayment") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("Payment_CC") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("Payment_OC") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
        DataMaster.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
        DataMaster.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
        DataMaster.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
        DataMaster.fields("TaxPayment_CC") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("TaxPayment_OC") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("Num_Doc") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("SL_Tag") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("AssetF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("InvestmentF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("SourceF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("BranchF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("ChequeF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("BackDatedF") = 0
        Source = Get_Source_Doc(Branch, "GJ", RefNo, "", "", "", SL_Cat, DataMaster.fields("Date"), sAutoNum, "")
        If Source = "" Then
            StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        DataMaster.fields("Source") = Source

        Dim Br_End_Date As Date
        Dim br_Month As Integer
        Dim br_year As Integer
        Dim BackDatedDate As Date
        Dim BackDatedF As Boolean
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select B.end_date, B.Month, B.Year, B.BackDatedDate, S.BackDatedF from Branch B, SysUser S Where S.ID='" & db_user & "' and B.Branch='" & Branch & "'")
        Br_End_Date = todate(rs.fields("end_date"))
        br_Month = toval(rs.fields("month"))
        br_year = toval(rs.fields("year"))
        BackDatedDate = todate(rs.fields("BackDatedDate"))
        BackDatedF = toval(rs.fields("BackDatedF"))
        If (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, VoucherDate, Br_End_Date) = 0 And br_Month = Month(VoucherDate) And br_year = Year(VoucherDate)) Or (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, VoucherDate, Br_End_Date) > 0) Then
            If BackDatedF And DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, VoucherDate, BackDatedDate) >= 0 Then
                DataMaster.fields("BackDatedF") = True
                DataMaster.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
                DataMaster.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
                DataMaster.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
                DataMaster.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
                DataMaster.fields("Status") = "P"
            Else
                ' Can't Backdated
                StrMsg = "Please Check The Last Ending Posting Date !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
        Else
            DataMaster.fields("BackDatedF") = False
            DataMaster.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
            DataMaster.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
            DataMaster.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
            DataMaster.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
            DataMaster.fields("Status") = "U"
        End If
        If Not DataMaster.Update() Then GoTo errmsg

        i = 0
        Dim rsV As RemoteSet
        Dim sAccount As String
        Dim Description As String
        Dim sCT As String
        Dim sCurrency As String
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim j As Integer
        rsV = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select vPattern.*, Account.Description, Account.Monetary_Flag from vPattern, Account Where Account.Account=vPattern.Account and vPattern.VType='" & VType & "'")
        Do While Not rsV.Eof
            sAccount = tostr(rsV.fields("Account"))
            Description = tostr(rsV.fields("Description"))
            If toval(rsV.fields("Debit")) <> 0 Then
                nDebit = Amount(Abs(toval(rsV.fields("Debit"))))
                nCredit = 0
            End If

            If toval(rsV.fields("Credit")) <> 0 Then
                nDebit = 0
                nCredit = Amount(Abs(toval(rsV.fields("Credit"))))
            End If

            If nDebit <> 0 OrElse nCredit <> 0 Then
                If rsV.fields("Monetary_Flag") Then
                    sCurrency = Ccy
                Else
                    sCurrency = br_currency
                    nDebit = nDebit * Rate
                    nCredit = nCredit * Rate
                End If

                If nDebit < 0 Then
                    nCredit = -nDebit
                    nDebit = 0
                ElseIf nCredit < 0 Then
                    nDebit = -nCredit
                    nCredit = 0
                End If
                sCT = ""

                j = Find_3_Element(bufferdata, 0, i - 1, 0, sAccount, 2, sCurrency, 7, sCT)
                If j < i Then
                    ' Combine for the same account
                    i = i - 1
                Else
                    bufferdata(0, j) = sAccount
                    bufferdata(1, j) = Description
                    bufferdata(2, j) = sCurrency
                    bufferdata(3, j) = 0
                    bufferdata(4, j) = 0
                    bufferdata(5, j) = 0
                    bufferdata(6, j) = 0
                    bufferdata(7, j) = sCT
                    bufferdata(8, j) = 0
                    bufferdata(9, j) = 0
                End If

                bufferdata(3, j) = bufferdata(3, j) + nDebit
                bufferdata(4, j) = bufferdata(4, j) + nCredit
                If bufferdata(3, j) > bufferdata(4, j) Then
                    bufferdata(3, j) = bufferdata(3, j) - bufferdata(4, j)
                    nDebit = nDebit - bufferdata(4, j)
                    nCredit = nCredit - bufferdata(4, j)
                    bufferdata(4, j) = 0
                Else
                    bufferdata(4, j) = bufferdata(4, j) - bufferdata(3, j)
                    nDebit = nDebit - bufferdata(3, j)
                    nCredit = nCredit - bufferdata(3, j)
                    bufferdata(3, j) = 0
                End If

                If sCurrency = br_currency Then
                    bufferdata(5, j) = bufferdata(3, j)
                    bufferdata(6, j) = bufferdata(4, j)
                    bufferdata(8, j) = bufferdata(3, j) * Rate_RC / Rate
                    bufferdata(9, j) = bufferdata(4, j) * Rate_RC / Rate
                Else
                    bufferdata(5, j) = bufferdata(3, j) * Rate
                    bufferdata(6, j) = bufferdata(4, j) * Rate
                    bufferdata(8, j) = bufferdata(3, j) * Rate_RC
                    bufferdata(9, j) = bufferdata(4, j) * Rate_RC
                End If
                i = i + 1
            End If
            rsV.MoveNext()
        Loop
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mJournal where 1<>1")
        Dim MaxRows As Integer = CType(bufferdata, Object(,)).GetUpperBound(1) + 1
        For i = 0 To MaxRows - 1
            If Trim(bufferdata(0, i)) <> "" And Trim(bufferdata(2, i)) <> "" And (toval(bufferdata(5, i)) <> 0 Or toval(bufferdata(6, i)) <> 0 Or toval(bufferdata(8, i)) <> 0 Or toval(bufferdata(9, i)) <> 0) Then
                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("Voucher") = tostr(Voucher)
                rs.fields("SortNo") = i
                rs.fields("Account") = tostr(bufferdata(0, i))
                rs.fields("Description") = tostr(bufferdata(1, i))
                rs.fields("Currency") = tostr(bufferdata(2, i))

                If Not CalculationRoundingF Then
                    rs.fields("Debit_OC") = toval(bufferdata(3, i))
                    rs.fields("Credit_OC") = toval(bufferdata(4, i))
                    rs.fields("Debit") = toval(bufferdata(5, i))
                    rs.fields("Credit") = toval(bufferdata(6, i))
                    rs.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(bufferdata(8, i))
                    rs.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(bufferdata(9, i))
                Else
                    rs.fields("Debit_OC") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(3, i)), UCase(tostr(bufferdata(2, i))), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Credit_OC") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(4, i)), UCase(tostr(bufferdata(2, i))), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Debit") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(5, i)), br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Credit") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(6, i)), br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Debit_RC") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(8, i)), br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Credit_RC") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(9, i)), br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                End If

                rs.fields("CT") = tostr(bufferdata(7, i))
                rs.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
                rs.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                rs.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                If Not rs.UpdateRow Then GoTo errmsg

                'Updating Journal_Account
                If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, rs.fields("Account"), rs.fields("Currency"), rs.fields("CT"), rs.fields("Debit"), rs.fields("Credit"), rs.fields("Debit_OC"), rs.fields("Credit_OC"), rs.fields("Debit_RC"), rs.fields("Credit_RC"), StrMsg) Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
        Next i
        If Not rs.Eof Then
            If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        End If
        rs.CloseRemoteSet()

        ' Update AccLink Status
        If Not db.Execute("Update Acclink Set VoucherF=1, Voucher='" & Voucher & "' Where AccNo=" & AccNo & " and VoucherF=0") Then GoTo errmsg
        If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then GoTo errmsg

        Create_AccLink_Memorial = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Create_AccLink_Memorial = False
    End Function
    Private Function Delete_AccLink(ByVal PType As String, ByVal PDate As Date, Optional ByVal AllF As Boolean = True) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim PEDate As Date
        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from AccLog Where Type='" & PType & "' and Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
        If rs.Eof Then
            StrMsg = "Accounting Log Not Found !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        PEDate = rs.fields("PEDate")


        If PType = "IF" Then
            If AllF Then
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Distinct Voucher.Voucher from mVoucher Voucher, AccLink Where Voucher.Voucher=AccLink.Voucher and AccLink.VoucherF=1 and AccLink.Type in ('IFIB','IFRB','IFIP','IFRP','IFPB','IFPP') and AccLink.PDate>'" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and AccLink.PDate<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Distinct Voucher.Voucher from mVoucher Voucher, AccLink Where Voucher.Voucher=AccLink.Voucher and AccLink.VoucherF=1 and AccLink.Type in ('IFIB','IFRB','IFIP','IFRP','IFPB','IFPP') and AccLink.PDate>'" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and AccLink.PDate<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and (AccLink.Extra_1='' or AccLink.Extra_1 is null)")
            End If
        Else
            If AllF Then
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Distinct Voucher.Voucher from mVoucher Voucher, AccLink Where Voucher.Voucher=AccLink.Voucher and AccLink.VoucherF=1 and AccLink.Type='" & PType & "' and AccLink.PDate>'" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and AccLink.PDate<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            Else
                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Distinct Voucher.Voucher from mVoucher Voucher, AccLink Where Voucher.Voucher=AccLink.Voucher and AccLink.VoucherF=1 and AccLink.Type='" & PType & "' and AccLink.PDate>'" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and AccLink.PDate<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and (AccLink.Extra_1='' or AccLink.Extra_1 is null)")
            End If
        End If

        Do While Not rs.Eof
            If Not Cancel_AccLink_Memorial(rs.fields("Voucher")) Then GoTo errmsg
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        ' Delete AccLink
        If PType = "IF" Then
            If Not db.Execute("Delete from AccLink Where Acclink.VoucherF=0 and Acclink.Type in ('IFIB','IFRB','IFIP','IFRP','IFPB','IFPP') and AccLink.PDate>'" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and AccLink.PDate<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        Else
            If Not db.Execute("Delete from AccLink Where Acclink.VoucherF=0 and Acclink.Type='" & PType & "' and AccLink.PDate>'" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and AccLink.PDate<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'") Then GoTo errmsg
        End If

        ' Undo Process 
        Select Case PType
            Case "IF"   ' IFRS Monthly Process
                If Not Me.Direct_Call_Process_Execute("ProcessIFRS.NetV3.Proclib", "IFRS_UNDOPROCESS", PDate) Then
                    Throw New Exception(Me.ErrDesc)
                End If
        End Select

        ' Delete Log
        If Not db.Execute("Delete from AccLog Where Type='" & PType & "' and Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        Delete_AccLink = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Delete_AccLink = False
    End Function
    Private Function Cancel_AccLink_Memorial(ByVal Voucher As String) As Boolean

        Dim rs_Journal As RemoteSet
        Dim Source As String
        Dim Account As String
        Dim vCurrency As String
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim nDebit_OC As Double
        Dim nCredit_OC As Double
        Dim nDebit_RC As Double
        Dim nCredit_RC As Double
        Dim CT As String

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.Execute("Update ACCLINK Set VoucherF=0 Where Voucher='" & Voucher & "' and VoucherF=1") Then GoTo errmsg
        If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then GoTo errmsg

        rs_Journal = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select V.Source, J.* from mJournal J, mVoucher V where V.Voucher=J.Voucher and V.Voucher='" & Voucher & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
        Do While Not rs_Journal.Eof
            Source = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Source"))
            Account = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Account"))
            vCurrency = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("Currency"))
            nDebit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Debit"))
            nCredit = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Credit"))
            nDebit_OC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Debit_OC"))
            nCredit_OC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Credit_OC"))
            nDebit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Debit_RC"))
            nCredit_RC = toval(rs_Journal.fields("Credit_RC"))
            CT = tostr(rs_Journal.fields("CT"))

            'Updating Journal_Account
            If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, Account, vCurrency, CT, -nDebit, -nCredit, -nDebit_OC, -nCredit_OC, -nDebit_RC, -nCredit_RC, StrMsg) Then GoTo errmsg

            rs_Journal.MoveNext()
        Loop
        If Not db.Execute("Delete from mVoucher where Voucher='" & Voucher & "' and Status='U'") Then GoTo errmsg
        If db.RecordsAffected = 0 Then
            StrMsg = "Please Check Voucher. " & Voucher & " Status !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        Cancel_AccLink_Memorial = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Cancel_AccLink_Memorial = False
    End Function
    Private Function Process_Admlink(ByVal PType As String, ByVal PDate As Date, Optional ByVal PEDate As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param1 As Object = Nothing) As Boolean
        Try
            Dim EDate As Date
            Dim SQL As String = ""
            Dim AdmNo As Long
            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            Dim rsAdm As RemoteSet

            ' Process Administration Link Must Create Admlink
            If Not Create_Admlink_Voucher_All(PDate) Then
                Throw New Exception("Can't Create Admlink Voucher ! " & StrMsg)
            End If

            ' Save to Log
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from AdmLog Where Type='" & PType & "' and Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            If Not rs.Eof Then
                Throw New Exception("Administration Log for Type : " & PType & ", Date : " & Format(PDate, "d") & " Already exists !")
            End If
            rs.AddNew()
            rs.fields("Type") = PType
            rs.fields("Date") = PDate
            rs.fields("db_User") = db_user
            rs.fields("PDate") = PDate
            rs.fields("PEDate") = todate(PEDate)
            rs.fields("PTime") = Time()
            If Not rs.Update Then Throw New Exception(rs.ErrDesc)

            EDate = PDate
            If PEDate Is Nothing Then
                PEDate = Last_date_of_period(DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, -1, PDate), 1)
            End If
            Select Case PType.ToUpper
                Case "FUND HELD"
                    SQL = "SELECT * FROM TREATY_FUNDHELD_PROCESS('" & PType & "','" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "','" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'," & tovalSF(Param1) & ")"
            End Select
            If SQL = "" Then
                Throw New Exception("No Process type !")
            End If
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL)
            rsAdm = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from Admlink Where 1<>1")
            Do While Not rs.Eof
                rsAdm.AddNew()
                AdmNo = db.GetIdentity("AdmLink") + 1
                rsAdm.fields("AdmNo") = AdmNo
                rsAdm.fields("BAdmNo") = rs.fields("AdmNo")
                rsAdm.fields("DocNo") = rs.fields("DocNo")
                rsAdm.fields("RefNo") = rs.fields("RefNo")
                rsAdm.fields("Branch") = rs.fields("Branch")
                rsAdm.fields("CT") = rs.fields("CT")
                rsAdm.fields("Remarks") = rs.fields("Remarks")
                rsAdm.fields("ID") = rs.fields("ID")
                rsAdm.fields("A_ID") = rs.fields("A_ID")
                rsAdm.fields("Source") = rs.fields("Source")
                rsAdm.fields("ISType") = rs.fields("ISType")
                rsAdm.fields("Segment") = rs.fields("Segment")
                rsAdm.fields("MSCode") = rs.fields("MSCode")
                rsAdm.fields("Type") = rs.fields("Type")
                rsAdm.fields("Code") = rs.fields("Code")
                rsAdm.fields("Currency") = rs.fields("Currency")
                rsAdm.fields("Date") = PDate
                rsAdm.fields("PDate") = PDate
                rsAdm.fields("BillDate") = PDate
                rsAdm.fields("WPC") = 0
                rsAdm.fields("A_PolicyNo") = rs.fields("A_PolicyNo")
                rsAdm.fields("A_MO") = rs.fields("A_MO")
                rsAdm.fields("RefType") = rs.fields("RefType")
                rsAdm.fields("AccountNo") = rs.fields("AccountNo")
                rsAdm.fields("BankAccount") = rs.fields("BankAccount")
                rsAdm.fields("BankName") = rs.fields("BankName")
                rsAdm.fields("Bank") = rs.fields("Bank")
                rsAdm.fields("BankBranch") = rs.fields("BankBranch")
                rsAdm.fields("A_SDate") = todate(rs.fields("A_SDate"))
                rsAdm.fields("A_EDate") = todate(rs.fields("A_EDate"))
                rsAdm.fields("Statement") = toval(rs.fields("Statement"))
                rsAdm.fields("ASource") = rs.fields("ASource")
                rsAdm.fields("BizMapID") = rs.fields("BizMapID")
                rsAdm.fields("TaxType") = rs.fields("TaxType")
                rsAdm.fields("PNominal_Tax") = toval(rs.fields("PNominal_Tax"))
                rsAdm.fields("PNominal_VAT") = toval(rs.fields("PNominal_VAT"))
                rsAdm.fields("IRate") = toval(rs.fields("IRate"))

                ' Extra Field
                rsAdm.fields("Extra_1") = PType
                ' Premium Subject
                rsAdm.fields("Subject") = "Premium"
                For i = 1 To 20
                    rsAdm.fields("Amount_" & i) = toval(rs.fields("Amount_" & i))
                Next

                Dim TAmount As Double
                TAmount = toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_1")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_2")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_3")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_4")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_5")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_6")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_7")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_8")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_9")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_10")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_15")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_16")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_19")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_20"))
                rsAdm.fields("Amount_11") = RoundCCY(TAmount, rsAdm.fields("Currency")) - TAmount

                rsAdm.fields("Amount") = toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_1")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_2")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_3")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_4")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_5")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_6")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_7")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_8")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_9")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_10")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_11")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_12")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_13")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_14")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_15")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_16")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_17")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_18")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_19")) + toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount_20"))

                rsAdm.fields("LAmount") = 0
                'Instalment Amount
                rsAdm.fields("IAmount") = toval(rsAdm.fields("Amount")) - toval(rsAdm.fields("LAmount"))
                rsAdm.fields("VoucherF") = 0
                rsAdm.fields("LUserID") = db_user
                rsAdm.fields("LDate") = Today.Date
                rsAdm.fields("LTime") = Time()
                rsAdm.fields("WriteF") = False

                If Not rsAdm.Update Then
                    StrMsg = rsAdm.ErrDesc
                    Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                End If
                If Me.Direct_Call_Process_Execute("Process.NetV3.Proclib", "Create_Voucher", AdmNo, False) = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "Can't Create Voucher ! " & Me.ErrDesc
                    Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                End If

                rs.MoveNext()
            Loop
            Process_Admlink = True
        Catch ex As Exception
            StrMsg = ex.Message
            Process_Admlink = False
        End Try
    End Function
    Private Function Delete_Admlink(ByVal PType As String, ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim PEDate As Date
        On Error GoTo errmsg

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from AdmLog Where Type='" & PType & "' and Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
        If rs.Eof Then
            StrMsg = "Administration Log Not Found !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        PEDate = rs.fields("PEDate")

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Distinct Voucher.Voucher from nVoucher Voucher, Admlink Where Voucher.Voucher=Admlink.Voucher and Admlink.VoucherF=1 and Admlink.Extra_1='" & PType & "' and Admlink.PDate>'" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Admlink.PDate<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")

        Do While Not rs.Eof
            If Not Me.Direct_Call_Process_Execute("Process.NetV3.Proclib", "Cancel_Voucher", rs.fields("Voucher"), False) Then Throw New Exception(Me.ErrDesc)
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop

        ' Delete AccLink
        If Not db.Execute("Delete from Admlink Where Admlink.VoucherF=0 and Admlink.Extra_1='" & PType & "' and Admlink.PDate>'" & Format(PEDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Admlink.PDate<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'") Then GoTo errmsg


        ' Delete Log
        If Not db.Execute("Delete from AdmLog Where Type='" & PType & "' and Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'") Then GoTo errmsg

        Delete_Admlink = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Delete_Admlink = False
    End Function
    Private Function Check_Backdated(ByVal Branch As String, ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Br_End_Date As Date
        Dim Br_Month As Integer
        Dim br_Year As Integer
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select End_Date, Month, Year from Branch where branch='" & Branch & "'")
        Br_End_Date = todate(rs.fields("End_Date"))
        Br_Month = toval(rs.fields("Month"))
        br_Year = toval(rs.fields("Year"))

        If (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, Br_End_Date) = 0 And Br_Month = Month(PDate) And br_Year = Year(PDate)) Or (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, Br_End_Date) > 0) Then
            Check_Backdated = True
            Exit Function
        End If
        Check_Backdated = False
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Check_Backdated = True
    End Function

    Private Function Create_Amortization_Memorial(ByVal AID As String, ByVal PDate As String, ByVal TType As String, Optional ByVal Rate As Double = 0, Optional ByVal Rate_RC As Double = 0) As String
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Amount(20) As Double
        Dim Nominal As Double
        Dim Diff As Double
        Dim bufferdata As Object

        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Nominal = 0
        Diff = 0
        For i = 1 To 20
            Amount(i) = 0
        Next i

        Dim SL_Cat As String
        Dim Ccy As String
        Dim CT As String
        Dim Branch As String
        Dim RefNo As String
        Dim DocNo As String
        Dim Vat As Double
        Dim TAX As Double
        Dim VoucherDate As Date
        Dim BillDate As Date
        Dim Source As String
        Dim VType As String
        Dim Remarks As String
        Dim Voucher As String

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select A1.Description, A1.Branch, A2.*, A3.VTRegister, A3.VTAmortization, A3.VTDisposal from AmortizationH A1, AmortizationD A2, AmorType A3 where A3.AmorType=A1.AmorType and A1.AID=A2.AID and A2.AID='" & AID & "' and A2.Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and A2.Type='" & TType & "' and A2.VoucherF=0")
        If Not rs.Eof Then
            ' Initialize BufferData
            ReDim bufferdata(9, MaxJournalEntries - 1)
            For i = 0 To MaxJournalEntries - 1
                bufferdata(0, i) = ""   'Account
                bufferdata(1, i) = ""   'Description
                bufferdata(2, i) = ""   'Currency
                bufferdata(3, i) = 0    'Debit_OC
                bufferdata(4, i) = 0    'Credit_OC
                bufferdata(5, i) = 0    'Debit_BC
                bufferdata(6, i) = 0    'Credit_BC
                bufferdata(7, i) = ""    'CT
                bufferdata(8, i) = 0    'Debit_RC
                bufferdata(9, i) = 0    'Credit_RC
            Next i

            For i = 1 To 20
                Amount(i) = toval(rs.fields("Amount_" & i))
                If Amount(i) > 0 Then
                    Nominal = Nominal + Amount(i)
                Else
                    Diff = Diff - Amount(i)
                End If
            Next i
        End If

        Voucher = tostr(rs.fields("Voucher"))
        Ccy = tostr(rs.fields("Currency"))
        CT = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
        Branch = tostr(rs.fields("Branch"))
        RefNo = tostr(rs.fields("RefNo"))
        DocNo = tostr(rs.fields("DocNo"))
        Vat = toval(Amount(13))
        TAX = toval(Amount(14))
        VoucherDate = todate(rs.fields("Date"))
        BillDate = todate(rs.fields("Date"))
        Remarks = tostr(rs.fields("Description"))

        SL_Cat = "M"

        Select Case TType
            Case "Register"
                VType = rs.fields("VTRegister")
            Case "Amortization"
                VType = rs.fields("VTAmortization")
            Case "Disposal"
                VType = rs.fields("VTDisposal")
        End Select
        rs.CloseRemoteSet()

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("SELECT VType.vType, VType.Description, AutoNum, DebtorF, CreditorF, sAutoNum From VType WHERE VType='" & VType & "'")

        If rs.Eof Then GoTo errmsg
        Dim CreditorF As Boolean
        Dim DebtorF As Boolean
        Dim AutoNum As String
        Dim sAutoNum As String

        VType = tostr(rs.fields("VType"))
        DebtorF = toval(rs.fields("DebtorF"))
        CreditorF = toval(rs.fields("CreditorF"))
        AutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("AutoNum"))
        sAutoNum = tostr(rs.fields("sAutoNum"))
        If Remarks = "" Then
            Remarks = tostr(rs.fields("Description"))
        Else
            Remarks = Remarks + " - " + tostr(rs.fields("Description"))
        End If

        If Rate = 0 Then Rate = exchange_rate(Ccy, VoucherDate, br_currency)
        If Rate = 0 Then
            StrMsg = "No Exchange Rate for Currency " & Ccy & " to Base Currency !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        If br_currency_1 <> "" AndAlso br_currency_1.ToUpper <> br_currency.ToUpper Then
            If Rate_RC = 0 Then Rate_RC = exchange_rate_RC(Ccy, VoucherDate, br_currency_1)
            If Rate_RC = 0 Then
                StrMsg = "No Exchange Rate for Currency " & Ccy & " to 2nd Base Currency !"
                GoTo errmsg
            End If
        Else
            Rate_RC = Rate
        End If

        If Check_Backdated(Branch, VoucherDate) Then
            StrMsg = "Backdated Transaction, Please Check Posting/Closing Date for Branch '" & Branch & "' !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        Dim DataMaster As RemoteSet
        DataMaster = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mVoucher Where 1<>1")
        DataMaster.AddNew()
        If Voucher = "" Then Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, VoucherDate, Branch)
        If Voucher = "" Then
            StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        DataMaster.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
        DataMaster.fields("VType") = VType
        DataMaster.fields("Date") = VoucherDate
        DataMaster.fields("PDate") = VoucherDate
        DataMaster.fields("Rate") = Rate
        DataMaster.fields("Rate_RC") = Rate_RC
        DataMaster.fields("DueDate") = BillDate
        DataMaster.fields("CT") = CT
        DataMaster.fields("CreditorF") = toval(CreditorF)
        DataMaster.fields("DebtorF") = toval(DebtorF)
        DataMaster.fields("SL_Flag") = True
        DataMaster.fields("Status") = "U"
        DataMaster.fields("Currency") = Ccy
        DataMaster.fields("Nominal_CC") = Nominal
        DataMaster.fields("Diff_CC") = Diff
        DataMaster.fields("Nominal") = Nominal * Rate
        DataMaster.fields("Diff") = Diff * Rate
        DataMaster.fields("Branch") = Branch
        DataMaster.fields("SL_Cat") = SL_Cat
        DataMaster.fields("RefNo") = RefNo
        DataMaster.fields("DocNo") = DocNo
        DataMaster.fields("Remarks") = Remarks
        DataMaster.fields("NDueDate") = todate(DataMaster.fields("DueDate"))
        DataMaster.fields("CDate") = Today.Date
        DataMaster.fields("CTime") = Time()
        DataMaster.fields("UserID") = db_user
        DataMaster.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
        DataMaster.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
        DataMaster.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
        DataMaster.fields("nPayment") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("Payment_CC") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("Payment_OC") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("Posting_Flag") = True
        DataMaster.fields("Posting_Time") = Time()
        DataMaster.fields("Posting_Date") = Today.Date
        DataMaster.fields("Posting_Opr") = db_user
        DataMaster.fields("TaxPayment_CC") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("TaxPayment_OC") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("Num_Doc") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("SL_Tag") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("AssetF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("InvestmentF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("SourceF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("BranchF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("ChequeF") = 0
        DataMaster.fields("BackDatedF") = 0
        Source = Get_Source_Doc(Branch, "GJ", RefNo, "", "", "", SL_Cat, DataMaster.fields("Date"), sAutoNum, "")
        If Source = "" Then
            StrMsg = "Generate Journal Source No. Failed !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        DataMaster.fields("Source") = Source
        If Not DataMaster.Update() Then GoTo errmsg

        i = 0
        Dim rsV As RemoteSet
        Dim sAccount As String
        Dim Description As String
        Dim sCT As String
        Dim sCurrency As String
        Dim nDebit As Double
        Dim nCredit As Double
        Dim j As Integer
        rsV = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select vPattern.*, Account.Description, Account.Monetary_Flag from vPattern, Account Where Account.Account=vPattern.Account and vPattern.VType='" & VType & "'")
        Do While Not rsV.Eof
            sAccount = tostr(rsV.fields("Account"))
            Description = tostr(rsV.fields("Description"))
            If rsV.fields("Monetary_Flag") Then
                sCurrency = Ccy
                If toval(rsV.fields("Debit")) <> 0 Then
                    nDebit = Amount(Abs(toval(rsV.fields("Debit"))))
                    nCredit = 0
                End If

                If toval(rsV.fields("Credit")) <> 0 Then
                    nDebit = 0
                    nCredit = Amount(Abs(toval(rsV.fields("Credit"))))
                End If
            Else
                sCurrency = br_currency
                If toval(rsV.fields("Debit")) <> 0 Then
                    nDebit = Amount(Abs(toval(rsV.fields("Debit")))) * Rate
                    nCredit = 0
                End If

                If toval(rsV.fields("Credit")) <> 0 Then
                    nDebit = 0
                    nCredit = Amount(Abs(toval(rsV.fields("Credit")))) * Rate
                End If
            End If
            If nDebit < 0 Then
                nCredit = -nDebit
                nDebit = 0
            ElseIf nCredit < 0 Then
                nDebit = -nCredit
                nCredit = 0
            End If
            sCT = CT

            j = Find_3_Element(bufferdata, 0, i - 1, 0, sAccount, 2, sCurrency, 7, sCT)
            If j < i Then
                ' Combine for the same account
                i = i - 1
            Else
                bufferdata(0, j) = sAccount
                bufferdata(1, j) = Description
                bufferdata(2, j) = sCurrency
                bufferdata(3, j) = 0
                bufferdata(4, j) = 0
                bufferdata(5, j) = 0
                bufferdata(6, j) = 0
                bufferdata(7, j) = sCT
                bufferdata(8, j) = 0
                bufferdata(9, j) = 0
            End If

            bufferdata(3, j) = bufferdata(3, j) + nDebit
            bufferdata(4, j) = bufferdata(4, j) + nCredit
            If bufferdata(3, j) > bufferdata(4, j) Then
                bufferdata(3, j) = bufferdata(3, j) - bufferdata(4, j)
                nDebit = nDebit - bufferdata(4, j)
                nCredit = nCredit - bufferdata(4, j)
                bufferdata(4, j) = 0
            Else
                bufferdata(4, j) = bufferdata(4, j) - bufferdata(3, j)
                nDebit = nDebit - bufferdata(3, j)
                nCredit = nCredit - bufferdata(3, j)
                bufferdata(3, j) = 0
            End If

            If sCurrency = br_currency Then
                bufferdata(5, j) = bufferdata(3, j)
                bufferdata(6, j) = bufferdata(4, j)
                bufferdata(8, j) = bufferdata(3, j) * Rate_RC / Rate
                bufferdata(9, j) = bufferdata(4, j) * Rate_RC / Rate
            Else
                bufferdata(5, j) = bufferdata(3, j) * Rate
                bufferdata(6, j) = bufferdata(4, j) * Rate
                bufferdata(8, j) = bufferdata(3, j) * Rate_RC
                bufferdata(9, j) = bufferdata(4, j) * Rate_RC
            End If
            i = i + 1
            rsV.MoveNext()
        Loop

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from mJournal where 1<>1")
        Dim MaxRows As Integer = CType(bufferdata, Object(,)).GetUpperBound(1) + 1
        For i = 0 To MaxRows - 1
            If Trim(bufferdata(0, i)) <> "" And Trim(bufferdata(2, i)) <> "" And (toval(bufferdata(5, i)) <> 0 Or toval(bufferdata(6, i)) <> 0 Or toval(bufferdata(8, i)) <> 0 Or toval(bufferdata(9, i)) <> 0) Then
                rs.AddNew()
                rs.fields("Voucher") = tostr(Voucher)
                rs.fields("SortNo") = i
                rs.fields("Account") = tostr(bufferdata(0, i))
                rs.fields("Description") = tostr(bufferdata(1, i))
                rs.fields("Currency") = tostr(bufferdata(2, i))

                If Not CalculationRoundingF Then
                    rs.fields("Debit_OC") = toval(bufferdata(3, i))
                    rs.fields("Credit_OC") = toval(bufferdata(4, i))
                    rs.fields("Debit") = toval(bufferdata(5, i))
                    rs.fields("Credit") = toval(bufferdata(6, i))
                    rs.fields("Debit_RC") = toval(bufferdata(8, i))
                    rs.fields("Credit_RC") = toval(bufferdata(9, i))
                Else
                    rs.fields("Debit_OC") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(3, i)), UCase(tostr(bufferdata(2, i))), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Credit_OC") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(4, i)), UCase(tostr(bufferdata(2, i))), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Debit") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(5, i)), br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Credit") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(6, i)), br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Debit_RC") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(8, i)), br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                    rs.fields("Credit_RC") = RoundCCY(toval(bufferdata(9, i)), br_currency_1, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                End If

                rs.fields("CT") = tostr(bufferdata(7, i))
                rs.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
                rs.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                rs.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                If Not rs.UpdateRow Then GoTo errmsg

                'Updating Journal_Account
                If Not Posting_to_Journal_Account(Source, rs.fields("Account"), rs.fields("Currency"), rs.fields("CT"), rs.fields("Debit"), rs.fields("Credit"), rs.fields("Debit_OC"), rs.fields("Credit_OC"), rs.fields("Debit_RC"), rs.fields("Credit_RC"), StrMsg) Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
        Next i
        If Not rs.Eof Then
            If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        End If
        rs.CloseRemoteSet()

        Create_Amortization_Memorial = Voucher
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Create_Amortization_Memorial = ""
    End Function

    Private Function Amortization_Process(ByVal br_branch As String, ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean 'F&A
        Dim rsAmorH As RemoteSet
        Dim rsAmorD As RemoteSet
        Dim Voucher As String
        Dim Amount As Double

        On Error GoTo errmsg

        If Not db.GetLockedObject("AMORTIZATION" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Acquire Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If

        rsAmorH = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from AmortizationH A1 Where A1.Status='R' and A1.Branch='" & br_branch & "' and A1.Booked<'" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
        If Not rsAmorH.Eof Then
            rsAmorD = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from AmortizationD Where 1<>1")
        End If
        Do While Not rsAmorH.Eof
            Amount = Calc_Amor(rsAmorH.fields("Amount"), rsAmorH.fields("Accumulation"), rsAmorH.fields("SDate"), rsAmorH.fields("EDate"), PDate)
            If Amount <> 0 Then
                rsAmorD.AddNew()
                rsAmorD.fields("AID") = rsAmorH.fields("AID")
                rsAmorD.fields("Type") = "Amortization"
                rsAmorD.fields("Date") = PDate
                rsAmorD.fields("Currency") = rsAmorH.fields("Currency")
                rsAmorD.fields("CT") = rsAmorH.fields("CT")
                rsAmorD.fields("Amount") = Amount
                rsAmorD.fields("RefNo") = rsAmorH.fields("RefNo")
                rsAmorD.fields("RefDate") = rsAmorH.fields("Booked")
                rsAmorD.fields("Amount_1") = rsAmorD.fields("Amount") / rsAmorH.fields("Amount") * rsAmorH.fields("Amount_1")
                rsAmorD.fields("Amount_2") = rsAmorD.fields("Amount") / rsAmorH.fields("Amount") * rsAmorH.fields("Amount_2")
                rsAmorD.fields("Amount_3") = rsAmorD.fields("Amount") / rsAmorH.fields("Amount") * rsAmorH.fields("Amount_3")
                rsAmorD.fields("Amount_4") = rsAmorD.fields("Amount") / rsAmorH.fields("Amount") * rsAmorH.fields("Amount_4")
                rsAmorD.fields("Amount_5") = rsAmorD.fields("Amount") / rsAmorH.fields("Amount") * rsAmorH.fields("Amount_5")
                rsAmorD.fields("Last_Update") = Today.Date
                rsAmorD.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                rsAmorD.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user
                rsAmorD.fields("VoucherF") = 0
                If Not rsAmorD.Update Then GoTo errmsg
                Voucher = Create_Amortization_Memorial(rsAmorH.fields("AID"), PDate, rsAmorD.fields("Type"))
                If Voucher = "" Then GoTo errmsg
                rsAmorD.Edit()

                rsAmorD.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                rsAmorD.fields("VoucherF") = 1
                If Not rsAmorD.Update Then GoTo errmsg

                rsAmorH.Edit()
                rsAmorH.fields("RefNo") = Voucher
                rsAmorH.fields("Booked") = PDate
                rsAmorH.fields("Accumulation") = toval(rsAmorH.fields("Accumulation")) + Amount
                rsAmorH.fields("BookValue") = toval(rsAmorH.fields("Amount")) - toval(rsAmorH.fields("Accumulation"))
                If Not rsAmorH.Update Then GoTo errmsg
            End If
            rsAmorH.MoveNext()
        Loop

        If Not db.ReleaseLockedObject("AMORTIZATION" & br_branch) Then
            StrMsg = "Failed To Release Locked Process !"
            GoTo errmsg
        End If
        Amortization_Process = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        If db.CheckLockedObject("AMORTIZATION" & br_branch) Then db.ReleaseLockedObject("AMORTIZATION" & br_branch)
        Amortization_Process = False
    End Function

    Private Function Amortization_Process_All(ByVal PDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rsBranch As RemoteSet
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, End_Date, Month, Year from Branch Where OP_Flag=1 and Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
        Do While Not rsBranch.Eof

            If (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, rsBranch.fields("End_Date")) = 0 And rsBranch.fields("Month") = Month(PDate) And rsBranch.fields("Year") = Year(PDate)) Or (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, rsBranch.fields("End_Date")) > 0) Then
                StrMsg = StrMsg & "Branch : '" & rsBranch.fields("Branch") & "' Closing Month & Ending Date Constraint !" & vbCrLf
                GoTo errmsg
            Else
                If Not Amortization_Process(rsBranch.fields("Branch"), PDate) Then
                    GoTo errmsg
                End If
            End If
            rsBranch.MoveNext()
        Loop
        Amortization_Process_All = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Amortization_Process_All = False
    End Function

    Private Function Calc_Amor(ByVal Nominal As Double, ByVal Accumulation As Double, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date, ByVal PDate As Date) As Double
        Dim Amount As Double

        If SDate.Day > 15 Then
            ' Skip One Month
            SDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, 1, SDate)
            EDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Month, 1, EDate)
        End If
        PDate = DateAdd(DateInterval.Day, 1, PDate)

        If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, SDate, PDate) < 0 Then
            PDate = SDate
        End If
        If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, SDate, EDate) < 0 Then
            EDate = SDate
        End If
        If DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, EDate, PDate) > 0 Then
            PDate = EDate
        End If

        If (DateDiff(DateInterval.Month, SDate, EDate)) <> 0 Then
            Amount = (DateDiff(DateInterval.Month, SDate, PDate)) / (DateDiff(DateInterval.Month, SDate, EDate)) * Nominal
        Else
            Amount = Nominal
        End If

        If Amount < Accumulation Then
            Calc_Amor = 0
        Else
            Calc_Amor = Amount - Accumulation
        End If
    End Function
    Private Function Get_Sub_Branch(ByVal Parent As String) As String
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Branch As String
        Dim SubBranch As String

        Branch = "'" & Parent & "'"

        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, OP_Flag from Branch Where Parent='" & Parent & "'")
        Do While Not rs.Eof
            If toval(rs.fields("Op_Flag")) Then
                SubBranch = "'" & tostr(rs.fields("Branch")) & "'"
            Else
                SubBranch = Get_Sub_Branch(tostr(rs.fields("Branch")))
            End If
            If Branch = "" Then
                Branch = SubBranch
            Else
                Branch = Branch & "," & SubBranch
            End If
            rs.MoveNext()
        Loop
        Get_Sub_Branch = Branch
    End Function
    Private Function Create_WithHoldingTax_All(ByVal PBranch As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal PaidF As Boolean, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date, Optional ByVal pTaxType As String = "")
        Try
            Dim NumThreads As Integer = toval(GetAppSetting("Process_FA_NumThreads"))
            If NumThreads <= 0 Then NumThreads = 1
            Dim newDB As ClassLib
            Dim rs As RemoteSet
            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch.Branch, TaxType.TaxType, VType.VType from Branch, VType, TaxType where Branch.Op_Flag=1 and Branch.Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and TaxType.TaxType=VType.TaxType and VType.CreditorF=1 and VType.SL_Cat='N' and VType.TaxF=1 and Branch.Branch in (" & Get_Sub_Branch(PBranch) & ") and (TaxType.TaxType='" & pTaxType & "' or '" & pTaxType & "'='')")
            Dim i As Long
            Dim AllOk As Boolean = True
            Dim NumDb As Integer = 0

            Do While True
                Dim arrResult(NumThreads - 1) As IAsyncResult
                Dim arrCaller(NumThreads - 1) As Process_WithHoldingTax_Delegate
                Dim arrdb(NumThreads - 1) As ClassLib

                For i = 0 To NumThreads - 1
                    arrCaller(i) = Nothing
                    arrResult(i) = Nothing
                Next

                For i = 0 To NumThreads - 1
                    newDB = New ClassLib
                    If Not newDB.OpenConnection("", db_Name, db_Server, db_sysuser, db_syspassword, db_TimeOut, db_Trusted) Then
                        Throw New Exception(newDB.ErrDesc)
                    End If
                    newDB.Var_db_user = db_user
                    newDB.Var_db_Password = db_password
                    newDB.Var_db_User_Type = db_user_type
                    newDB.Begintrans()

                    Save_Log_Process(newDB, "Create_WithHoldingTax_All", PDate, rs.fields("Branch"))

                    arrCaller(i) = New Process_WithHoldingTax_Delegate(AddressOf Process_WithholdingTax)
                    arrResult(i) = arrCaller(i).BeginInvoke(newDB, rs.fields("Branch"), PDate, PaidF, SDate, EDate, rs.fields("TaxType"), rs.fields("VType"), Nothing, Nothing)
                    arrResult(i).AsyncWaitHandle.WaitOne(0, False)
                    arrdb(i) = newDB

                    rs.MoveNext()
                    If rs.Eof Then Exit For
                Next

                Dim ExitLoop As Boolean = False
                Do While Not ExitLoop
                    For i = 0 To NumThreads - 1
                        If Not arrResult(i) Is Nothing Then
                            If arrResult(i).IsCompleted Then
                                ExitLoop = True
                            Else
                                ExitLoop = False
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Else
                            ExitLoop = True
                        End If
                    Next
                    If Not ExitLoop Then System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(60000)
                Loop

                For i = 0 To NumThreads - 1
                    If Not arrCaller(i) Is Nothing Then
                        If Not arrCaller(i).EndInvoke(arrResult(i)) Then
                            AllOk = False
                            arrdb(i).RollBack()
                        Else
                            arrdb(i).Committrans()
                        End If
                    End If
                Next

                If Not AllOk Then Exit Do

                If rs.Eof Then Exit Do
            Loop

            If Not AllOk Then Throw New Exception(StrMsg)

            Create_WithHoldingTax_All = True
        Catch ex As Exception
            StrMsg = ex.Message
            Create_WithHoldingTax_All = False
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Delegate Function Process_WithHoldingTax_Delegate(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal PBranch As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal PaidF As Boolean, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date, ByVal pTaxType As String, ByVal pVType As String) As Boolean
    Private Function Process_WithholdingTax(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal PBranch As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal PaidF As Boolean, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date, ByVal pTaxType As String, ByVal pVType As String) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Voucher As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_SL As RemoteSet
        Dim rsA As RemoteSet

        Dim PVoucher As String
        Dim PID As String       ' Proacc ID
        Dim PSubject As String
        Dim Num_Doc As Integer
        Dim PNominal As Double
        Dim PNominalNA As Double
        Dim Nominal_CC As Double
        Dim Nominal_OC As Double
        Dim PNominal_Tax As Double
        Dim Diff_CC As Double
        Dim TAX As Double
        Dim SQL As String
        Dim TaxPayment_CC As Double
        Dim AdjustmentF As Boolean
        Dim rsBranch As RemoteSet
        Dim VType As String
        Dim rsVType As RemoteSet
        Dim Code As String
        Dim CodeExt As String
        Dim AutoNum As String
        Dim A_Hint As Integer
        Dim A_Account As String
        Dim Branch As String
        Dim TaxType As String
