        Dim Voucher As String
        Dim MTaxType As String
        Dim TaxPCT As Double
        Dim TaxRate As Double
        Dim Tax_CC As Double
        Dim PType As String
        Dim br_Start_Date As Date
        Dim Rate As Double
        Dim AccruedF As Boolean
        Dim ProAcc As String

        Try
            If PBranch <> "" Then
                rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, Start_Date, End_Date, Month, Year, KPPID from Branch Where Branch='" & PBranch & "' and OP_Flag=1 and Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            Else
                rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, Start_Date, End_Date, Month, Year, KPPID from Branch Where OP_Flag=1 and Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            End If

            Do While Not rsBranch.Eof
                Branch = rsBranch.fields("Branch")

                If tostr(rsBranch.fields("KPPID")) <> "" Then
                    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select ProAcc from ProAcc where ID='" & tostr(rsBranch.fields("KPPID")) & "' and Currency='" & br_currency & "'")
                    ProAcc = rs.fields("ProAcc")
                Else
                    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select ProAcc from ProAcc where Description like '%kantor pajak%' and Currency='" & br_currency & "'")
                    ProAcc = rs.fields("ProAcc")
                End If
                If ProAcc = "" Then
                    StrMsg = "No Tax Office Profile !"
                    Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                End If

                br_Start_Date = rsBranch.fields("Start_Date")
                If (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, rsBranch.fields("End_Date")) = 0 And rsBranch.fields("Month") = Month(PDate) And rsBranch.fields("Year") = Year(PDate)) Or (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, rsBranch.fields("End_Date")) > 0) Then
                    StrMsg = StrMsg & "Branch : '" & Branch & "' Closing Month & Ending Date Constraint !" & vbCrLf
                    Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                End If
                If pTaxType <> "" Then
                    If pVType <> "" Then
                        rsVType = db.OpenRemoteSet("select VType.Vtype, VType.TaxType, VType.Code, VType.CodeExt, VType.AutoNum, TaxType.MTaxType, TaxType.TaxRate from VType, TaxType where TaxType.TaxType=VType.TaxType and VType.CreditorF=1 and VType.SL_Cat='N' and VType.TaxF=1 and VType.TaxType='" & pTaxType & "' and VType.VType='" & pVType & "'")
                    Else
                        rsVType = db.OpenRemoteSet("select VType.Vtype, VType.TaxType, VType.Code, VType.CodeExt, VType.AutoNum, TaxType.MTaxType, TaxType.TaxRate from VType, TaxType where TaxType.TaxType=VType.TaxType and VType.CreditorF=1 and VType.SL_Cat='N' and VType.TaxF=1 and VType.TaxType='" & pTaxType & "'")
                    End If
                Else
                    rsVType = db.OpenRemoteSet("select VType.Vtype, VType.TaxType, VType.Code, VType.CodeExt, VType.AutoNum, TaxType.MTaxType, TaxType.TaxRate from VType, TaxType where TaxType.TaxType=VType.TaxType and VType.CreditorF=1 and VType.SL_Cat='N' and VType.TaxF=1")
                End If
                Do While Not rsVType.Eof
                    VType = tostr(rsVType.fields("VType"))
                    TaxType = tostr(rsVType.fields("TaxType"))
                    MTaxType = tostr(rsVType.fields("MTaxType"))
                    Code = tostr(rsVType.fields("Code"))
                    CodeExt = tostr(rsVType.fields("CodeExt"))
                    AutoNum = tostr(rsVType.fields("AutoNum"))
                    A_Hint = 0
                    A_Account = ""

                    rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select top 1 nVoucher.Voucher from nVoucher, VType Where VType.VType=nVoucher.VType and VType.TaxF=1 and nVoucher.Branch='" & Branch & "' and nVoucher.VType='" & VType & "' and nVoucher.Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
                    If rs.Eof Then
                        Try
                            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 V.Credit as Hint, A.Account from VPattern V, Account A where A.Account=V.Account and A.Open_ItemF=1 and V.Vtype='" & VType & "' and V.Credit<>-14")
                            If rs.Eof Then
                                StrMsg = "No Adjustment Account for Withholding Tax Voucher Type !"
                                Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                            End If
                            A_Hint = toval(rs.fields("Hint"))
                            A_Account = tostr(rs.fields("Account"))

                            ' TaxType from Voucher
                            If PaidF Then ' Paid Only
                                SQL = " Select VoucherP.Voucher as PVoucher, Profile.ID, VoucherP.Last_Date, VoucherP.Last_Time, Max(dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(SL.Nominal_OC, VoucherP.SL_Cat, VoucherP.DebtorF, Voucher.DebtorF)) as Nominal_OC, Max(Voucher.Rate) as PRate,"
                                SQL = SQL & " Max(Case When Profile.TaxPCT=0 Then 100 Else Profile.TaxPCT end) as TaxPCT, "
                                SQL = SQL & " sum(Admlink.PNominal_Tax) as PNominal_Tax, Max(Admlink.Subject) as Subject, Max(Admlink.Type) as Type, "
                                SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Voucher, cast(Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as Int)) as Bit) as DebtorF, Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, Max(Voucher.CT) as CT"
                                SQL = SQL & " from"
                                SQL = SQL & " PVoucher VoucherP"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join pSL SL on (SL.Voucher=VoucherP.Voucher and SL.PaymentF=1)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join nVoucher Voucher on (Voucher.Voucher=SL.Doc_No and Voucher.TaxF=1 and Voucher.Branch='" & Branch & "' and Voucher.TaxType='" & tostr(TaxType) & "')"
                                If CodeExt <> "" Then
                                    SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Admlink on (Admlink.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Admlink.Subject='" & CodeExt & "' and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%')"
                                Else
                                    SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Admlink on (Admlink.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%')"
                                End If
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Profile on (Profile.ID=Admlink.ID)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Outer Apply (Select Top 1 nSL.Doc_no from nSL Inner Join nVoucher on (nVoucher.Voucher=nSL.Voucher and nVoucher.TaxF=1) Where nSL.Doc_No=Voucher.Voucher and nSL.pVoucher=VoucherP.Voucher and nSL.PaymentF=1 Group by nSL.Doc_No having (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)<>0) or (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)=0 and count(*)=1)) R"
                                SQL = SQL & " Where VoucherP.Status<>'N' and VoucherP.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and coalesce(R.Doc_No,'')=''"
                                SQL = SQL & " and not ('" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Between coalesce(Profile.TaxHSDate,'01/01/1900') and coalesce(Profile.TaxHEDate,'01/01/1900'))"
                                SQL = SQL & " Group By Profile.ID, VoucherP.Last_Date, VoucherP.Last_Time, VoucherP.Voucher, Voucher.Voucher"

                                SQL = SQL & " Union All "

                                SQL = SQL & " Select '-' as PVoucher, Profile.ID, Voucher.Last_Date, Voucher.Last_Time, sum(Admlink.Amount) as Nominal_OC, Max(Voucher.Rate) as PRate,"
                                SQL = SQL & " Max(Case When Profile.TaxPCT=0 Then 100 Else Profile.TaxPCT end) as TaxPCT, "
                                SQL = SQL & " sum(Admlink.PNominal_Tax) as PNominal_Tax, Max(Admlink.Subject) as Subject, Max(Admlink.Type) as Type, "
                                SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Voucher, cast(Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as Int)) as Bit) as DebtorF, Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, Max(Voucher.CT) as CT"
                                SQL = SQL & " from Admlink"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join nVoucher Voucher on (Voucher.Voucher=Admlink.Voucher and Voucher.TaxF=1 and Voucher.Branch='" & Branch & "' and Voucher.TaxType='" & tostr(TaxType) & "')"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Profile on (Profile.ID=Admlink.ID)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Outer Apply (Select Top 1 nSL.Doc_no from nSL Inner Join nVoucher on (nVoucher.Voucher=nSL.Voucher and nVoucher.TaxF=1) Where nSL.Doc_No=Voucher.Voucher and nSL.pVoucher='-' and nSL.PaymentF=1 Group by nSL.Doc_No having (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)<>0) or (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)=0 and count(*)=1)) R"
                                SQL = SQL & " Where Admlink.Subject<>'Others' and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%'"
                                If CodeExt <> "" Then
                                    SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Subject='" & CodeExt & "'"
                                End If
                                SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and coalesce(R.Doc_No,'')=''"
                                SQL = SQL & " and not ('" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Between coalesce(Profile.TaxHSDate,'01/01/1900') and coalesce(Profile.TaxHEDate,'01/01/1900'))"
                                SQL = SQL & " and round(Voucher.Nominal_CC-Voucher.Diff_CC,4)=0"
                                SQL = SQL & " Group By Profile.ID, Voucher.Last_Date, Voucher.Last_Time, Voucher.Voucher"

                                SQL = SQL & " Order By ID, Last_Date, Last_Time, pVoucher, Voucher"
                            Else

                                SQL = "Select '-' as PVoucher, Profile.ID, sum(Admlink.Amount) as Nominal_OC, Max(Voucher.Rate) as PRate,"
                                SQL = SQL & " Max(Case When Profile.TaxPCT=0 Then 100 Else Profile.TaxPCT end) as TaxPCT, "
                                SQL = SQL & " sum(Admlink.PNominal_Tax) as PNominal_Tax, Max(Admlink.Subject) as Subject, Max(Admlink.Type) as Type, "
                                SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Voucher, cast(Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as Int)) as Bit) as DebtorF, Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, Max(Voucher.CT) as CT"
                                SQL = SQL & " from Admlink"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join nVoucher Voucher on (Voucher.Voucher=Admlink.Voucher and Voucher.TaxF=1 and Voucher.Branch='" & Branch & "' and Voucher.TaxType='" & tostr(TaxType) & "')"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Profile on (Profile.ID=Admlink.ID)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Outer Apply (Select Top 1 nSL.Doc_no from nSL Inner Join nVoucher on (nVoucher.Voucher=nSL.Voucher and nVoucher.TaxF=1) Where nSL.Doc_No=Voucher.Voucher and nSL.pVoucher='-' and nSL.PaymentF=1 Group by nSL.Doc_No having (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)<>0) or (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)=0 and count(*)=1)) R"
                                SQL = SQL & " Where Admlink.Subject<>'Others' and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%'"
                                If CodeExt <> "" Then
                                    SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Subject='" & CodeExt & "'"
                                End If
                                SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and coalesce(R.Doc_No,'')=''"
                                SQL = SQL & " and not ('" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Between coalesce(Profile.TaxHSDate,'01/01/1900') and coalesce(Profile.TaxHEDate,'01/01/1900'))"
                                SQL = SQL & " Group By Profile.ID, Voucher.Voucher"

                                SQL = SQL & " Union All "

                                'Other Transaction always Paid Tax
                                SQL = SQL & " Select VoucherP.Voucher as PVoucher, Profile.ID, Max(dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(SL.Nominal_OC, VoucherP.SL_Cat, VoucherP.DebtorF, Voucher.DebtorF)) as Nominal_OC, Max(Voucher.Rate) as PRate,"
                                SQL = SQL & " Max(Case When Profile.TaxPCT=0 Then 100 Else Profile.TaxPCT end) as TaxPCT, "
                                SQL = SQL & " sum(Admlink.PNominal_Tax) as PNominal_Tax, Max(Admlink.Subject) as Subject, Max(Admlink.Type) as Type, "
                                SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Voucher, cast(Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as Int)) as Bit) as DebtorF, Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, Max(Voucher.CT) as CT"
                                SQL = SQL & " from"
                                SQL = SQL & " PVoucher VoucherP"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join pSL SL on (SL.Voucher=VoucherP.Voucher and SL.PaymentF=1)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join nVoucher Voucher on (Voucher.Voucher=SL.Doc_No and Voucher.TaxF=1 and Voucher.Branch='" & Branch & "' and Voucher.TaxType='" & tostr(TaxType) & "')"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Admlink on (Admlink.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and (Admlink.Subject='" & CodeExt & "' or '" & CodeExt & "'='') and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%')"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Profile on (Profile.ID=Admlink.ID)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Outer Apply (Select Top 1 nSL.Doc_no from nSL Inner Join nVoucher on (nVoucher.Voucher=nSL.Voucher and nVoucher.TaxF=1) Where nSL.Doc_No=Voucher.Voucher and nSL.pVoucher=VoucherP.Voucher and nSL.PaymentF=1 Group by nSL.Doc_No having (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)<>0) or (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)=0 and count(*)=1)) R"
                                SQL = SQL & " Where VoucherP.Status<>'N' and VoucherP.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and coalesce(R.Doc_No,'')=''"
                                SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Subject='Others'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and not ('" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Between coalesce(Profile.TaxHSDate,'01/01/1900') and coalesce(Profile.TaxHEDate,'01/01/1900'))"
                                SQL = SQL & " Group By Profile.ID, VoucherP.Voucher, Voucher.Voucher"
                                SQL = SQL & " Order by ID, pVoucher, Voucher"
                            End If
                            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL, adOpenForwardonly)

                            PVoucher = ""
                            PID = ""

                            Do While Not rs.Eof
                                PVoucher = tostr(rs.fields("PVoucher"))
                                PID = tostr(rs.fields("ID"))
                                PSubject = tostr(rs.fields("Subject"))
                                PType = tostr(rs.fields("Type"))
                                rs_Voucher = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from nVoucher Where 1<>1")
                                rs_Voucher.AddNew()
                                Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, PDate, Branch)
                                If Voucher = "" Then
                                    StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                                    Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                                End If
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                                rs_Voucher.fields("RefCCYDATE") = PDate
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Date") = PDate
                                rs_Voucher.fields("ProAcc") = ProAcc
                                rs_Voucher.fields("VType") = VType
                                If PVoucher <> "-" Then
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Remarks") = "Reference Voucher No. : " & PVoucher & "..."
                                Else
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Remarks") = "Reference Voucher No. : " & PVoucher & "..."
                                End If
                                rs_Voucher.fields("DocNo") = ""     ' Only Fill if Creating WithHolding Tax for Each Payment Voucher
                                rs_Voucher.fields("RefNo") = PID
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Rate") = 1
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Rate_RC") = exchange_rate_RC(db, br_currency, PDate, br_currency_1)
                                rs_Voucher.fields("TaxType") = TaxType
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Currency") = br_currency
                                rs_Voucher.fields("DebtorF") = False
                                rs_Voucher.fields("CreditorF") = True
                                rs_Voucher.fields("TaxF") = True
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Posting_Flag") = False
                                rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Cat") = "N"
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Branch") = Branch
                                rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Flag") = True
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Status") = "U"
                                rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Tag") = 0
                                rs_Voucher.fields("PDate") = PDate
                                rs_Voucher.fields("CDate") = Today.Date
                                rs_Voucher.fields("CTime") = Time()
                                rs_Voucher.fields("UserID") = db_user
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user

                                rs_SL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * From nSL where 1<>1")
                                Num_Doc = 0
                                PNominal = 0
                                PNominalNA = 0
                                Nominal_CC = 0
                                Diff_CC = 0
                                TAX = 0
                                Nominal_OC = 0
                                TaxPCT = toval(rs.fields("TaxPCT"))
                                TaxRate = toval(rsVType.fields("TaxRate"))
                                Do While Not rs.Eof And PID = tostr(rs.fields("ID"))
                                    PVoucher = tostr(rs.fields("PVoucher"))

                                    If rs.fields("DebtorF") Then
                                        Nominal_OC = toval(rs.fields("Nominal_OC"))
                                        PNominal_Tax = toval(rs.fields("PNominal_Tax"))
                                    Else
                                        Nominal_OC = -toval(rs.fields("Nominal_OC"))
                                        PNominal_Tax = -toval(rs.fields("PNominal_Tax"))
                                    End If

                                    Dim rsT As RemoteSet
                                    Dim Amount As Double
                                    rsT = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Voucher.* from nVoucher Voucher where Voucher.Voucher='" & tostr(rs.fields("Voucher")) & "'")
                                    Amount = (toval(rsT.fields("Nominal_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Diff_CC")))
                                    Tax_CC = toval(rsT.fields("Tax_CC"))

                                    If Tax_CC = 0 And MTaxType <> "PASAL21" Then
                                        AccruedF = False
                                        Tax_CC = -PNominal_Tax * TaxRate / 100 * TaxPCT / 100
                                        Rate = Tax_Rate(db, tostr(rs.fields("Currency")), PDate)
                                    Else
                                        AccruedF = True
                                        Rate = Tax_Rate(db, tostr(rs.fields("Currency")), PDate)
                                    End If

                                    If rs.fields("DebtorF") Then
                                        If Nominal_OC = Amount Then
                                            TaxPayment_CC = Tax_CC
                                        Else
                                            TaxPayment_CC = Nominal_OC / Amount * Tax_CC
                                        End If
                                        If Tax_CC > 0 Then
                                            If rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC") + TaxPayment_CC > Tax_CC Then
                                                TaxPayment_CC = Tax_CC - rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC")
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            If rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC") + TaxPayment_CC < Tax_CC Then
                                                TaxPayment_CC = Tax_CC - rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC")
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        If Nominal_OC = Amount Then
                                            TaxPayment_CC = -(Tax_CC)
                                        Else
                                            TaxPayment_CC = -(Nominal_OC / Amount * Tax_CC)
                                        End If
                                        If Tax_CC < 0 Then
                                            If rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC") - TaxPayment_CC < Tax_CC Then
                                                TaxPayment_CC = -(Tax_CC - rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC"))
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            If rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC") - TaxPayment_CC > Tax_CC Then
                                                TaxPayment_CC = -(Tax_CC - rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC"))
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                    TaxPayment_CC = RoundCCY(TaxPayment_CC, UCase(tostr(rs.fields("Currency"))), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))

                                    If TaxPayment_CC <> 0 Or MTaxType = "PASAL21" Then
                                        rs_SL.AddNew()
                                        rs_SL.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                                        rs_SL.fields("pVoucher") = PVoucher
                                        rs_SL.fields("Doc_No") = tostr(rs.fields("Voucher"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("Doc_Date") = todate(rs.fields("Date"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("CT") = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))

                                        Dim Nominator As Double
                                        Dim Denominator As Double

                                        If Rate > 1 Then
                                            Nominator = Math.Round(Rate, 4, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
                                            Denominator = 1
                                        Else
                                            Nominator = 1
                                            Denominator = Math.Round(1 / Rate, 4, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
                                        End If
                                        Rate = Nominator / Denominator

                                        If AccruedF Then
                                            rs_SL.fields("TaxPayment_OC") = TaxPayment_CC
                                            rs_SL.fields("Doc_Ccy") = tostr(rs.fields("Currency"))
                                            rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC") = TaxPayment_CC
                                            rs_SL.fields("Rate") = Rate
                                            rs_SL.fields("Nominator") = Nominator
                                            rs_SL.fields("Denominator") = Denominator
                                            rs_SL.fields("Nominal") = RoundCCY(TaxPayment_CC * Rate, UCase(br_currency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                                        Else
                                            rs_SL.fields("TaxPayment_OC") = TaxPayment_CC
                                            rs_SL.fields("Doc_Ccy") = tostr(rs.fields("Currency"))
                                            rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC") = TaxPayment_CC
                                            rs_SL.fields("Rate") = Rate
                                            rs_SL.fields("Nominator") = Nominator
                                            rs_SL.fields("Denominator") = Denominator
                                            rs_SL.fields("Nominal") = RoundCCY(TaxPayment_CC * Rate, UCase(br_currency), toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                                        End If

                                        rsA = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 V.Credit as Hint, A.Account, A.Description from VPattern V, Account A where A.Account=V.Account and A.Open_ItemF=1 and V.Vtype='" & VType & "' and V.Credit=-14")
                                        If rsA.Eof Then
                                            StrMsg = "No Voucher Type Account for " & VType
                                            Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                                        End If
                                        rs_SL.fields("Hint") = toval(rsA.fields("Hint"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("Account") = tostr(rsA.fields("Account"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("Remarks") = tostr(rsA.fields("Description"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("PaymentF") = True
                                        rs_SL.fields("LockedF") = False
                                        If Not rs_SL.UpdateRow() Then Throw New Exception(rs_SL.ErrDesc)

                                        rsT.Edit()
                                        If toval(rsT.fields("CreditorF")) Then
                                            rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC") = toval(rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC")) - TaxPayment_CC
                                        Else
                                            rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC") = toval(rsT.fields("TaxPayment_CC")) + TaxPayment_CC
                                        End If
                                        If Not rsT.Update Then Throw New Exception(rsT.ErrDesc)

                                        If Not AccruedF Or toval(rsT.fields("BackdatedF")) Then
                                            If PNominal_Tax = 0 Then
                                                PNominal_Tax = (toval(rsT.fields("Nominal_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Diff_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Vat_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Tax_CC")))
                                            End If
                                            AdjustmentF = False
                                        Else
                                            If PType = "DI" And PSubject = "Claim" Then
                                                If PNominal_Tax = 0 Then
                                                    SQL = " Select sum(PNOMINAL_TAX) as PNOMINAL_TAX"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " FROM "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " ("
                                                    SQL = SQL & " Select Distinct C1.CNO, C1.INO, C1.NOC as TypeCode, C1.Payment as ID, C1.Currency, C1.RefNo, (Case When C2.CAmount=0 then -1 else 1 end)*C1.VATAmount as PNOMINAL_VAT, (Case When C2.CAmount=0 then -1 else 1 end)*C1.TaxAmount as PNOMINAL_TAX				"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " from CLLOSS C1, ClaAss C2, Claim C3, Claim C4, ADMLINK A"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " Where C2.Type='DI' and C2.Code='A' and C2.AdmNo=A.AdmNO "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C3.CNO=C2.CNO and C1.CNO=(Case When C2.CAmount<>0 Then C3.CNO else C3.pCNO end) and C4.CNO=C1.CNO"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C1.Currency=C2.Currency and C1.Payment=C2.ID "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C2.RefNo=(Case When C1.RefNO<>'' Then C1.RefNo else C4.RefNo end)"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and A.Voucher='" & rsT.fields("Voucher") & "'"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " Union All"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " Select Distinct C1.CNO, 0 as INO, C1.DCode as TypeCode, C1.ID, C1.Currency, '' as RefNo, -(Case When C2.CAmount=0 then -1 else 1 end)*C1.VATAmount as PNOMINAL_VAT, -(Case When C2.CAmount=0 then -1 else 1 end)*C1.TaxAmount as PNOMINAL_TAX				"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " from CLDED C1, ClaAss C2, Claim C3, Claim C4, Admlink A"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " Where C2.Type='DI' and C2.Code='E' and C2.AdmNo=A.AdmNO "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C3.CNO=C2.CNO and C1.CNO=(Case When C2.CAmount<>0 Then C3.CNO else C3.pCNO end) and C4.CNO=C1.CNO"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C1.Currency=C2.Currency and C1.ID=C2.ID "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C2.RefNo=(Case When C1.RefNO<>'' Then C1.RefNo else C4.RefNo end)"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and A.Voucher='" & rsT.fields("Voucher") & "'"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " ) C"
                                                    rsT = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL)
                                                    If Not rsT.Eof Then
                                                        PNominal_Tax = toval(rsT.fields("PNOMINAL_TAX"))
                                                        If MTaxType <> "PASAL21" And AccruedF = True And Rate = 1 Then
                                                            AdjustmentF = False
                                                        Else
                                                            AdjustmentF = True
                                                        End If
                                                    Else
                                                        AdjustmentF = False
                                                    End If
                                                Else
                                                    If MTaxType <> "PASAL21" And AccruedF = True And Rate = 1 Then
                                                        AdjustmentF = False
                                                    Else
                                                        AdjustmentF = True
                                                    End If
                                                End If
                                            ElseIf PType = "OP" And PSubject = "Claim" Then
                                                If PNominal_Tax = 0 Then
                                                    SQL = " Select sum(PNOMINAL_TAX) as PNominal_Tax"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " FROM"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " ("
                                                    SQL = SQL & " Select Distinct C1.CNO, C1.ID, C1.CCTYPE, C1.Currency, C1.RefNo, "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " (Case when C2.CAmount=0 then -1 else 1 end)*(Case When C3.ISType='L' and C1.ContributionF=1 and C1.CCOLLECTIONF=0 Then (100-R.MShare)/100 Else 1 End)*C1.GrossAmount as PNOMINAL_VAT, "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " (Case when C2.CAmount=0 then -1 else 1 end)*(Case When C3.ISType='L' and C1.ContributionF=1 and C1.CCOLLECTIONF=0 Then (100-R.MShare)/100 Else 1 End)*C1.GrossAmount as PNOMINAL_TAX "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " from CLCOST C1, ClaAss C2, Claim C3, Claim C4, RArrHeader R, ADMLINK A"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " Where C2.Type='OP' and C2.Code='L' and C2.AdmNo=A.AdmNO "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C3.CNO=C2.CNO and C1.CNO=(Case When C2.CAmount<>0 Then C3.CNO else C3.pCNO end) and C4.CNO=C1.CNO and R.ANO=C4.ANO"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C1.Currency=C2.Currency and C1.ID=C2.ID "
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and C2.RefNo=(Case When C1.RefNO<>'' Then C1.RefNo else C4.RefNo end)"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " and A.Voucher='" & rsT.fields("Voucher") & "'"
                                                    SQL = SQL & " ) C"
                                                    rsT = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL)
                                                    If Not rsT.Eof Then
                                                        PNominal_Tax = toval(rsT.fields("PNOMINAL_TAX"))
                                                        If MTaxType <> "PASAL21" And AccruedF = True And Rate = 1 Then
                                                            AdjustmentF = False
                                                        Else
                                                            AdjustmentF = True
                                                        End If
                                                    Else
                                                        AdjustmentF = False
                                                    End If
                                                Else
                                                    If MTaxType <> "PASAL21" And AccruedF = True And Rate = 1 Then
                                                        AdjustmentF = False
                                                    Else
                                                        AdjustmentF = True
                                                    End If
                                                End If
                                            ElseIf PType <> "BB" And PType <> "BA" And PType <> "BO" And PType <> "BC" And PType <> "BP" And PType <> "BD" And PType <> "OP" Then
                                                If PNominal_Tax = 0 Then
                                                    PNominal_Tax = (toval(rsT.fields("Nominal_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Diff_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Vat_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Tax_CC")))
                                                End If
                                                AdjustmentF = False
                                            Else
                                                If PNominal_Tax = 0 Then
                                                    PNominal_Tax = (toval(rsT.fields("Nominal_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Diff_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Vat_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Tax_CC")))
                                                End If
                                                AdjustmentF = True
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                        If Tax_CC <> 0 Then
                                            Dim PTaxAmount As Double = 0
                                            PTaxAmount = (PNominal_Tax) / (-Tax_CC)
                                            If AdjustmentF Then
                                                TAX = TAX + toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal"))
                                                PNominal = PNominal + toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate")) * toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC")) * PTaxAmount
                                            Else
                                                PNominalNA = PNominalNA + toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate")) * toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC")) * PTaxAmount
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            Dim tPNominal As Double = 0
                                            tPNominal = (PNominal_Tax)

                                            ' Proportion by Nominal
                                            tPNominal = tPNominal * Nominal_OC / Amount

                                            If rs.fields("DebtorF") Then
                                                tPNominal = -tPNominal
                                            End If

                                            If AdjustmentF Then
                                                TAX = TAX + toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal"))
                                                PNominal = PNominal + tPNominal * toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate"))
                                            Else
                                                PNominalNA = PNominalNA + tPNominal * toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate"))
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                        If toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")) >= 0 Then
                                            Nominal_CC = Nominal_CC + (toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")))
                                        Else
                                            Diff_CC = Diff_CC - (toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")))
                                        End If
                                        Num_Doc = Num_Doc + 1
                                    End If

                                    rs.MoveNext()
                                Loop

                                If Num_Doc <> 0 Then
                                    ' Tax Adjustment
                                    If AdjustmentF Then
                                        Dim NTaxIncome As Double
                                        Dim TaxIncome As Double
                                        Dim TaxDiscount As Double
                                        Dim AccruedTax As Double
                                        Dim rsT As RemoteSet

                                        rsT = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Tax, AccruedTax, NTaxIncome, (Case When '" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' Between coalesce(Profile.TaxHSDate,'01/01/1900') and coalesce(Profile.TaxHEDate,'01/01/1900') then 0 Else (Case When Profile.TaxPCT=0 Then 100 Else Profile.TaxPCT end) end) as TaxPCT from Profile Where ID='" & PID & "'")
                                        TaxPCT = toval(rsT.fields("TaxPCT"))
                                        If TaxRate = 0 Then TaxRate = toval(rsT.fields("Tax"))
                                        NTaxIncome = toval(rsT.fields("NTaxIncome"))
                                        AccruedTax = toval(rsT.fields("AccruedTax"))

                                        rsT = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select TaxDiscount, PASAL4, PASAL21, PASAL23, PASAL26, UseTaxTypeF from MParam Where Type='" & PType & "'")
                                        TaxDiscount = toval(rsT.fields("TaxDiscount"))
                                        If MTaxType = "PASAL21" Then
                                            If TaxTableF Then
                                                Dim TAmount As Double
                                                Dim TTax As Double

                                                ' TAmount and TTax already in Base Currency
                                                Get_Tax_Table_Paid(db, PID, PDate, TAmount, TTax)

                                                'Looking at Progressive Table
                                                TaxIncome = (-TAmount + PNominal) * (100 - TaxDiscount) / 100 - NTaxIncome * (DateDiff(DateInterval.Month, DateSerial(Year(PDate), 1, 1), PDate) + 1)
                                                If TaxIncome < 0 Then TaxIncome = 0

                                                'TaxIncome = (-TAmount + PNominal) * (100 - TaxDiscount) / 100

                                                TAX = -RoundCCY(Calc_Tax_Amount(db, br_currency, PDate, TaxIncome, TaxPCT, 1), br_currency, 0.01) - TTax - TAX
                                            Else
                                                TaxIncome = (PNominal) * (100 - TaxDiscount) / 100
                                                TAX = -RoundCCY(Calc_Tax_Amount(db, br_currency, PDate, TaxIncome, TaxPCT, 1), br_currency, 0.01) - TAX
                                            End If
                                        Else    ' Pasal23 / Pasal26
                                            If InStr("BBBABOBCBPBDSC", PType) <> 0 OrElse PSubject.ToUpper = "PREMIUM" Then
                                                If Not toval(rsT.fields("UseTaxTypeF")) Then
                                                    If toval(rsT.fields(MTaxType)) <> 0 Then
                                                        TaxRate = toval(rsT.fields(MTaxType))
                                                    Else
                                                        TaxRate = AccruedTax
                                                    End If
                                                End If
                                            End If
                                            TAX = RoundCCY(PNominal * TaxRate / 100 * TaxPCT / 100, br_currency, 0.01) - TAX
                                        End If

                                        If TAX <> 0 Then
                                            TAX = RoundCCY(TAX, br_currency, toval(GetAppSetting("FARoundingDecimalValue")))
                                            rs_SL.AddNew()
                                            rs_SL.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                                            rs_SL.fields("Doc_No") = "Adjustment Tax"
                                            rs_SL.fields("Doc_Date") = PDate
                                            rs_SL.fields("Doc_Ccy") = br_currency
                                            rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC") = TAX
                                            rs_SL.fields("Rate") = 1
                                            rs_SL.fields("Nominal") = TAX
                                            rs_SL.fields("Hint") = A_Hint
                                            rs_SL.fields("Account") = A_Account
                                            rs_SL.fields("PaymentF") = False
                                            rs_SL.fields("LockedF") = False
                                            If Not rs_SL.UpdateRow Then Throw New Exception(rs_SL.ErrDesc)
                                            If toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")) >= 0 Then
                                                Nominal_CC = Nominal_CC + (toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")))
                                            Else
                                                Diff_CC = Diff_CC - (toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")))
                                            End If
                                            Num_Doc = Num_Doc + 1
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal_CC") = Nominal_CC
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Diff_CC") = Diff_CC
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal") = Nominal_CC * toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Rate"))
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Diff") = Diff_CC * toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Rate"))
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Num_doc") = Num_Doc

                                    If TaxTableF And MTaxType = "PASAL21" Then
                                        rs_Voucher.fields("RefAmount") = PNominal
                                        If Not Update_Tax_Table_Paid(db, PID, PDate, -PNominal, Nominal_CC - Diff_CC) Then
                                            StrMsg = "Failed in Calculating Vat/Tax !"
                                            Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                    If Not rs_Voucher.Update() Then Throw New Exception(rs_Voucher.ErrDesc)
                                    ' Finally
                                    If Not rs_SL.Update Then Throw New Exception(rs_SL.ErrDesc)

                                    ' Insert into BPP Record
                                    'Insert_Into_BPP_Record(Branch, TaxRate, PNominal + PNominalNA, Nominal_CC - Diff_CC, rs_Voucher.fields("Voucher"), PDate, PID, MTaxType, PType)
                                Else ' No Withholding Tax Voucher Created !
                                    rs_Voucher.DeleteRow()
                                    rs_Voucher.CancelUpdate()
                                End If
                            Loop
                        Catch ex As Exception
                            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
                        End Try
                    End If
                    rsVType.MoveNext()
                Loop

                rsBranch.MoveNext()
            Loop

            Process_WithholdingTax = True

        Catch ex As Exception
            StrMsg = ex.Message
            Process_WithholdingTax = False
        Finally
        End Try
    End Function

    Private Function Create_WithHoldingVAT_All(ByVal PBranch As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal PaidF As Boolean, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date)
        Try
            Dim NumThreads As Integer = toval(GetAppSetting("Process_FA_NumThreads"))
            If NumThreads <= 0 Then NumThreads = 1
            Dim newDB As ClassLib
            Dim rs As RemoteSet

            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch.Branch from Branch where Branch.Op_Flag=1 and Branch.Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and Branch.Branch in (" & Get_Sub_Branch(PBranch) & ")")
            Dim i As Long
            Dim AllOk As Boolean = True
            Dim NumDb As Integer = 0

            Do While True
                Dim arrResult(NumThreads - 1) As IAsyncResult
                Dim arrCaller(NumThreads - 1) As Process_WithHoldingVAT_Delegate
                Dim arrdb(NumThreads - 1) As ClassLib

                For i = 0 To NumThreads - 1
                    arrCaller(i) = Nothing
                    arrResult(i) = Nothing
                Next

                For i = 0 To NumThreads - 1
                    newDB = New ClassLib
                    If Not newDB.OpenConnection("", db_Name, db_Server, db_sysuser, db_syspassword, db_TimeOut, db_Trusted) Then
                        Throw New Exception(newDB.ErrDesc)
                    End If
                    newDB.Var_db_user = db_user
                    newDB.Var_db_Password = db_password
                    newDB.Var_db_User_Type = db_user_type
                    newDB.Begintrans()

                    Save_Log_Process(newDB, "Create_WithHoldingVAT_All", PDate, rs.fields("Branch"))

                    arrCaller(i) = New Process_WithHoldingVAT_Delegate(AddressOf Process_WithHoldingVAT)
                    arrResult(i) = arrCaller(i).BeginInvoke(newDB, rs.fields("Branch"), PDate, PaidF, SDate, EDate, Nothing, Nothing)
                    arrResult(i).AsyncWaitHandle.WaitOne(0, False)
                    arrdb(i) = newDB

                    rs.MoveNext()
                    If rs.Eof Then Exit For
                Next

                Dim ExitLoop As Boolean = False
                Do While Not ExitLoop
                    For i = 0 To NumThreads - 1
                        If Not arrResult(i) Is Nothing Then
                            If arrResult(i).IsCompleted Then
                                ExitLoop = True
                            Else
                                ExitLoop = False
                                Exit For
                            End If
                        Else
                            ExitLoop = True
                        End If
                    Next
                    If Not ExitLoop Then System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(60000)
                Loop

                For i = 0 To NumThreads - 1
                    If Not arrCaller(i) Is Nothing Then
                        If Not arrCaller(i).EndInvoke(arrResult(i)) Then
                            AllOk = False
                            arrdb(i).RollBack()
                        Else
                            arrdb(i).Committrans()
                        End If
                    End If
                Next

                If Not AllOk Then Exit Do

                If rs.Eof Then Exit Do
            Loop

            If Not AllOk Then Throw New Exception(StrMsg)

            Create_WithHoldingVAT_All = True
        Catch ex As Exception
            StrMsg = ex.Message
            Create_WithHoldingVAT_All = False
        End Try
    End Function
    Public Delegate Function Process_WithHoldingVAT_Delegate(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal PBranch As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal PaidF As Boolean, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date) As Boolean
    Private Function Process_WithHoldingVAT(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal PBranch As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal PaidF As Boolean, ByVal SDate As Date, ByVal EDate As Date) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_Voucher As RemoteSet
        Dim rs_SL As RemoteSet
        Dim rsA As RemoteSet

        Dim PVoucher As String
        Dim PID As String       ' Proacc ID
        Dim PSubject As String
        Dim Num_Doc As Integer
        Dim PNominal As Double
        Dim PNominalNA As Double
        Dim Nominal_CC As Double
        Dim Nominal_OC As Double
        Dim PNominal_VAT As Double
        Dim Diff_CC As Double
        Dim VAT As Double
        Dim SQL As String
        Dim VATPayment_CC As Double
        Dim AdjustmentF As Boolean
        Dim rsBranch As RemoteSet
        Dim VType As String
        Dim rsVType As RemoteSet
        Dim Code As String
        Dim CodeExt As String
        Dim AutoNum As String
        Dim A_Hint As Integer
        Dim A_Account As String
        Dim Branch As String
        Dim Voucher As String
        Dim VAT_CC As Double
        Dim PType As String
        Dim br_Start_Date As Date
        Dim Rate As Double
        Dim ProAcc As String

        Try
            If PBranch <> "" Then
                rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, Start_Date, End_Date, Month, Year, KPPID from Branch Where Branch='" & PBranch & "' and OP_Flag=1 and Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            Else
                rsBranch = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Branch, Start_Date, End_Date, Month, Year, KPPID from Branch Where OP_Flag=1 and Start_Date<='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
            End If

            Do While Not rsBranch.Eof
                Branch = rsBranch.fields("Branch")

                rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select top 1 nVoucher.Voucher from nVoucher, VType Where VType.VType=nVoucher.VType and VType.VatF=1 and nVoucher.Branch='" & Branch & "' and nVoucher.Date='" & Format(PDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
                If rs.Eof Then

                    If tostr(rsBranch.fields("KPPID")) <> "" Then
                        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select ProAcc from ProAcc where ID='" & tostr(rsBranch.fields("KPPID")) & "' and Currency='" & br_currency & "'")
                        ProAcc = rs.fields("ProAcc")
                    Else
                        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("select ProAcc from ProAcc where Description like '%kantor pajak%' and Currency='" & br_currency & "'")
                        ProAcc = rs.fields("ProAcc")
                    End If
                    If ProAcc = "" Then
                        StrMsg = "No Tax Office Profile !"
                        Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                    End If

                    br_Start_Date = rsBranch.fields("Start_Date")
                    If (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, rsBranch.fields("End_Date")) = 0 And rsBranch.fields("Month") = Month(PDate) And rsBranch.fields("Year") = Year(PDate)) Or (DateDiff(DateInterval.Day, PDate, rsBranch.fields("End_Date")) > 0) Then
                        StrMsg = StrMsg & "Branch : '" & Branch & "' Closing Month & Ending Date Constraint !" & vbCrLf
                        Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                    End If

                    Try
                        rsVType = db.OpenRemoteSet("select VType.Vtype, VType.Code, VType.CodeExt, VType.AutoNum from VType where VType.CreditorF=1 and VType.SL_Cat='N' and VType.VatF=1")
                        Do While Not rsVType.Eof
                            VType = tostr(rsVType.fields("VType"))
                            Code = tostr(rsVType.fields("Code"))
                            CodeExt = tostr(rsVType.fields("CodeExt"))
                            AutoNum = tostr(rsVType.fields("AutoNum"))
                            A_Hint = 0
                            A_Account = ""

                            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 V.Credit as Hint, A.Account from VPattern V, Account A where A.Account=V.Account and A.Open_ItemF=1 and V.Vtype='" & VType & "' and V.Credit<>-13")
                            If rs.Eof Then
                                StrMsg = "No Adjustment Account for Withholding VAT Voucher Type !"
                                Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                            End If
                            A_Hint = toval(rs.fields("Hint"))
                            A_Account = tostr(rs.fields("Account"))

                            ' Voucher
                            If PaidF Then ' Paid Only
                                SQL = " Select VoucherP.Voucher as PVoucher, Profile.ID, VoucherP.Last_Date, VoucherP.Last_Time, Max(dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(SL.Nominal_OC, VoucherP.SL_Cat, VoucherP.DebtorF, Voucher.DebtorF)) as Nominal_OC, Max(Voucher.Rate) as PRate,"
                                SQL = SQL & " sum(Admlink.PNominal_VAT) as PNominal_VAT, Max(Admlink.Subject) as Subject, Max(Admlink.Type) as Type, "
                                SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Voucher, cast(Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as Int)) as Bit) as DebtorF, Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, Max(Voucher.CT) as CT"
                                SQL = SQL & " from"
                                SQL = SQL & " PVoucher VoucherP"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join pSL SL on (SL.Voucher=VoucherP.Voucher and SL.PaymentF=1)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join nVoucher Voucher on (Voucher.Voucher=SL.Doc_No and Voucher.VatF=1 and Voucher.Branch='" & Branch & "')"
                                If CodeExt <> "" Then
                                    SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Admlink on (Admlink.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Admlink.Subject='" & CodeExt & "' and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%')"
                                Else
                                    SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Admlink on (Admlink.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%')"
                                End If
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Profile on (Profile.ID=Admlink.ID)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Outer Apply (Select Top 1 nSL.Doc_no from nSL Inner Join nVoucher on (nVoucher.Voucher=nSL.Voucher and nVoucher.VatF=1) Where nSL.Doc_No=Voucher.Voucher and nSL.pVoucher=VoucherP.Voucher and nSL.PaymentF=1 Group by nSL.Doc_No having (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)<>0) or (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)=0 and count(*)=1)) R"
                                SQL = SQL & " Where VoucherP.Status<>'N' and VoucherP.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and coalesce(R.Doc_No,'')=''"
                                SQL = SQL & " and (Case When Admlink.PNominal_VAT=0 Then 0 else Admlink.Amount_13/Admlink.PNominal_VAT end)<0"
                                SQL = SQL & " Group By Profile.ID, VoucherP.Last_Date, VoucherP.Last_Time, VoucherP.Voucher, Voucher.Voucher"

                                SQL = SQL & " Union All "

                                SQL = SQL & " Select '-' as PVoucher, Profile.ID, Voucher.Last_Date, Voucher.Last_Time, sum(Admlink.Amount) as Nominal_OC, Max(Voucher.Rate) as PRate,"
                                SQL = SQL & " sum(Admlink.PNominal_VAT) as PNominal_VAT, Max(Admlink.Subject) as Subject, Max(Admlink.Type) as Type, "
                                SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Voucher, cast(Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as Int)) as Bit) as DebtorF, Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, Max(Voucher.CT) as CT"
                                SQL = SQL & " from Admlink"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join nVoucher Voucher on (Voucher.Voucher=Admlink.Voucher and Voucher.VatF=1 and Voucher.Branch='" & Branch & "')"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Profile on (Profile.ID=Admlink.ID)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Outer Apply (Select Top 1 nSL.Doc_no from nSL Inner Join nVoucher on (nVoucher.Voucher=nSL.Voucher and nVoucher.VatF=1) Where nSL.Doc_No=Voucher.Voucher and nSL.pVoucher='-' and nSL.PaymentF=1 Group by nSL.Doc_No having (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)<>0) or (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)=0 and count(*)=1)) R"
                                SQL = SQL & " Where Admlink.Subject<>'Others' and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%'"
                                If CodeExt <> "" Then
                                    SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Subject='" & CodeExt & "'"
                                End If
                                SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and coalesce(R.Doc_No,'')=''"
                                SQL = SQL & " and (Case When Admlink.PNominal_VAT=0 Then 0 else Admlink.Amount_13/Admlink.PNominal_VAT end)<0"
                                SQL = SQL & " and round(Voucher.Nominal_CC-Voucher.Diff_CC,4)=0"
                                SQL = SQL & " Group By Profile.ID, Voucher.Last_Date, Voucher.Last_Time, Voucher.Voucher"

                                SQL = SQL & " Order By ID, Last_Date, Last_Time, pVoucher, Voucher"
                            Else

                                SQL = "Select '-' as PVoucher, Profile.ID, sum(Admlink.Amount) as Nominal_OC, Max(Voucher.Rate) as PRate,"
                                SQL = SQL & " sum(Admlink.PNominal_VAT) as PNominal_VAT, Max(Admlink.Subject) as Subject, Max(Admlink.Type) as Type, "
                                SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Voucher, cast(Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as Int)) as Bit) as DebtorF, Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, Max(Voucher.CT) as CT"
                                SQL = SQL & " from Admlink"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join nVoucher Voucher on (Voucher.Voucher=Admlink.Voucher and Voucher.VATF=1 and Voucher.Branch='" & Branch & "')"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Profile on (Profile.ID=Admlink.ID)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Outer Apply (Select Top 1 nSL.Doc_no from nSL Inner Join nVoucher on (nVoucher.Voucher=nSL.Voucher and nVoucher.VatF=1) Where nSL.Doc_No=Voucher.Voucher and nSL.pVoucher='-' and nSL.PaymentF=1 Group by nSL.Doc_No having (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)<>0) or (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)=0 and count(*)=1)) R"
                                SQL = SQL & " Where Admlink.Subject<>'Others' and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%'"
                                If CodeExt <> "" Then
                                    SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Subject='" & CodeExt & "'"
                                End If
                                SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and coalesce(R.Doc_No,'')=''"
                                SQL = SQL & " and (Case When Admlink.PNominal_VAT=0 Then 0 else Admlink.Amount_13/Admlink.PNominal_VAT end)<0"
                                SQL = SQL & " Group By Profile.ID, Voucher.Voucher"

                                SQL = SQL & " Union All "

                                'Other Transaction always Paid
                                SQL = SQL & " Select VoucherP.Voucher as PVoucher, Profile.ID, Max(dbo.fn_SL_Payment_Nominal(SL.Nominal_OC, VoucherP.SL_Cat, VoucherP.DebtorF, Voucher.DebtorF)) as Nominal_OC, Max(Voucher.Rate) as PRate,"
                                SQL = SQL & " sum(Admlink.PNominal_VAT) as PNominal_VAT, Max(Admlink.Subject) as Subject, Max(Admlink.Type) as Type, "
                                SQL = SQL & " Voucher.Voucher, cast(Max(Cast(Voucher.DebtorF as Int)) as Bit) as DebtorF, Max(Voucher.Date) as Date, Max(Voucher.Currency) as Currency, Max(Voucher.CT) as CT"
                                SQL = SQL & " from"
                                SQL = SQL & " PVoucher VoucherP"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join pSL SL on (SL.Voucher=VoucherP.Voucher and SL.PaymentF=1)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join nVoucher Voucher on (Voucher.Voucher=SL.Doc_No and Voucher.VatF=1 and Voucher.Branch='" & Branch & "')"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Admlink on (Admlink.Voucher=Voucher.Voucher and (Admlink.Subject='" & CodeExt & "' or '" & CodeExt & "'='') and Admlink.Type Like '" & Code & "%')"
                                SQL = SQL & " Inner Join Profile on (Profile.ID=Admlink.ID)"
                                SQL = SQL & " Outer Apply (Select Top 1 nSL.Doc_no from nSL Inner Join nVoucher on (nVoucher.Voucher=nSL.Voucher and nVoucher.VatF=1) Where nSL.Doc_No=Voucher.Voucher and nSL.pVoucher=VoucherP.Voucher and nSL.PaymentF=1 Group by nSL.Doc_No having (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)<>0) or (round(sum(nSL.Nominal_OC),4)=0 and count(*)=1)) R"
                                SQL = SQL & " Where VoucherP.Status<>'N' and VoucherP.Date Between '" & Format(SDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "' and '" & Format(EDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and coalesce(R.Doc_No,'')=''"
                                SQL = SQL & " and Admlink.Subject='Others'"
                                SQL = SQL & " and (Case When Admlink.PNominal_VAT=0 Then 0 else Admlink.Amount_13/Admlink.PNominal_VAT end)<0"
                                SQL = SQL & " Group By Profile.ID, VoucherP.Voucher, Voucher.Voucher"
                                SQL = SQL & " Order by ID, pVoucher, Voucher"
                            End If


                            rs = db.OpenRemoteSet(SQL, adOpenForwardonly)

                            PVoucher = ""
                            PID = ""

                            Do While Not rs.Eof
                                PVoucher = tostr(rs.fields("PVoucher"))
                                PID = tostr(rs.fields("ID"))
                                PSubject = tostr(rs.fields("Subject"))
                                PType = tostr(rs.fields("Type"))
                                rs_Voucher = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from nVoucher Where 1<>1")
                                rs_Voucher.AddNew()
                                Voucher = db.GetNextDocNo(AutoNum, PDate, Branch)
                                If Voucher = "" Then
                                    StrMsg = "Generate Voucher No. Failed !"
                                    Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                                End If
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                                rs_Voucher.fields("RefCCYDATE") = PDate
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Date") = PDate
                                rs_Voucher.fields("ProAcc") = ProAcc
                                rs_Voucher.fields("VType") = VType
                                If PVoucher <> "-" Then
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Remarks") = "Reference Voucher No. : " & PVoucher & "..."
                                Else
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Remarks") = "Reference Voucher No. : " & PVoucher & "..."
                                End If
                                rs_Voucher.fields("DocNo") = ""     ' Only Fill if Creating WithHolding VAT for Each Payment Voucher
                                rs_Voucher.fields("RefNo") = PID
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Rate") = 1
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Rate_RC") = exchange_rate_RC(db, br_currency, PDate, br_currency_1)
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Currency") = br_currency
                                rs_Voucher.fields("DebtorF") = False
                                rs_Voucher.fields("CreditorF") = True
                                rs_Voucher.fields("VATF") = True
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Posting_Flag") = False
                                rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Cat") = "N"
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Branch") = Branch
                                rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Flag") = True
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Status") = "U"
                                rs_Voucher.fields("SL_Tag") = 0
                                rs_Voucher.fields("PDate") = PDate
                                rs_Voucher.fields("CDate") = Today.Date
                                rs_Voucher.fields("CTime") = Time()
                                rs_Voucher.fields("UserID") = db_user
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Date") = Today.Date
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Time") = Time()
                                rs_Voucher.fields("Last_Opr") = db_user

                                rs_SL = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * From nSL where 1<>1")
                                Num_Doc = 0
                                PNominal = 0
                                PNominalNA = 0
                                Nominal_CC = 0
                                Diff_CC = 0
                                VAT = 0
                                Nominal_OC = 0
                                Do While Not rs.Eof And PID = tostr(rs.fields("ID"))
                                    PVoucher = tostr(rs.fields("PVoucher"))

                                    If rs.fields("DebtorF") Then
                                        Nominal_OC = toval(rs.fields("Nominal_OC"))
                                        PNominal_VAT = toval(rs.fields("PNominal_VAT"))
                                    Else
                                        Nominal_OC = -toval(rs.fields("Nominal_OC"))
                                        PNominal_VAT = -toval(rs.fields("PNominal_VAT"))
                                    End If

                                    Dim rsT As RemoteSet
                                    Dim Amount As Double
                                    rsT = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Voucher.* from nVoucher Voucher where Voucher.Voucher='" & tostr(rs.fields("Voucher")) & "'")
                                    Amount = (toval(rsT.fields("Nominal_CC")) - toval(rsT.fields("Diff_CC")))
                                    VAT_CC = toval(rsT.fields("VAT_CC"))

                                    Rate = Tax_Rate(db, tostr(rs.fields("Currency")), PDate)

                                    If rs.fields("DebtorF") Then
                                        If Nominal_OC = Amount Then
                                            VATPayment_CC = VAT_CC
                                        Else
                                            VATPayment_CC = Nominal_OC / Amount * VAT_CC
                                        End If
                                        If VAT_CC > 0 Then
                                            If toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC")) + VATPayment_CC > VAT_CC Then
                                                VATPayment_CC = VAT_CC - toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC"))
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            If toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC")) + VATPayment_CC < VAT_CC Then
                                                VATPayment_CC = VAT_CC - toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC"))
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    Else
                                        If Nominal_OC = Amount Then
                                            VATPayment_CC = -(VAT_CC)
                                        Else
                                            VATPayment_CC = -(Nominal_OC / Amount * VAT_CC)
                                        End If
                                        If VAT_CC < 0 Then
                                            If toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC")) - VATPayment_CC < VAT_CC Then
                                                VATPayment_CC = -(VAT_CC - toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC")))
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            If toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC")) - VATPayment_CC > VAT_CC Then
                                                VATPayment_CC = -(VAT_CC - toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC")))
                                            End If
                                        End If
                                    End If

                                    If VATPayment_CC <> 0 Then
                                        Dim Nominator As Double
                                        Dim Denominator As Double

                                        If Rate > 1 Then
                                            Nominator = Math.Round(Rate, 4, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
                                            Denominator = 1
                                        Else
                                            Nominator = 1
                                            Denominator = Math.Round(1 / Rate, 4, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)
                                        End If
                                        Rate = Nominator / Denominator

                                        rs_SL.AddNew()
                                        rs_SL.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                                        rs_SL.fields("pVoucher") = PVoucher
                                        rs_SL.fields("Doc_No") = tostr(rs.fields("Voucher"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("Doc_Date") = todate(rs.fields("Date"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("CT") = tostr(rs.fields("CT"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("VATPayment_OC") = VATPayment_CC
                                        rs_SL.fields("Doc_Ccy") = tostr(rs.fields("Currency"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC") = VATPayment_CC
                                        rs_SL.fields("Rate") = Rate
                                        rs_SL.fields("Nominator") = Nominator
                                        rs_SL.fields("Denominator") = Denominator
                                        rs_SL.fields("Nominal") = toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC")) * toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate"))

                                        rsA = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select Top 1 V.Credit as Hint, A.Account, A.Description from VPattern V, Account A where A.Account=V.Account and A.Open_ItemF=1 and V.Vtype='" & VType & "' and V.Credit=-13")
                                        If rsA.Eof Then
                                            StrMsg = "No Voucher Type Account for " & VType
                                            Throw New Exception(StrMsg)
                                        End If
                                        rs_SL.fields("Hint") = toval(rsA.fields("Hint"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("Account") = tostr(rsA.fields("Account"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("Remarks") = tostr(rsA.fields("Description"))
                                        rs_SL.fields("PaymentF") = True
                                        rs_SL.fields("LockedF") = False
                                        If Not rs_SL.UpdateRow() Then Throw New Exception(rs_SL.ErrDesc)

                                        rsT.Edit()
                                        If toval(rsT.fields("CreditorF")) Then
                                            rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC") = toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC")) - VATPayment_CC
                                        Else
                                            rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC") = toval(rsT.fields("VATPayment_CC")) + VATPayment_CC
                                        End If
                                        If Not rsT.Update Then Throw New Exception(rsT.ErrDesc)

                                        If toval(rsT.fields("BackdatedF")) Then
                                            AdjustmentF = False
                                        Else
                                            If Rate = 1 Then
                                                AdjustmentF = False
                                            Else
                                                AdjustmentF = True
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                        If VAT_CC <> 0 Then
                                            Dim PVATAmount As Double = 0
                                            PVATAmount = (PNominal_VAT) / (-VAT_CC)
                                            If AdjustmentF Then
                                                VAT = VAT + toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal"))
                                                PNominal = PNominal + toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate")) * toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC")) * PVATAmount
                                            Else
                                                PNominalNA = PNominalNA + toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate")) * toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC")) * PVATAmount
                                            End If
                                        Else
                                            Dim tPNominal As Double = 0
                                            tPNominal = (PNominal_VAT)

                                            ' Proportion by Nominal
                                            tPNominal = tPNominal * Nominal_OC / Amount

                                            If rs.fields("DebtorF") Then
                                                tPNominal = -tPNominal
                                            End If

                                            If AdjustmentF Then
                                                VAT = VAT + toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal"))
                                                PNominal = PNominal + tPNominal * toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate"))
                                            Else
                                                PNominalNA = PNominalNA + tPNominal * toval(rs_SL.fields("Rate"))
                                            End If
                                        End If

                                        If toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")) >= 0 Then
                                            Nominal_CC = Nominal_CC + (toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")))
                                        Else
                                            Diff_CC = Diff_CC - (toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")))
                                        End If
                                        Num_Doc = Num_Doc + 1
                                    End If

                                    rs.MoveNext()
                                Loop

                                If Num_Doc <> 0 Then
                                    ' VAT Adjustment
                                    Dim rsT As RemoteSet
                                    Dim AccruedVAT As Double
                                    Dim VATRate As Double

                                    rsT = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select AccruedVAT from Profile Where ID='" & PID & "'")
                                    AccruedVAT = toval(rsT.fields("AccruedVAT"))

                                    rsT = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select VAT from MParam Where Type='" & PType & "'")

                                    If toval(rsT.fields("VAT")) <> 0 Then
                                        VATRate = -toval(rsT.fields("VAT"))
                                    Else
                                        VATRate = -AccruedVAT
                                    End If

                                    VAT = RoundCCY(PNominal * VATRate / 100, br_currency, 0.01) - VAT

                                    If VAT <> 0 Then
                                        rs_SL.AddNew()
                                        rs_SL.fields("Voucher") = Voucher
                                        rs_SL.fields("Doc_No") = "Adjustment VAT"
                                        rs_SL.fields("Doc_Date") = PDate
                                        rs_SL.fields("Doc_Ccy") = br_currency
                                        rs_SL.fields("Nominal_OC") = VAT
                                        rs_SL.fields("Rate") = 1
                                        rs_SL.fields("Nominal") = VAT
                                        rs_SL.fields("Hint") = A_Hint
                                        rs_SL.fields("Account") = A_Account
                                        rs_SL.fields("PaymentF") = False
                                        rs_SL.fields("LockedF") = False
                                        If Not rs_SL.UpdateRow Then Throw New Exception(rs_SL.ErrDesc)
                                        If toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")) >= 0 Then
                                            Nominal_CC = Nominal_CC + (toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")))
                                        Else
                                            Diff_CC = Diff_CC - (toval(rs_SL.fields("Nominal")))
                                        End If
                                        Num_Doc = Num_Doc + 1
                                    End If

                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal_CC") = Nominal_CC
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Diff_CC") = Diff_CC
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Nominal") = Nominal_CC * toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Rate"))
