                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Diff") = Diff_CC * toval(rs_Voucher.fields("Rate"))
                                    rs_Voucher.fields("Num_doc") = Num_Doc

                                    If Not rs_Voucher.Update() Then Throw New Exception(rs_Voucher.ErrDesc)
                                    ' Finally
                                    If Not rs_SL.Update Then Throw New Exception(rs_SL.ErrDesc)

                                Else ' No Withholding VAT Voucher Created !
                                    rs_Voucher.DeleteRow()
                                    rs_Voucher.CancelUpdate()
                                End If
                            Loop

                            rsVType.MoveNext()
                        Loop

                    Catch ex As Exception
                        Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
                    End Try
                End If

                rsBranch.MoveNext()
            Loop

            Process_WithHoldingVAT = True

        Catch ex As Exception
            StrMsg = ex.Message
            Process_WithHoldingVAT = False
        Finally
        End Try
    End Function

    Private Function Get_Tax_Table_Paid(ByVal PID As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByRef Amount As Double, ByRef Tax As Double) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Yr As Integer
        Dim Mth As Integer
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Amount = 0
        Tax = 0
        Yr = Year(PDate)
        Mth = Month(PDate)
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select PNominal, Tax from TaxTablePaid Where Year=" & Yr & " and Month=" & Mth & " and ID='" & PID & "'")
        Amount = toval(rs.fields("PNominal"))
        Tax = toval(rs.fields("Tax"))
        Get_Tax_Table_Paid = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Get_Tax_Table_Paid = False
    End Function
    Private Function Get_Tax_Table_Paid(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal PID As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByRef Amount As Double, ByRef Tax As Double) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Yr As Integer
        Dim Mth As Integer
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Amount = 0
        Tax = 0
        Yr = Year(PDate)
        Mth = Month(PDate)
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select PNominal, Tax from TaxTablePaid Where Year=" & Yr & " and Month=" & Mth & " and ID='" & PID & "'")
        Amount = toval(rs.fields("PNominal"))
        Tax = toval(rs.fields("Tax"))
        Get_Tax_Table_Paid = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Get_Tax_Table_Paid = False
    End Function
    Private Function Update_Tax_Table_Paid(ByVal PID As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal Amount As Double, ByVal Tax As Double) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Yr As Integer
        Dim Mth As Integer
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        Yr = Year(PDate)
        Mth = Month(PDate)
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from TaxTablePaid Where Year=" & Yr & " and Month=" & Mth & " and ID='" & PID & "'")
        If rs.Eof Then
            rs.AddNew()
            rs.fields("Year") = Yr
            rs.fields("Month") = Mth
            rs.fields("ID") = PID
        Else
            rs.Edit()
        End If
        rs.fields("PNominal") = toval(rs.fields("PNominal")) + Amount
        rs.fields("Tax") = toval(rs.fields("Tax")) + Tax
        If Not rs.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        Update_Tax_Table_Paid = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Tax_Table_Paid = False
    End Function
    Private Function Update_Tax_Table_Paid(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal PID As String, ByVal PDate As Date, ByVal Amount As Double, ByVal Tax As Double) As Boolean
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        Dim Yr As Integer

        End If

        Exit Function
errmsg:
        StrMsg = StrError(StrMsg)
        Update_Tax_Table_Paid = False
    End Function
    Private Function GetLockedDate(ByVal Branch As String) As Date
        Dim LockedDate As Date
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select LockedDate from SysTab", adOpenForwardonly)
        LockedDate = todate(rs.fields("LockedDate"))
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select LockedDate from Branch Where Branch='" & Branch & "' and LockedDate>'" & Format(LockedDate, "MM/DD/YYYY") & "'")
        If Not rs.Eof Then
            LockedDate = todate(rs.fields("LockedDate"))
        End If
        GetLockedDate = LockedDate
    End Function

    Private Function GetBackDatedDate(ByVal Branch As String) As Date
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select BackDatedDate from Branch Where Branch='" & Branch & "'", adOpenForwardonly)
        GetBackDatedDate = todate(rs.fields("BackDatedDate"))
    End Function

    Private Function GetUserBLLocked(ByVal ID As String) As Integer
        Dim rs As RemoteSet
        rs = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select BLLocked from SysUser Where ID='" & ID & "'")
        GetUserBLLocked = toval(rs.fields("BLLocked"))
    End Function
    Private Function Save_Log_Process(ByVal db As ClassLib, ByVal ProcName As String, ByVal PDate As Date, Optional ByVal Param1 As String = "") As Boolean
        Dim rsProcess As RemoteSet
        On Error GoTo errmsg
        rsProcess = db.OpenRemoteSet("Select * from sysLogProcess Where 1<>1")
        rsProcess.AddNew()
        rsProcess.fields("ProcName") = ProcName
        rsProcess.fields("PDate") = PDate
        rsProcess.fields("Param1") = Param1
        rsProcess.fields("PTime") = Time()
        rsProcess.fields("UserID") = db_user
        rsProcess.fields("Message") = StrMsg
        If Not rsProcess.Update Then GoTo errmsg
        Save_Log_Process = True
        Exit Function
errmsg:
        Save_Log_Process = False
    End Function
    Private Function Find_Element(ByRef Arr As Object, ByVal first_row As Integer, ByVal last_row As Integer, ByVal Col As Integer, ByVal selement As String) As Integer
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim MaxRows As Integer = CType(Arr, Object(,)).GetUpperBound(1)
        Dim MaxCols As Integer = CType(Arr, Object(,)).GetUpperBound(0)
        If last_row > MaxRows Then last_row = MaxRows
        For i = first_row To last_row
            If Arr(Col, i) = selement Then
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        If i > last_row Then
            ReDim Preserve Arr(MaxCols, MaxRows + 1)
        End If
        Find_Element = i
    End Function

    Private Function Find_3_Element(ByRef Arr As Object, ByVal first_row As Integer, ByVal last_row As Integer, ByVal Col_1 As Integer, ByVal selement_1 As String, ByVal Col_2 As Integer, ByVal selement_2 As String, ByVal Col_3 As Integer, ByVal selement_3 As String) As Integer
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim MaxRows As Integer = CType(Arr, Object(,)).GetUpperBound(1)
        Dim MaxCols As Integer = CType(Arr, Object(,)).GetUpperBound(0)
        If last_row > MaxRows Then last_row = MaxRows
        For i = first_row To last_row
            If Arr(Col_1, i) = selement_1 And Arr(Col_2, i) = selement_2 And Arr(Col_3, i) = selement_3 Then
                Exit For
            End If
        Next i
        If i > last_row Then
            ReDim Preserve Arr(MaxCols, MaxRows + 1)
        End If
        Find_3_Element = i
    End Function
    Private Function Direct_Call_Process_Execute(ByVal ProcLib As String, ByVal ProcName As String, Optional ByVal Param1 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param2 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param3 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param4 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param5 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param6 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal NewSessionFlag As Boolean = False) As Object
        Dim PObj As Object = Nothing
        Try
            StrMsg = ""
            Select Case UCase(ProcLib)
                Case "PROCESS.NETV4.PROCLIB", "PROCESS.NETV3.PROCLIB"
                    PObj = New Process.NetV4.Proclib
                Case "PROCESSIFRS.NETV4.PROCLIB", "PROCESSIFRS.NETV3.PROCLIB"
                    PObj = New ProcessIFRS.NetV4.Proclib
                Case "PROCESSFA.NETV4.PROCLIB", "PROCESSFA.NETV3.PROCLIB"
                    PObj = New ProcessFA.NetV4.Proclib
            End Select

            PObj.Var_db_User = db.Var_db_user
            PObj.Var_db_User_Type = db.Var_db_User_Type
            PObj.Var_db_User_CCode = db.Var_db_User_CCode
            PObj.Var_Db_Name = db_Name
            PObj.Var_Db_Server = db_Server
            PObj.Var_db_Password = db_password
            PObj.Var_db_SysUser = db_sysuser
            PObj.Var_db_SysPassword = db_syspassword
            PObj.Var_db_TimeOut = db_TimeOut
            PObj.Var_db_Trusted = db_Trusted
            PObj.Var_ObjSession = db.Var_ObjSession
            If NewSessionFlag Then
                Direct_Call_Process_Execute = PObj.Call_Process("", ProcName, Param1, Param2, Param3, Param4, Param5, Param6)
            Else
                Direct_Call_Process_Execute = PObj.Call_Process_Execute("", ProcName, Param1, Param2, Param3, Param4, Param5, Param6, NewSessionFlag)
            End If
            StrMsg = PObj.ErrDesc
            PObj = Nothing
        Catch ex As Exception
            StrMsg = ex.Message
            PObj = Nothing
            Return False
        End Try
    End Function
    Private Function Direct_Call_Ext_Process(ByVal ProcLib As String, ByVal ProcName As String, Optional ByVal Param1 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param2 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param3 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param4 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param5 As Object = Nothing, Optional ByVal Param6 As Object = Nothing) As Object
        Direct_Call_Ext_Process = Direct_Call_Process_Execute(ProcLib, ProcName, Param1, Param2, Param3, Param4, Param5, Param6, True)
    End Function
End Class
